# Workshop ERP

ERP for a luxury car workshop in Dubai (about 45 users). The owner is not a developer: explain steps in plain language and say exactly what to click. Build one phase at a time and stop for approval after each.

## Read first

- `docs/workshop-erp-blueprint.md` - the full specification and agreed decisions.
- `docs/phase-1-decisions.md` - answers that shape the build (tablets, VIP privacy, plates, retention).
- `docs/phase-2-spec.md` - authoritative Phase 2 spec (gate-in, approval page, job cards, floor board, gate-out); overrides the blueprint on those topics.
- `docs/later-phases-notes.md` - owner notes for phases 4 to 6 (quotation rules, parts flow, profit per invoice).
- `docs/workshop-erp-dashboard-design (1).html` - the look: white, black, grey; colour only for timing.

## Stack

- Next.js 16 (App Router, `src/`), React 19, Tailwind v4, TypeScript. Light theme only.
- Supabase: Postgres + Auth + Storage. Access rules live in the database (row level security), not only in the UI.
- Hosting: Vercel (project workshop-erp). Live address: https://erp.ofjauto.com (the vercel.app address redirects there). Code: private GitHub repository github.com/OFJAUTO/workshop-erp; every push to `main` deploys automatically.
- Secrets in `.env.local` (never committed). Keys: `NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL`, `NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY`, `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY`, `SUPABASE_DB_PASSWORD`, `SUPABASE_ACCESS_TOKEN`, `CRON_SECRET` (the same value must exist in Vercel for the hourly `/api/cron/housekeeping` job).
- Live updates use Supabase Realtime (`LiveRefresh` component); tables are in the `supabase_realtime` publication. Two project-specific lessons: the browser client must call `supabase.realtime.setAuth(session.access_token)` before subscribing (otherwise it joins as `anon` and RLS hides everything), and server-side filters on uuid columns (`id=eq.<uuid>`) were never registered, so subscriptions are unfiltered and matched in the browser. Subscribing to a table that is not in the publication silently breaks the whole channel. Notifications: `src/lib/notifications.ts` (`notifyStaff`, `notifyRoles`, `NOTIFICATION_TYPES`).
- Logins: `staff.login_type` is `password`, `pin` or `both` (owner sets it). Devices (`devices.kind` shared/personal) are registered by the owner on the device at `/tablet/register`; the proxy signs out handheld sessions whose device was removed.

## Commands

```bash
npm run dev          # local app at http://localhost:3000
npm run build        # production build (also type-checks)
npx eslint . --max-warnings=0
npx tsc --noEmit
npx supabase db push --linked        # apply supabase/migrations to the hosted project (needs link)
node --env-file=.env.local scripts/bootstrap-owner.mjs --email <owner email> --name "Omar Aljaf"
```

## Conventions

- Every business table has `created_at/by`, `updated_at/by`, an audit trigger (`write_audit_log`) and a no-delete trigger. Mark records inactive instead of deleting.
- Permissions: `src/lib/roles.ts` mirrors the SQL policies. Pages call `requireStaff()` or `requirePermission()` from `src/lib/auth.ts`.
- Standing rule (Supabase change of 30 October 2026: new tables get no automatic API grants): EVERY migration that creates a table must itself enable RLS, add the role policies and grant explicitly — `authenticated` only when the user client touches the table, `service_role` always, `anon` never (customer pages go through server routes). Template: `supabase/migration-template.sql`. A server-only table (tokens, secrets) gets NO policies and NO anon/authenticated grants at all. `npm run test:security` enforces all of this (RLS on everywhere, zero anon grants/policies/functions, no owner views, server-only tables dark, live anon probes) and runs after every batch together with `npm run test:layout`.
- `createClient()` (server) acts as the logged-in person. `createAdminClient()` bypasses rules: server only, used for logins, PIN checks, tablet registration, settings reads.
- PC login: email + password (form post to `/api/auth/login`). Handheld login: name + 4-digit PIN on registered devices only (`devices` table, `erp_device` cookie). PIN login issues a session through an admin-generated magic link verified server-side. Password setup links: `setup_links` table, `/auth/setup/[token]`, 24 h, used only when a password is saved.
- Setup/password links are generated by the owner and sent on WhatsApp; no email sending is configured.
- Forms: client components using `ActionForm` (`src/components/forms.tsx`); server actions return `{ error, success, values }`.
- Plain words on screen, 44px minimum touch targets, one-tap choice buttons instead of drop-downs where the list is short.
- Standing rule: the result of a click appears at or next to the thing that was clicked, or in a centre window (`SendLinkDialog`). Never somewhere the person has to scroll or search for.
- Standing rule for every customer link (approval, inspection report, later quotations and estimates): a written WhatsApp message from a template in Settings, and a public page whose preview shows the logo, a title and a one-line description (`customerPageMetadata` in `src/lib/customer-pages.ts`; the path must be in `PUBLIC_PREFIXES` in `src/proxy.ts`).
- STANDING RULE (1 November 2026, night): a database change that needs the owner's "apply" never holds up the work. Take the live backup (`scripts/db-backup.mjs`, read-only), keep the code dormant behind `schemaReady` so nothing live breaks, add the migration to `docs/waiting-for-apply.md` (the WAITING FOR APPLY list, with its backup) and carry on with the next task. Show that list in every report. The owner applies them together.
- STANDING RULE (1 November 2026): There must ALWAYS be a way to record a payment for any car or customer at any time. Never remove or hide it. THE way is the Record payment window (`src/components/RecordPaymentHost.tsx`, `openRecordPayment`, `RecordPaymentButton`), on every car page, customer page, job card, money page, the Invoices and payments page and the search results ("Pay"), for owner and accounts; `npm run test:payanytime` proves it for every job stage and for a car or customer with no job.
- STANDING RULE (1 November 2026, B1): the text staff type for the customer is SAVED in capitals (`capsText` in `src/lib/text-case.ts`; the database trigger `erp_caps_text` on quotation_lines, quotations.customer_note, new invoice_lines, work_lines, job_requests, services, part_catalogue). Never fake capitals with a screen-only style for that text. A part prints its ONE name with side and type (`partLineName` in quotes.ts: "HEADLIGHT – FRONT LEFT · GENUINE", the side only when the name does not say it). The legal name "O F J AUTOMOTIVE WORKS L.L.C" is THE name on documents and the bank line (`unbroken` keeps it on one line).
- Standing rule: every box where people type words is spell checked in English WHILE TYPING by our own check (`SpellHost`, `data-spell`; `Input`/`Textarea` in `src/components/ui.tsx` do this, a raw box carries `data-spell=""`; keep it for anything built later). Names, phones, plates, VINs, part numbers, addresses, amounts and codes are never checked (`noSpellName`). The dictionary is shared: customer, staff and supplier names, makes and models, the parts list, services, brands, and the words added with "Add to dictionary" (owner and advisors). Sending is never blocked or asked about spelling.

## Speed rules (standing, from 29 October 2026; follow them in every batch)

1. Tests: after each section run only the layout and security checks for the pages that section changed, at 800×1280 and 1440×900 (`PAGES=/a,/b SIZES=800x1280,1440x900 ROLE=<role> node --env-file=.env.test.local tests/browser/layout-check.mjs`; Git Bash needs `MSYS_NO_PATHCONV=1` for the paths). Run the FULL `npm run test:layout` (all pages, all sizes, pop-ups, 130% text) + `npm run test:security` ONCE at the end of the batch, fix what it finds, then re-run only what failed.
2. Deploys: push after each section but never sit waiting for the Vercel deployment; carry on with the next section. Before each push check that the previous deployment succeeded (the live `https://erp.ofjauto.com/version.json` moved on; if a build failed, fix that first). At the end, check the live site with `npm run check:live` (read-only, see rule 7).
3. The 15-minute rule is for stuck background tasks (dev servers, watchers), not for a test run that is still making progress. Never stop and restart the same test run in a loop.
4. Fast tests: the full layout check runs against ONE production build (`npm run test:server` builds into `.next-test` and serves it on port 3102; `E2E_LOCAL_SERVER=1` lets the local test routes answer there, never on Vercel), one browser with one login per role reused by every worker (`WORKERS`, default 4), each page loaded once per screen family and resized instead of reloaded, and no fixed waits (fonts loaded and two animation frames instead).
5. Proof screenshots come from the test runs themselves (`tests/browser/shots/...`), never from a separate screenshot pass.
6. Timing report at the end of every batch: how long each section took, plus the total time spent on tests, builds and deploy waits.
7. TESTS NEVER TOUCH THE LIVE DATABASE (standing rule from 30 October 2026, after test invoices reached Zoho and tests used up real job, customer and estimate numbers). Every test, proof screenshot and layout check runs against the TEST Supabase project (`workshop-erp-test`, about USD 10 a month, approved by the owner) through a test server: `npm run test:server` (production build, port 3102) or `npm run dev:test` (port 3101), both on `.env.test.local` (`ERP_ENV=test`). The live site gets READ-ONLY checks only: `npm run check:live` (pages open with no errors, signed-out visitors sent to sign in, cron refuses without its secret, the database's rules read-only). Nothing is created, changed or deleted on live by a test, ever: no test documents, no sign-ins, no forms, no cron calls with the secret. The guards: the /api/e2e routes refuse unless the server is the test server on a non-live project (`isTestEnvironment` in `src/lib/environment.ts`), `pw-lib` refuses to start without `ERP_ENV=test`, `tests/e2e-cleanup.sql` refuses unless `settings.database_role = 'test'` (live says 'live'), and the Zoho door refuses the REAL organization outright on the test server. Set-up: `npm run test:db:create` (once), `npm run test:db:migrate` (after every new migration, as well as applying it to live), `npm run test:db:copy` (a fresh copy of the live records with customers' names, phones, emails and addresses replaced everywhere, staff PINs 1234; the live connection is read-only for the run).

## Phases

1. Foundation (done): logins, roles, PINs, tablets, customers, contacts, cars, photos, settings, change log.
2. Gate-in, approval page, job cards, dashboard and floor board, gate-out (built, in testing). Mechanical path built 8 October 2026: `jobs.department`, `/assign` (managers by department via `staff.department_id`), technician screen `/my-jobs/[id]`, `inspections` + items/findings/media/change requests, report `/jobs/[id]/inspection`, checklist in `settings.inspection_checklist` (editor at `/settings/inspection`), files in bucket `inspection-media`. The gate-in role sees `/gate-in` only. Evening round of 8 October 2026: mileage in km or miles (`gate_ins.mileage_unit`, `mileage_miles`, car remembers `vehicles.mileage_unit`), model year read from the VIN, the manager chooses the road test at assignment (`road_tests.decision`; a needed test holds the inspection), assignment target setting with advisor notes and reminders, N/A on checklist items, QC inspector limited to road tests, approved reports and the workshop list. 3. Calendar (built 8 October 2026: `appointments` table with `kind` customer_visit/car_drop/we_collect/customer_collects, `/calendar`, gate-in hand-off via `?appointment=`, `staff.colour`, reminders in the hourly cron), QC, manager dashboard. 4. Quotes, approvals, invoices, Zoho Books. Quotations and parts pricing built 8 to 9 October 2026: `quotations` (kind quotation/estimate, Q-/E- numbers with versions) + `quotation_lines`, `part_requests` (from the approved report) + `part_items` (exact parts, priced by Parts, confirmed by the technician), `service_categories` + `services` (the owner's services list behind "Add service", editor at `/settings/services`; replaced the old packages), `declined_work`; builder at `/jobs/[id]/quote/[qid]` and `/estimates/[id]` (autosave through `/api/quote/save`), Parts desk `/parts`, customer page `/quote/[token]` (answer via `/api/quote/[token]`), rules in `src/lib/quotes.ts`, data in `src/lib/quote-data.ts`, customer answers in `src/lib/quote-respond.ts`. Rules of 9 October 2026: the minimum parts markup is a floor on the net price after every discount; advisors cannot discount parts (the owner can, with a logged reason); the total discount touches labour and services only; Other and Recovery lines can be hidden from the customer (`visible_to_customer`, cost still counts against profit); every line is Urgent or Recommended (`urgency`); the customer approves all or nothing, or asks for an urgent-only version (`urgent_requested`, `urgentOnlyVersion`); bank charge settings feed the owner's profit when `payment_by_card` is ticked; an advisor may add one "small part" per quotation (`advisor_added`), other parts go to Parts as price requests; quotations are visible to the owner and the job's advisor only (`can_see_quotation`, Parts see `quotation_part_lines`). PDFs (quotation, estimate, inspection report) come from `@react-pdf/renderer` through `/api/pdf/*` with one template in `src/lib/pdf/` (company details from Settings). Invoices and Zoho Books still to do.
5. Parts, purchase orders, materials stock. 6. Time clock, bodyshop sub-steps, customer messages.

The rest of the mechanical path was built 9 October 2026 (migration `20261014090000_work_to_gate_out.sql`): purchase orders (`purchase_orders` + lines, `suppliers`; raised at `/parts`, approved by the owner or head accountant at `/parts/orders/[id]`, deposit rule with owner override, ordered with expected dates, received line by line with wrong/damaged flags, supplier invoice scan or "to follow", QR labels at `/parts/labels/[jobId]` sized by settings, issue with the technician's PIN at `/parts/issue/[jobId]`, returns, consumables `stock_items`/`stock_issues` at `/parts/stock`); work (`work_lines`, `work_sessions` one open per technician, `shifts`, `additional_work`; manager at `/jobs/[id]/work`, technician panel on `/my-jobs/[id]`, attendance at `/attendance`); QC (`qc_checks` with items built from the job plus `settings.qc_general_checks`, `/qc`); wash (`washes`, `/wash`); invoices and payments (`invoices` kind tax_invoice/proforma/credit_note + `invoice_lines`, `payments` with cheques and bank charges; built at `/jobs/[id]/invoice`, list `/invoices`, customer page `/invoice/[token]`, CSV export `/api/export/accounts`); gate-out with delivery (`gate_outs` columns, status `in_delivery`, follow-up reminders); profit (`src/lib/profit.ts`, `/profit`, live panel). Document template for every PDF in `src/lib/pdf/template.tsx` with company details and bank from Settings, QR codes (`qrcode`), the dirham symbol as a vector (`src/lib/money.ts`, `public/dirham.svg`, CC0 from Wikimedia Commons) switchable to "AED" by the `document_currency` setting. Files after the inspection (work photos, wash, delivery, supplier invoices, QC post-scan) go to bucket `job-files` via `/api/files/prepare` + `register` (`JobFileUpload`). Not built: Zoho Books, the bodyshop path, the phone app.

Lessons from the end-to-end test of 9 October 2026: pages that must print bare (the part labels) live in the `(print)` route group, outside the app shell, and render a `<div>` with a `<style>` tag, never their own `<html>`. A part marked "in stock" never goes on a purchase order: at customer approval it gets `received_qty` and a label and can be issued (`quote-respond.ts`, `order-actions.ts`). An additional quotation (car already in Work, QC or wash) never moves the job backwards, only moves the promised date later, and adds its labour lines to the work order through `ensureWorkLines` (called only at transitions, never from a page load; `20261015090000_work_lines_unique.sql` keeps one work line per quotation line). After a failed QC the technician's panel and the manager's work order show the failed items in a red box. An agreed invoice total is landed exactly: the VAT line takes the rounding. Test helpers: a temporary `scripts/_test-login.tmp.mjs` (magic-link logins per role, deleted before commit), seed SQL with fixed ids `22222222-0000-4000-8000-0000000000xx`, and a clean-up SQL with `session_replication_role = replica`; PDFs are checked by rasterizing them with pdfjs in the scratchpad, since the browser pane cannot show a PDF.

Round 2 of fixes from the owner's live test (9 October 2026, evening; migration `20261016090000_round2_fixes_and_improvements.sql`, delivery note `docs/round-2-delivery-note.md`): the Parts desk rebuilt without the technician confirm step (`part_items` rows carry `part_type`/`brand`/`option_group`, "Ask workshop manager" questions at `/parts/question/[partId]`, "Already covered in another request"); the quotation builder rebuilt as two blocks (labour and services, then a compact parts table where advisors change only the markup; `completeQuotation` gates Send, `quotations.completed_at` is cleared by `refreshQuoteTotals(..., { reopen: true })` on any change; the automatic hidden bank-charge Fee line `fee_kind = 'bank_charge'` is kept by `refreshQuoteTotals` and counted in `profit.ts` until a payment is recorded; part options use `option_group`/`chosen`; `loadQuoteChecks`/`loadPartsWait` feed the blockers and the Remind/Escalate buttons); the inspection form rework (`inspection_items` leak/fluid/disc fields, `parts_rows` jsonb carried into `part_requests.quantity/unit` one request per row with `source_key = item_key#index`, the Dangerous switch forcing Urgent and the customer's safety warning plus acknowledgement, limits in `settings.inspection_limits`, tap-first suggestions learned into `settings.item_suggestions` at approval, `estimated_hours` agreed by the manager at approval, the pre-scan gate behind `settings.prescan_gate_enabled`); quick codes (`quick_codes`, `/api/quick-code`, `/q/[code]`: one-minute single-use QR that signs the same person in on a phone for two hours with `erp_login = quick`); scan reports by email (`/api/inbound/scan?token=<settings.inbound_scan_token>` taking Postmark inbound JSON, `inbound_scans` + bucket `scan-reports`, matched by VIN to the open job, pre/post by `scanKindFor(status)` in `src/lib/scans.ts`, page `/scans`); the owner's "Act as" mode (`erp_act_as` cookie, `CurrentStaff.actingAs`, display name "Omar, on behalf of <name>", proxy allows changes); the car picture as the first phone-checklist item (`gate_in_media.kind = 'car_picture'`, copied to `vehicle-photos` on register). Lessons: Next.js route files may export only handlers, so shared helpers live in `src/lib` (`scans.ts`); SectionLabel text is upper-cased by CSS, so text checks in browser tests must be case-insensitive; the inbound webhook token is a setting shown only to the owner.

Overnight round of 9 to 10 October 2026 (migrations `20261017090000_overnight_round3.sql`, `20261017120000_overnight_round3b.sql`; note in `docs/overnight-delivery-note.md`): planning inside the Parts step (`jobs.plan_*`, `src/lib/planning.ts`, `PlanningCard`, `jobs/planning-actions.ts`; Parts plan each part at `/parts/[jobId]`, the manager releases the car with technicians, the advisor confirms the date); work on the tablet (`src/lib/work-flow.ts`: `job_technicians`, `work_pauses`, Working/Pause/Leave/Job finished, manager confirm or send back, `/pauses`); QC opens its round itself (`openQcRound`, `applyStageSideEffects` on owner moves); job summary at QC pass (`src/lib/job-summary.ts`, `/scoreboard`); wash with Send to wash and a display board at `/board/wash` (registered device, no login); payments (`src/lib/payments.ts`: one receipt per tap via `client_key`, duplicates refused, over-balance `pending_owner`, void and verify); parts handover with one PIN (`part_handovers`, `/parts/handover/[jobId]`, `/parts/trail/[jobId]`); personal PINs with `staff_private.pin_must_change` and `/api/auth/pin/change`; notification tones in `src/lib/tones.ts`; comebacks (`jobs.comeback_*`, `src/lib/comebacks.ts`, free jobs approved on sending, `invoices.warranty_credit_aed`, `/comebacks`); every customer page uses `CustomerDocument`. End-to-end test: `POST /api/e2e/run` with the `x-e2e-secret` header (`E2E_SECRET` in `.env.local`, refused in production) against `npx next dev -p 3100`, then the clean-up SQL from the scratchpad (fixed ids `22222222-0000-4000-8000-00000000e2xx`). Lint rule: a server page that reads the clock does it once behind `// eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/purity`.

Morning of 10 October 2026 (note `docs/morning-10-oct-note.md`): the document design is `docs/ofj-invoice-design.html` with the logo `docs/ofj-logo.svg` (copied to `public/logo.svg`, used as it is); every customer page (`CustomerDocument`) and every PDF (`src/lib/pdf/template.tsx`, font Plus Jakarta Sans from `public/fonts`, traced into the PDF routes by `outputFileTracingIncludes`) follows it. The invoice is a straight copy of the approved quotation (`buildInvoiceDraft(jobId, settings)`: itemised labour, the quoted discount, the quotation's rounded total; no options at invoice time); only a part line with a `part_item_id` goes under Spare parts, and a quotation part line without one blocks sending. Clear test data: `public.clear_test_data()` (TRUNCATE in one transaction, numbering restarted; every update in it needs a WHERE clause because the API connection refuses updates without one, and `session_replication_role` cannot be set inside a function there), `src/lib/clear-test-data.ts` (backup of every table to the private `backups` bucket, then the function, then the job buckets emptied), the owner's button in Settings behind `settings.test_mode_enabled`, and `scripts/db-restore.mjs <backup folder>` to put a backup back. Local test routes behind `E2E_SECRET`: `/api/e2e/run`, `/api/e2e/clear`, `/api/e2e/login` (cookies for a script), `/api/e2e/car`; PDFs are checked by rasterizing with the `mupdf` package in the scratchpad.

First day of staff testing, 10 October 2026 (migrations `20261019090000_staff_day1_fixes.sql`, `20261019093000_sentence_case_fix.sql`, `20261019094000_request_unavailable.sql`; note `docs/staff-day-1-fixes-note.md`): `FormGuard` in the app layout keeps the scroll position across form submits and swallows a second tap on any button for five seconds; the quotation editor sends an `opId` with every change and the save route stores it as `client_key` (quotation lines, part requests) so retries never make a second row. Technicians: `assignJob` takes several ticked technicians (chips, `TechnicianChecks`), the first is the lead; `job_technicians` rows from the first assignment; `TechniciansCard` (add, Take off, Make lead) on the job card and the work order; `technicianOnJob` in `src/lib/job-technicians.ts` lets anyone on the car fill the report and add files. Photos are redrawn as JPEG in the browser (`InspectionMedia`, camera and gallery pickers). Link previews use `public/preview.jpg` (1200 by 630). Proforma first: `src/lib/invoice-flow.ts` (`ensureProforma` at Ready from the wash and from owner moves, `convertProformaIfPaid` at zero balance from `settleJob`, invoices carry `converted_from/converted_to/issue_reason`, payments move to the tax invoice, the customer's proforma link shows the tax invoice afterwards, receipts open with `?t=<invoice token>`); `issueInvoice` is the owner's early tax invoice with a reason; `jobs.invoice_sent_at` and `whatsapp_invoice_template`. Bank charges per method in `bankChargePercentFor`, quotations use `highestBankCharge`. Text tidiness: SQL functions `erp_title_case`, `erp_sentence_case`, `erp_upper_code` with BEFORE triggers (`*_tidy`) and the `known_words` setting; the same rules in `src/lib/text-case.ts` behind the `textCase` prop of `Input`/`Textarea` (known words come from the `erp-known-words` meta tag in the layout). No staff names on anything the customer sees (no Prepared by anywhere). Purchase orders are LPOs (`LPO-` numbers, suppliers at `/parts/suppliers`, `manageSuppliers` permission). Manager time budget in `jobs.budget_*` (`setTimeBudget`). Browser tests: `playwright-core` with the installed Edge in the scratchpad, seeds from `POST /api/e2e/stage` (`{ stage: "assign" | "inspection" | "quote" | "approved" | "ready" }`, fixed ids e3xx), ad-hoc SQL from `POST /api/e2e/sql`, the clean-up SQL removes every `22222222-0000-4000-8000-00000000e%` row. Lessons: wait for a form's redirect by URL change, never for "networkidle" (the notification poll never stops); a bash heredoc that silently fails leaves the old file in place, so check the file after writing; `$1` in a double-quoted bash string is expanded by bash.

Overnight round of 10 to 11 October 2026 (migration `20261020090000_overnight_round4.sql`, note `docs/overnight-11-oct-note.md`): services priced per unit (`services.price_per/usual_quantity/time_allowance_hours/includes_oil_change`, `quotation_lines.price_per`; `ensureWorkLines` gives package lines allowance × quantity); consumables only by the "+ Consumables" button; parts handover as a request the technician confirms with his PIN on his own device or "Sign here" at the counter (`src/lib/handover.ts`: `requestHandover`/`confirmHandover`, `part_handovers.status`, `part_items.pending_handover_id`); stickers (`src/lib/stickers.ts`, `src/components/StickerArt.tsx`, print pages under `src/app/(print)/print/*`, QR short links `qr_links` + `/s/[code]`, battery warranties `battery_warranties`, the oil service sticker in `jobs.service_sticker` and `vehicles.next_service_*`, Settings > Stickers); breaks (`src/lib/breaks.ts`, `working-time.ts` with `breakStart/breakEnd`, `work_sessions.through_break`, `autoPauseBreaks` in the cron, a person's own break in `staff.break_start/break_end`); technician load (`src/lib/technician-load.ts`, `TechnicianPicker`, `/workshop-load`); device modes (`devices.blocked_at`, `setDeviceKind`/`blockDevice`, IdleLock only on shared devices, PIN to finish a job on a personal device, red dot on the tablet photos); loose items (`jobs.job_kind = 'loose'` on a hidden vehicle of `kind = 'loose'` with make "Loose items", `job_items`, `src/lib/loose-items.ts`, `/gate-in/loose`, tags at `/print/item-tags/[jobId]`, path without inspection or wash via `LOOSE_SKIP_STAGES`, `collectItems` on the gate-out page, QC pass goes straight to Ready); the owner's reports (`src/lib/owner-report.ts`: `recordOwnerReport` at `closeJob`, `jobs.owner_report`, `/reports/monthly|cars|people`, `/reports/jobs/[id]`, PDF `/api/pdf/monthly?month=`, the cron sends last month's summary once, `report_good_margin_percent`); scan emails (`inbound_emails` for non-scan emails, the set-up steps and the hidden address on `/scans`, `regenerateInboundToken`). `formatPlate` returns the item summary for a loose vehicle (every plate select also reads `kind`). Lessons: a `"use server"` file must export only async functions (an exported constant breaks every page that imports the file, and the broken state stays in `.next` until it is deleted: a dev server that reloads every page once a second means delete `.next` and restart); never edit source files while browser tests run against the dev server (each save reloads the pages mid-test); test rows made through the screens are found by the test customer's phone (`+9715000000…`) in the clean-up SQL.

Rework of 11 October 2026, morning list (note `docs/rework-11-oct-note.md`; the owner's message ended at "A7", the rest never arrived). The main rule: findings live in the inspection report only; after it the work runs on a labour list (the advisor's, `+ Labour`/`Add service`) and a parts list (`part_requests`/`part_items`, priced by Parts). The quotation builder lost the request headings, the BAD/AVG badges and the "not quoted" findings list (`QuoteEditor` has no `findings` prop; `startQuotation` pre-fills no labour lines; `suggestedLines` is unused); QC items are work lines, parts and general checks only (`buildQcItems`, no complaint items). A1: `CarPictureCrop` (16:10 frame, pinch/drag/slider, "Use photo", reopen with the current picture) replaces the plain capture for `car_picture` in `MediaChecklist` (`carPictureUrl` prop from the media page and `/u/[token]`). A3: View as / Act as are per tab: `ModeGuard` (sessionStorage `erp-tab-mode`, asserts the tab's mode through `POST /api/view-as/set`, the start actions redirect with `?as=1`), `ModeExit`, red bar for acting, `generateMetadata` title in the app layout, `/api/notifications` reads as the person on screen through the admin client. A4: `tyre_<pos>_brand` measurements; "Same as front left" copies year, tread, brand and (rear) size. A5: the inspection submit flushes the autosave first, shows "Submitting…" at once and the server's answer in the bar (`SubmitOutcome`, `ProblemsBar.error`); `FormGuard` marks every tapped submit button `data-busy` ("…" and a pulse, CSS in globals). A6: − and + on per-unit service lines. A2: `HANDOVER_TYPES` in `src/lib/notifications.ts` can never be switched off; the overdue-assignment reminder also goes to the department's manager. Bug found by the new test: `/parts/[jobId]` crashed in planning because `toValues` was used before its declaration. Tests now live in the repository: `tests/browser/` (`pw-lib.mjs`, `test-notify.mjs` = one car through all sixteen hand-overs checking who is told, `test-a.mjs`, `test-b.mjs`, `debug-nav.mjs`), `tests/e2e-cleanup.sql`, `npm run test:notify|test:day1|test:overnight|test:cleanup`; `playwright-core` and `mupdf` are dev dependencies; `tests/**` is ignored by eslint. Lesson: delete `.next` between `npm run build` and `npx next dev`, or every page reloads itself once a second.

Rework of 11 October 2026, part 2 (migration `20261021090000_rework_11_oct.sql`, data script `scripts/migrate-checklist-11-oct.mjs`, note `docs/rework-11-oct-part2-note.md`): job status `pending_road_test` (set by `assignJob` when the road test is needed, cleared by `saveRoadTest`/`decideRoadTest` with `stage_entered_at` reset; `StageTrack` shows the Inspection step in two halves; the owner report counts the wait on its own). Inspection: customer requests are a read-only notice (`inspections.requests_note`, the `inspection_findings` table is no longer written), checklist items carry `style` grade (Good/Average/Bad) or job (OK/Required) (`ChecklistItem.style`, `inspection_items.style`, labels through `itemStatusLabel`), tyre sizes in three boxes (`TyreSizeInput`, `tyreSizeParts`, saved on `vehicles.tyre_size_front/rear`, prefilled by `ensureInspection`, suggested by `tyreSizeSuggestionFor`), the `tyre_<pos>_reason` measurement (required above `TYRE_REASON_ABOVE_MM`), battery choices Good/Weak/Replace, complaint tags from `settings.complaint_keywords`, the manager's one-tap notes (`settings.manager_note_presets`/`manager_note_usage`, `inspections.manager_note_internal`), the resume position in localStorage, `FormDraft` on the gate-in and QC forms. Parts pool: `part_catalogue` (Settings > Parts list, `part_pool_custom` suggestions), `src/lib/parts-list.ts` (entries, `mergePartsList`, tyre and battery auto entries), `PartPicker`, the list in `inspections.parts_list` saved whole through the save route's `partsList`, confirmed at approval (`parts_list_confirmed_*`, required by `approveInspection`), turned into `part_requests` with `positions`/`note`/`catalogue_id` by `ensurePartRequests` (old reports keep the `parts_rows` path); the Parts job page is one table (`PARTS_GRID`); `part_items.positions` and `quotation_lines.positions` follow the part. Quotation: services are the one jobs list (`services.position_ask`, `suggest_for` keyed by item key, `tyre:<pos>`, `measure:battery_health`; `labour_jobs` merged in; `custom_jobs_used` suggestions), `QuoteReference` strip (`src/lib/quote-reference.ts`), thin rows with a three-dot menu, one `ServicePicker` with Suggested/Most used/custom/Edit the list, `PartPicker` behind "+ Add a part" (`requestPart` takes positions and catalogue_id), `removeLine.keepParts`, the tyres row made by `startQuotation`, `withPositions` on the customer page, PDF and invoice. Customer links: `CustomerDocument header="plain"` on the approval and report links, `CustomerMedia`/`Lightbox`, the report in the order Dangerous, Needs attention, Checked and passed, Measurements, Workshop note, fee note, Call/WhatsApp; `Logo` uses `/logo.svg` everywhere and `public/logo-mark.svg` only on the 20 mm part sticker. `WaitingPanel` (`src/lib/waiting.ts`, from `nextStepOf`) sits on every person's first page. Lessons: never `cd` inside a compound command that runs npx; a nested template literal in a patch script's replacement text must be escaped.


Evening list of 11 October 2026 (migration `20261022090000_evening_11_oct.sql`, note `docs/evening-11-oct-note.md`): the theme was less on screen. Customer links share `CustomerFront` (short first page: essentials, one View button to the full `CustomerDocument` with `?view=1` and `backHref`, the single action, `ContactButtons`); the quotation has Approve only (`approved_amount`, `quotations.viewed_full_at`; the advisor marks Declined with `declineQuotation`); Send rounds down by default (`roundingChoices.down`, shown as "Discount (rounding)"); payment links live in `payment_links` and open once through `POST /api/pay/[token]` (`src/lib/payment-links.ts`, `PayNowButton`, staff see the URL as text only). Manager review: `ApproveForm` chips from `settings.manager_note_groups`, the `PartsListTick` under the parts list (`inspections.parts_list_confirmed_at/by`, cleared when the list changes) required by `approveInspection`, decisions in the fixed `ReviewBar` (`ActionForm` takes an `id` so a bar can `requestSubmit()`). Parts: the pricing page is one autosaving table (`PartsTable` + `autoSavePartRow`, `src/lib/parts-rows.ts` holds `rowComplete` for both sides), `PartsBar` with "Pricing done" (`jobs.parts_pricing_done_at`), the supplier list text, price changes after sending flag `quotations.parts_price_changed_at`; `part_catalogue.unit = set` parts ask Front or Rear. Quotation editor: the report strip with per-device open state, "+ Add line" with Labour or Service, `NameBox` (type with suggestions, or the arrow to that kind's `ServicePicker`, now multi-select with `onDone(picked, custom)` and a `kind`), a pop-up three-dot menu (`MenuItem`), parts with `quotation_lines.left_off` (`isUnchosen` treats a left-off part as not counted everywhere), no parent-line linking or blocker, the internal breakdown (`quoteTotals` adds `recoveryCost`/`labourMargin` and computes the bank charge live as rate × total with VAT when `bankChargePercent` is given), the recovery row from `settings.recovery_types` (`quotation_lines.recovery_type`, `gate_ins.recovery_type` asked at gate-in, wording by `recoveryTitle`), the decision in a fixed bar; Settings > services shows two lists (`?list=labour|services`). The services duplicates were merged by name (list in the note). Handover: `confirmHandover` takes `receivedIds` (unticked parts get `not_received_at` and go back to Parts), the owner signs with no PIN and the manager with his own, `stillToReceive` blocks `finishMyPart`, one state per part in `src/lib/part-state.ts`, `exchangePart` (replacement row with `replaced_part_id`, original `exchange_*`), `partNotNeeded` + `setDisposition` (returned/stock/fit_pending, credit note scan in the parts bucket) + `approveGoodwill`, the owner's list at `/parts/no-home` with the cron telling the owner after three working days (`no_home_notified_at`). Technician: `WorkClock` shows Start work / Working (green, time) / Paused · reason (amber, Resume); `minePause` from `work_pauses`. Stickers: `BatterySticker` with the `line` (`batteryLine`), the black warranty band and the large VIN, mirrored in `sticker-pdf.tsx`; every print link opens a new tab (the oil sticker passes the typed details as query parameters to `/print/service-sticker`). Lessons: a helper exported from a "use client" file cannot be called by a server page (move it to `src/lib`); `backdrop-blur` on a fixed bar makes it the containing block of any fixed dialog rendered inside it (the send window was boxed in); a server action cannot open a new tab, so the print button uses `window.open` and submits the form as well; a test's own dialog handler conflicts with the shared one in `pw-lib`.


Fixes from the testing of 12 October 2026 (migrations `20261023090000_fixes_12_oct.sql` and `20261023093000_lpo_token_default.sql`, note `docs/fixes-12-oct-note.md`, test `tests/browser/test-c.mjs` = `npm run test:c`): the LPO has one supplier copy (`renderPoPdf` with `PoLinesTable`: list, discount, net, VAT, the VIN only, "Approved electronically" with a QR to the public `/lpo/[token]` page; `purchase_orders.verify_token` with a default, `/api/pdf/lpo/[token]`, `LpoSendControl` + `markLpoSent`; the internal screen keeps the job card and names); suppliers carry `default_discount_percent` and `discount_by_type` (owner and accounts edit), `part_items.list_price_aed/discount_percent` with `cost_aed` the net (`src/lib/parts-rows.ts`: `netOf`, `discountOf`, `supplierDiscount`), LPO lines keep `list_price/discount_percent`. Gate-in media: `MediaChecklist` is a client component holding the server checklist, the register route's checklist comes back through `onChecklist` so a tick is green only when the server counts it; `VideoCapture`/`MediaCapture` have a Processing state; the media page polls every 4 s while incomplete. Parts table rebuilt (`PartsTable` columns Part, Qty, Part number, Type, List, Disc %, Net, Supplier, Availability; stable row keys `req:<id>`; part number as text with Copy once saved; supplier `<select>` with `addSupplierQuick`; `autoSavePartRow` takes `quantity`, `name`, `list_price`, `discount` and writes `confirmed_quantity`, `original_description`, `quantity_changed_*`, notifies the advisor and manager and flags sent quotations through `flagSentQuotations`; `splitPart`; menus open upwards near the bottom). Customer links: View opens the PDF in a new tab (`/api/pdf/quote|report|invoice/[token]`, receipts with `?t=`), the HTML full views and `CustomerDocument` are gone, the quote PDF route sets `viewed_full_at`. Quotations: any advisor may edit or send (`mayEdit`, `can_see_quotation` for all advisors, the save route's advisor check removed), the car's advisor is told (`quote_other_editor`), one editor at a time (`quotations.editing_by/at`, the save route's `lock: take|release` with a 90 s freshness, the editor's heartbeat, `lockedBy` prop), `quotations.expected_payment_method` sets the fee line through `expectedChargePercent` (and the editor's live rate from `bankRateCard/bankRateLink`), `transfer` is a payment method (`bank_charge_transfer_percent`). Typing: `src/lib/refresh-idle.ts` (`refreshWhenIdle`) gates every `router.refresh` (LiveRefresh, the editor, the Parts table, the uploads); the editor keeps a `dirty` set of lines whose local values win on merge. PDFs: `PdfDocument` renders a compact header on pages after the first through the header's `render` prop, the content wrapper has `flexGrow: 1`, `DocumentTail` puts totals, words and payments, notes and the bank block (`wrap={false}`, `marginTop: auto`) in a fixed order, table headings are `fixed`. Handover: no technician PIN on Parts screens (`SignForForm` for the owner without a PIN and the manager with his own), the technician's `PartsForCar` section under the clock. Messages: short templates (defaults and stored values), the proforma address `/proforma/[token]` (same page, its own preview title) used by `ReadySendControl`, `invoices.sent_by/sent_at`. Shell: fixed bars use `left-0 right-0 md:left-60`; Sign out sits in a `<details>` under the name. Lessons: a helper that builds closures over refs during render (the menu `item()` helper) trips the React rules even when the closures are only handlers, so menu rows are plain components; `cutBetween` keeps the end marker (do not repeat it in the replacement); the e2e sql route cannot delete rows of tables with the no-delete trigger, so tests re-kind rows instead; PDF titles print in capitals, so text checks on PDFs ignore case; a link built in a client component from props never appears in the page HTML.

Evening list of 12 October 2026 (migrations `20261024090000_evening_12_oct_part1.sql`, `20261024120000_evening_12_oct_part2.sql`; note `docs/evening-12-oct-note.md`). PART 1: `NumericInput` keeps the typed text while focused and hands the finished number on about a second after typing stops or on leaving (dot or comma; "2." and ".5" stay as typed); hours columns are numeric(6,2) and `parseHours` rounds to two decimals. One part state in `src/lib/parts-rows.ts` (`rowState`: priced / not_available / removed / waiting_approval / not_finished; `rowMissing`, `pricingSummary`, `availabilityChoice`, `customerAvailabilityWords`, `daysFromDate`) read by the Parts table, its bar (now inside `PartsTable`), `pricingDone`, `tellAdvisorIfComplete`, `loadQuoteChecks`, `loadQuoteSummaries` and the quotation page through `loadPricingRows` in `src/lib/parts-pricing.ts` (server). The Parts row is a fixed-height grid plus one status line; availability is one dropdown (In stock / Same day / Next day / 2 days / 3 days / Other…) with `delivery_date` = today + days in Dubai; rows keep their opening order; the "Show only unpriced" filter is a snapshot; saves run in a chain with `client_key` = the row key (`part_items.client_key`) so a second quick save never makes a second part. "Pricing done" with open rows turns them red (`attempted`) with Go to next; Not available and Removed never block. PART 2: road test items are eight (`ROAD_TEST_ITEMS`, old keys kept) with one-tap chips (`src/lib/road-test-chips.ts`, settings `road_test_chips` / `road_test_chip_usage`, editor on Settings > Inspection); tyre brands (`tyre_brands`, `tyre_brand_custom` learned at approval, `tyre_<pos>_model` measurement); the pool carries `paired` and `goes_with` (`part_catalogue`; `PAIR_CHOICES`, `positionsWords`, `goesWithOffers`, `offerEntry` in `parts-list.ts`; offers in `PartPicker`, under the gathered list and in the quotation's parts block, never auto-added); structured remarks (`hasStructuredDetail`, `autoRemarkFor` in `inspection.ts`); `ReasonChips` + `src/lib/reasons.ts` (`reasonsFor(settings, context)`, settings `reason_presets`) on every "why" box; `FormGuard` swallows the browser's validity bubble and writes `.erp-field-msg` under the box; recovery `quotation_lines.recovery_paid_by` / `recovery_mention_free` ("Who pays?"; paid by us is hidden unless mentioned as free at AED 0, `recoveryTitle(label, trips, free)`); documents not sent (`src/lib/doc-reminders.ts` pure rules, `doc-reminders-data.ts` loader, settings `doc_reminder_limits`, `jobs.doc_reminders` for the once-only red reminders in cron step 15, notification `doc_not_sent`, dashboard "Not sent" chip); "Remove: not needed" (`src/app/(app)/parts/remove-actions.ts`: `removeNotNeeded`, `putBackPart`, `notNeededFromParts`; `part_items`/`part_requests` `removed_*` columns, removed rows stay inactive but listed); supplier discounts (Parts may set them, `supplier_discount_changes` log, "Apply n% to the open lines" via `applySupplierDiscount`, approval above `supplier_discount_approval_above_percent` → `part_items.discount_approval_status` and the owner's `/parts/approvals` page with `decideDiscountApproval`). Tests `tests/browser/test-d.mjs` (PART 1 proof) and `test-e.mjs` (PART 2), `npm run test:d|test:e`. Lessons: a `never`-returning redirect helper only narrows when its call is returned (`return back(...)`); `selectOption` does not move focus, so a number box left focused keeps its text until a real blur; the stage seed's own part sits last in the Parts table, so tests find rows by name.


Morning list of 13 October 2026 (migration `20261025090000_morning_13_oct.sql`, note `docs/morning-13-oct-note.md`, test `tests/browser/test-f.mjs` = `npm run test:f`): the gate-in finish button names the missing items (`src/lib/media-checklist.ts`: `missingItems`, `missingButtonLabel`, `itemsLeftLine`; `FinishGateInButton` fires `GATE_IN_ATTEMPT_EVENT`, `MediaChecklist` turns the items red; every capture carries `data-media-item`/`data-missing`/`data-media-status`); uploads never fail quietly (`CarPictureCrop` keeps the cropped preview through Uploading/Done/Retry and asks "Use this photo as it is?" on close; `putWithProgress` has a 90 s stall detector; `reportUploadFailure` → `/api/media/failure` → `upload_failures`, read at `/settings/uploads`); the camera inside the page (`CameraCapture` + `PhotoButton`: getUserMedia on touch devices, the file picker on PCs, permission steps per device, "Use the camera app instead"; used by `MediaCapture`, `CarPictureCrop`, `InspectionMedia`, `JobFileUpload`; videos keep the camera app); customer videos (`CustomerVideos` + public `GET /api/customer/media?t=<approval token>` in `PUBLIC_PREFIXES`: "Video getting ready…" slots polled every 8 s, fresh signed address on Play after 40 min or after an error, `playsInline muted`, "Couldn't load the video. Tap to try again"; `MediaGallery hideVideos`); the Parts table grid written out in full (two lines at md, one line from `min-[110rem]`; Tailwind never sees a class built from a template literal, and an arbitrary `min-[…px]` variant sorts before `md` while `min-[…rem]` sorts after it), Supplier options show the name only, the days box renders only for Other; `PartPicker` has `mode="rename"` (`onPick`), `CATEGORY_ORDER`/`sortCategories` in `parts-list.ts`, "Custom part" at the bottom, and is used by `PartsTable` for rename and add (`noteCustomPartNames` counts custom names; `RowSave.positions`); price import: `src/lib/price-import.ts` (pure: `readTable`, `parsePaste`, `mapHeadings`, `detectFormat` b2bconnect/landrover/generic/mapped/unknown, `checkTotals`, `suggestMatches`, `parseNumber`, `normalisePartNumber`, `parseAvailability`), `parts/import-actions.ts` (`parseImportUpload` with `xlsx`, `parseImportText`, `saveImportMapping`, `applyImport` through `autoSavePartRow`), page `/parts/[jobId]/import` + `ImportWizard`, `POST /api/import/ocr` (Anthropic vision, off without `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`); part numbers never on customer documents (titles are the name only, `partNumber: null` in the quote and invoice PDFs; the LPO keeps them). Samples in `docs/` (`B2BConnect_Format_9.xlsx`, `landrover-paste-sample*.txt`). Lessons: the stage seed's gate-in is complete (legacy rows), so media tests re-kind them to `gate_out_photo` and null the vehicle photo; an `Input`'s `w-full` beats a `w-0` added later, so hide a box by not rendering it; `MediaStream`/object URLs are freed on unmount only, never when the state changes.


Afternoon list of 13 October 2026 (migration `20261026090000_clear_bulk_import.sql`, note `docs/afternoon-13-oct-note.md`, test `tests/browser/test-g.mjs` = `npm run test:g`, `--clear` presses Clear test data at the end): `write_audit_log` stores only the changed columns on UPDATE and merges saves by the same person on the same row within 60 s into one entry; `clear_test_data()` finds the tables to truncate by following the foreign keys from jobs, vehicles, customers (and the base list) minus a keep list, and `src/lib/clear-test-data.ts` backs up every table but `audit_log` as gzip chunks of 2,000 rows (a failed table is a warning, never a stop), then empties the job buckets 100 files at a time (every step repeatable). Price imports: `price_imports` + `price_import_lines` (before/after snapshots) written by `applyImport`, `undoImport` puts exactly that back, "Undo last import (n)" in the Parts top bar (`lastImport` prop from the page); `parsePaste(text, valuesPerRow)` ignores blank lines, `guessValuesPerRow`, `guessMappingFromData` for headerless tables, `MAPPING_CHOICES` (Part number, Name, Qty, Price, Availability, Ignore), `compactPartNumber` for matching ("A 167 330 68 00" = "A1673306800"), availability kinds same_day/days/back_order/confirm → Same day = to_order with 0 days, notes in `part_items.availability_note` (shown on the status line through `infoOf`). Bulk delete: `parts/bulk-actions.ts` (`bulkDeleteParts` keeps ordered/received/issued/fitted parts, sets `is_active=false` + `deleted_at/deleted_by/delete_batch` on parts and requests, `undoBulkDelete` by batch), tick boxes (`data-row-select`, `data-select-all`), `data-delete-all`, the one question (`data-delete-confirm`), the minute toast (`data-delete-toast`, `data-undo-delete`). Lessons: a learned suggestion chip can carry the same words as a condition chip, so tests pick the first exact button; a test must wait for an undo that works line by line to be marked done before reading counts; the stage seed's gate-in media are legacy rows.

List of 14 October 2026 (migrations `20261027090000_quote_layout_commission_lpo.sql`, `20261027100000_inspection_media_remove.sql`; note `docs/14-oct-note.md`; test `tests/browser/test-h.mjs` = `npm run test:h`): inspection photos per tyre and on the vent reading (`InspectionMedia iconOnly`, `where.itemKey` `tyre.<pos>` / `measure.vent`, thumbnails with X through `POST /api/inspection-media/remove` setting `inspection_media.removed_at`, shown on the report link and PDF next to the reading), one `Lightbox` everywhere (slides of kind image or video, "n of m", Esc), the Photo / Gallery / Video row with the phone icon (`QuickCodeButton icon`), the Saved tick next to the item name (`data-item-saved`). Quotation: `quotation_lines.hours_estimate` (the "est." box; `quoteTotals` uses `labourEstimateHours` until `clockedHours` is passed, label "Labour at cost (h estimate|clocked × AED rate)"), `unit_cost` on service lines as "our cost" (`lineCost`, `isOutsideWork`), the labour discount in `quotations.discount_kind/discount_value` (`labourDiscountOf`, `takesLabourDiscount`; a null kind keeps the legacy `discount_percent` on labour and services), commission in `quotations.commission_to/kind/value/aed` (`commission_people` setting, `CommissionBox`, counted in `totalCost`, never on a customer output, `commission_paid_at/by` with "Mark paid" on `/reports/monthly` through `markCommissionPaid`; `MonthlySummary.commissions`), markup floor 5% (`parts_min_markup_percent`) with no cap: below goes back to the floor (owner excepted), at or above `confirmPercent` the save route asks once and `markup_confirmed` is stored. PDFs: `SectionBox` + `TotalsBox` in `src/lib/pdf/template.tsx`, used by the quotation, estimate and invoice PDFs (numbers restart per section, availability under the part name, part numbers never, green discount and Round-off rows). Parts: `PartsTable` `pricingLocked` (`jobs.parts_pricing_done_at` and no `parts_pricing_reopened_at`) with Edit (`reopenPricing`) and "Send updated prices" (`pricingDone` notifies "Prices changed on <car>" and names a sent quotation), the dashboard "Priced" chip, custom days as their own option ("15 days", `daysComplete`), `OUR_STOCK`/`isOurStock` in `parts-rows.ts` (the tidy trigger stores "Our Stock"). LPO flow in `src/lib/lpo.ts`: `createMainLpo` (one `purchase_orders.kind = 'main'` per job, every supplier, `sendLpoForApproval` in `parts/lpo-actions.ts`), `decidePurchaseOrder` approve of a main LPO calls `splitMainLpo` (supplier LPOs `kind = 'supplier'`, `parent_id`, `sub_number`, number `<main>-<n>`; parts re-pointed to their supplier LPO line), `orderSupplierLpo` (`markSupplierOrdered`: dates from `leadDays` = delivery_date − priced_at day, counted from the order day), `setPartExpected`, `finishPartsPlanning` (sets `plan_parts_done_at/ready_date` when nothing is left to order and tells the manager; also called from `applyCustomerResponse` when every part is from stock). `SupplierLpoCard` (Copy, PDF `/api/pdf/lpo/[token]`, Ordered, date chips). The Parts job page shows the summary and the one button instead of the planning card (`PartsBar`/`PlanParts` deleted; `partsPlanned`/`setPartPlan`/`allInStock` remain for the e2e route); the LPO page groups a main LPO by supplier with subtotals; the LPO list hides an approved main LPO. PDF import: `src/lib/pdf-read.ts` (mupdf, now a dependency and in `serverExternalPackages`; `readPdfRows` rebuilds cells from character gaps and joins lines on one baseline, drops page footers, finds the VIN; `renderPdfPages` for scans), `src/lib/import-ocr.ts` (`ocrTable`, shared by the route and the scanned-PDF path), `parseImportUpload` returns `scanned`, "Upload PDF" in `ImportWizard`; `linesFromRows` skips rows with no quantity and no price unless they have both a part number and a name; `TOTAL_LABELS` accepts "VAT 5%". `Card` passes data attributes through. Lessons: the tidy trigger title-cases supplier text, so fixed names are compared case-insensitively; Node 24 runs a `.ts` file with no imports directly (`import("file:///…/price-import.ts")`), handy for parser checks without the dev server; a `Card` without prop spreading swallows `data-*` test hooks.

Bodyshop of 14 October 2026, evening (migrations `20261028090000_bodyshop.sql`, `20261028093000_bodyshop_settings.sql`, `20261028100000_booked_gate_in_stamp.sql`, `20261028103000_job_files_bodyshop_kinds.sql`; note `docs/bodyshop-note.md`; test `tests/browser/test-i.mjs` = `npm run test:i`, screenshots written to `tests/browser/shots/`, ignored by git). Vocabulary in `src/lib/bodyshop-kinds.ts` (pure: `BODYSHOP_KINDS`, `SECTIONS`, `STAGE_DEFS`, `stageKeysFor`, `spreadDueDates`, `neededSignoffs`, `signoffKeyOf`, `sectionOfDepartment`); everything else in `src/lib/bodyshop.ts` (server). A bodyshop quotation before arrival is a job with `status = 'booked'`, `is_open = false`, `department = 'bodyshop'` (`createBodyshopJob` from `/bodyshop/new`); `applyCustomerResponse` leaves a booked job alone and tells the advisor (`bodyshop_booked`); `bookArrival` writes `promised_at`, `booked_arrival_at` and a `car_drop` appointment; `requestDeposit` makes a proforma with `invoices.deposit_for_quotation_id`, which `ensureProforma` folds into the final proforma (payments moved, `converted_to` set; `jobBalance` and `convertProformaIfPaid` skip deposit proformas). The gate-in action reuses the booked job through `bookedJobOf` (update, not insert); the media register route calls `startBodyshopWork` when the checklist is complete: `bodyshop_stages` from `services.bodyshop_kind` on the approved lines, due dates spread to the promised day, status `in_work`. Stages: `setStageDone` (Refit needs every `bodyshop_removed_items` row ticked back; the last stage before handover puts the car at Ready, prepares the proforma, opens the sign-off), `reopenStage`, `setStageDue` (the handover stage moves `promised_at` and tells everyone), `addStage`/`removeStage`/`moveStage`, `shiftAfterLateStage` + `bodyshopHourly` (cron step 16: late stages, due-out-tomorrow notices), heads-ups to the next section's leaders. People: role `team_leader` + department `detailing`; `bodyshopPeople()` groups managers (workshop managers of bodyshop departments), leaders and staff by section; `bodyshop_assignments` (one tap, "Need help"), `bodyshop_clockings` (`clockIn`/`clockOut`, one open per person; `bodyshopLabourCost` by department rate); shared section tablets carry `devices.section` (registration form) and open `/tablet/bodyshop` (`SectionTablet` + `POST /api/tablet/bodyshop` with the PIN, no session). Sign-off: `bodyshop_signoffs` (`signOff`, owner `on_behalf`, `notReady` reopens the section's stage and moves the handover a working day), `signoffState` locks `gateOutJob`; `afterBodyshopHandover` tells everyone and books the final check visit (`jobs.final_check_appointment_id`, a customer_visit appointment). Customer side: `bodyshop_updates` (kind arrived/film/ready, photo from `job_files` kind `bodyshop_photo`, public `/progress/[token]`), `jobs.bodyshop_handover_token` → `/handover/[token]` and `/api/pdf/warranty/[token]` (`src/lib/bodyshop-public.ts`, `src/lib/pdf/warranty-pdf.tsx`); settings `ppf_brand_name`, `ppf_warranty_years`, `ppf_warranty_text`, `ppf_care_text`, `ppf_removal_note`, `bodyshop_final_check_days`, `whatsapp_bodyshop_*_template`, `whatsapp_final_check_template` (Settings > Bodyshop). Quotation: `quotation_lines.film_colour/film_code/tint_percent`, `quotations.materials_cost_aed` (internal box Materials row, `quoteTotals.materialsCost`), `removal_ack_at/name` (the `/api/quote/[token]` route requires `removal_ack` when a `ppf_removal` line exists), the PPF warranty block on the link and the PDF, `EditorSettings.lastPrices`. Screens: `/bodyshop` (board, `BodyshopBoard`), `/board/bodyshop` (TV, registered device), `/bodyshop/mine`, `/bodyshop/[jobId]`, `/bodyshop/new`; the job card redirects booked jobs there and links bodyshop cars there; `/assign` lists the electrical resets; `/reports/monthly` has the bodyshop block (`bodyshopMonth`). The e2e seed `stage: "bodyshop"` with `at: sent | approved | in_work | ready` makes the leaders and an installer as staff with auth users (`E2e …`, PIN 1234); the clean-up SQL removes them and the `E2e …` devices. Lessons: a `.select()` string built by concatenation loses its type, so cast the result; `Card` passes `data-*` through; the first-import regex must allow `"use server"` on line one; the `protect_job_stamps` trigger lets the gate-in stamp change while a job is still `booked` (the booked car's real arrival) and never after; a new `job_files` kind needs the `job_files_kind_check` constraint widened as well as `FILE_KINDS`; a stage's Done closes the open clockings on it, so the next tablet tap clocks in again.

Labour estimate fix of 15 October 2026 (migration `20261029090000_fix_labour_estimates.sql`, note `docs/labour-estimate-fix-note.md`, test `tests/browser/test-j.mjs` = `npm run test:j`): the "est." box took the hourly rate (350) and labour at cost read 1,400 h. Every hour-based labour line now shows three labelled boxes, Hours (h), Est. h (owner only, grey, defaults to the quoted hours) and the rate (AED/h, saved on blur through `rateBlur`), in a `data-labour-boxes` group that wraps under the name on narrow cards (the shared `controlClass` is `w-full`, so these boxes carry inline widths). `quotes.ts`: `estimateOf`, `estimateLineSuspect` (over `ESTIMATE_MAX_LINE_HOURS` = 50), `ESTIMATE_TOTAL_FACTOR` = 3; `quoteTotals` returns `labourEstimateEntered`, `estimateSuspectLineIds`, `estimateTotalSuspect`, and the quoted hours stand in for flagged figures (amber `data-estimate-warning` on the line, `data-estimate-cost-warning` in the internal box, `data-estimate-footer` "Workshop estimate X h · quoted Y h" using the same X as the internal box, with the manager's approval figure appended when it differs). The migration reset estimates equal to the rate, over 50 h or over three times the quoted hours (Q-00004 v1, four lines). Lesson: a width class on `Input` loses to `controlClass`'s `w-full` by CSS order, so a fixed-width box needs an inline `style={{ width }}`; React splits text around `{expr}` with a comment node, so HTML text checks collapse whitespace first.

List of 15 October 2026 (migration `20261030090000_15_oct_changes.sql`, note `docs/15-oct-note.md`, test `tests/browser/test-k.mjs` = `npm run test:k`): revising a sent quotation (`reviseQuotation` makes version n+1 with its own `token`, every header choice and `quotation_lines.revised_from_line_id`; `customers.credit_terms_days/_set_by/_at` (owner-only block in `CustomerForm`, `creditTermsOf`, gate-out `creditDays`); `settings.damage_note_chips` + `DamageNote chips` (autosave in a promise chain, `savedBefore` ignores a stale refresh, no Save button); `TYRE_POSITIONS`/`WHEEL_KINDS` order fl, rl, rr, fr; `services.line_kind` with `isFixedService`, `PINNED_LABOUR`/`PINNED_SERVICES`/`pinnedRank` (Labour charges first; Minor at 750 and Major as services), the software items split (migration DO block on `inspection_checklist`); `quotations.discount_scope` (`takesLabourDiscount(l, scope)`, `isOwnService`, `discountLabelOf` used by the editor, link, PDFs and invoice); `quotations.markup_skipped_at/by` (`completeQuotation(id, { skipMarkup })` returns `ask: "markup"`, `MonthlySummary.markupSkipped`); capitals by CSS `uppercase` on the editor's name boxes, the pickers, `CustomerFront` strong text and `textTransform` on the PDF `desc`/`boxStrong` styles; `hasLabourChargesLine`/`tasksFromInspection` on the technician's screen, the work order and the job card; `countedMinutes` in `working-time.ts` behind `sessionMinutes(s, now, brk, wt)`, `clockedMinutes(rows, now, wt)`, the bodyshop end-of-shift close (`closed_reason`) and cron step 10b (`clock_overnight`, `overnight_notified_at`); `sticker_battery_mm`/`sticker_service_mm`, `PrintOne` (`body.printing-one .one:not(.print-this)` hidden in print); `src/lib/parts-steps.ts` (`partsSteps`, `stepPartOf`) + `PartsSteps` (bar on `/parts/[jobId]` with `#pricing`, `#lpo`, `#lpos`, `#received`, dots on `/parts` and the job card), `receivePartInFull` + `receivePartAction`; `.erp-field-msg` absolute under `.erp-field-host`. Lessons: a quoted heredoc here collapses a double backslash to one, so patch scripts with backslashes go through the Write tool or `String.raw`; `Notice` does not pass `data-*` through (wrap it); `limit 1` without an order in a test is a coin toss once several rows exist; a new column needs the shared select (`SERVICE_SELECT`) or the screens never see it.

Evening of 15 October 2026 (migration `20261031090000_evening_15_oct.sql`, note `docs/evening-15-oct-note.md`, test `tests/browser/test-l.mjs` = `npm run test:l`): a priced part always lands on a quotation. `src/lib/quote-versions.ts` holds `COPY_FIELDS` (now with price_per, left_off, recovery_type, hours_estimate, film and tint, recovery paid-by), `createNextVersion` (the DB part of `makeVersion`, token of its own, `syncMissingParts` at the end), `quoteTargetFor` (the open draft, else the next version from the latest sent/approved one plus the `quote_revised_by_parts` notification with `?send=1`), `syncPartLine` (moved from `parts/actions.ts`, which keeps a `syncLineForPart` wrapper and a `draftQuotation` helper for option groups; logs `parts_added/changed/removed` quotation events), `missingPricedParts`. `completeQuotation` returns `ask: "parts"` with the missing names (`addMissingParts` action, `skipParts` logged as `parts_left_off`); the editor's `data-parts-ask` dialog has Add / Leave off. The one-off check `POST /api/e2e/fix-missing-parts` (local, `dryRun`) added ENGINE OIL 0W20 ×9 to Q-00005 v2 on J-00111; nothing approved or invoiced was affected. Parts desk `/parts`: `?q=` search (plate, job number, VIN, customer; closed jobs too) and `data-all-cars` rows (`carRow`, green `data-all-done` when every step is done, `data-gone` when closed). Ordered parts: `RemoveDialog` kind `ordered` offers Return to supplier / Keep in stock (`notNeededFromParts` reads `disposition` and calls `setDisposition`). Quote page: `onRevise` (bottom bar `data-revise-bar`) and `autoSend` props on `QuoteEditor`, `SendQuoteControl autoOpen`, the Revise card gone, `data-mark-declined` details, `data-declined-next` card with `declineToGateOut` (`jobs.declined_out_at`, `declined_charge_invoice_id`; cancels open requests, `dropParts` declined, `job_declined_out` to the managers, optional `createChargeInvoice` in `invoice-flow.ts`: tax invoice with one services line) and the gate-out's free release (`declinedFree` when nothing is owed). `Shell` main has `pb-28`. Lessons: a test that simulates a gate-out must not touch `gated_out_at` (the stamp trigger refuses and the e2e sql helper returns nothing); `issue_status` is not nullable, reset it with `default`; a `until` must wait for the last step of a multi-step server action (the version, its lines, the supersede, then the notification), not the first row.


Night of 15 to 16 October 2026 (migrations `20261101090000_payment_flow_and_stock.sql`, `20261101093000_stock_order_lines.sql`; note `docs/night-15-oct-note.md`; tests `test-m` payment flow, `test-n` chooser and car photo, `test-o` Share PDF, viewer and supplier email, `test-p` parts, LPOs and stock). Money: `src/lib/money-steps.ts` (pure `moneyState` with the five steps Job done / Proforma sent / Payment / Tax invoice / Released, the chip, the one action, `methodsFor(role)`: accounts transfer/cheque/link, advisors cash/card, owner all; `confirmFullText`), `src/lib/money-data.ts` (`loadMoneyRows`, `loadMoneyTiles`, `loadCounterPaymentsToConfirm`), `MoneyRow`/`MoneyStepLine`, the accounts dashboard (`AccountsDashboard`, rendered by `/dashboard` for the accounts role) and `/invoices` rebuilt as money rows with filters; `/jobs/[id]/invoice` is the four-step page (`data-step`, `data-state`, the payment form folded under step 2 while the proforma is unsent); `ReadySendControl` asks the expected method (`prepareProformaSend` saves it with Pay now or a pasted link, `markReadySent` records the send and tells accounts `proforma_sent`); `PaymentForm` props `methods`/`charges`/`expected`/`nextTaxNumber`, the full-payment confirmation is a `type="button"` opening `data-confirm-full` whose Yes calls `requestSubmit()` (a React `onSubmit` cannot be used: `FormGuard`'s capture listener locks the form first); `customerSaysPaid`, `addPayNow`; `convertProformaIfPaid` tells the advisor `invoice_paid` "Paid: <plate>, AED x"; `src/lib/ngenius.ts` (N-Genius Online, env `NGENIUS_API_KEY`, `NGENIUS_OUTLET_REF`, `NGENIUS_ENV` sandbox/live/mock, `NGENIUS_WEBHOOK_SECRET`; the mock keeps its state on the `payment_links` row because a dev server does not share memory between routes), `src/lib/pay-now.ts` (`settleNgeniusOrder` asks the gateway and records through `recordPaymentCore` with `clientKey ngenius:<ref>` and a null actor), routes `/api/pay/ngenius/webhook|return|mock`, `ensureAutoPayLink` in `payment-links.ts` following the balance; cron step 17 `payment_waiting_long` (`settings.payment_waiting_hours`). `SharePdfButton` (share sheet on phones only, download with a hint on PCs, `/api/share-log`), `SendLinkDialog pdf`/`note` props, `DocViewer` behind every `CustomerFront view` (pages from `/api/pdf-pages?src=/api/pdf/...&page=n`, `renderPdfPage`/`pdfPageCount` in `pdf-read.ts`, `/api/pdf-pages` in `PUBLIC_PREFIXES`); `src/lib/gmail.ts` (service account JWT, env `GOOGLE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_EMAIL`/`GOOGLE_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_KEY`, settings `supplier_lpo_from_email`/`supplier_lpo_email_text`) and `emailSupplierLpo` with `purchase_orders.emailed_*`. Parts: `src/lib/stock.ts` (`recordStockMovement` with the weighted average cost, `addPartToStock`, `notifyLowStock`; `stock_movements`, `stock_items.part_number/reorder_quantity/supplier_id/last_counted_at/low_notified_at`), stock orders are `purchase_orders.kind = 'stock'` with `job_id` null and lines with `stock_item_id` (`parts/stock/actions.ts`; `orderSupplierLpo`, `markOrdered`, `decidePurchaseOrder` and `receivePurchaseOrder` skip the job and part steps for them), the supplier invoice at receipt (`applySupplierInvoice`, `purchase_order_lines.invoice_check` match/mismatch/accepted/disputed above AED 1, `decideInvoiceCheck`), price memory (`/api/parts/price-memory?pn=`, the `data-price-memory` chip on the Parts table's status line), the LPO list with tabs/search/supplier filter, `/parts/suppliers/totals`, Returns and leftovers (`disposition = 'scrap'`, `data-no-home` cards), the labels prompt `data-labels-prompt`, QC's `stickerPrompts` (`sticker_prompt`), cron step 18 (low stock, `stock_count_reminded_for`). `CustomerCarChooser` + `CustomerCarStartForm` (client wrapper: a server page cannot pass a render function to a client `ActionForm`) + `src/lib/customer-car.ts` (`resolveCustomerCar`, duplicates by phone, VIN or plate, `/api/lookup`) on `/estimates/new` and `/bodyshop/new`; `CarThumb`/`CarHeaderThumb` with `PageHeader picture`; `LinkButton` passes data attributes through; the TV boards are deleted. Lessons: two foreign keys between two tables (jobs.declined_charge_invoice_id and invoices.job_id) make every unhinted embed fail silently, so embeds name the key (`jobs!invoices_job_id_fkey`); the Supabase CLI could not resolve the database host (IPv6 only) while Node could, so a migration applied with `scripts/db-sql.mjs` is recorded with `scripts/db-record-migration.mjs`; Git Bash turns a leading `/path` argument into a Windows path, so pass `MSYS_NO_PATHCONV=1` to scripts that take URL paths; the e2e sql helper hides insert errors, so a NOT NULL column (`purchase_order_lines.part_item_id`) made the stock order's lines vanish silently; Edge on Windows has `navigator.share`, so the share sheet is phone-only by user agent; headless Edge needs `data-*` hooks on `Card`/`LinkButton`, not on `Notice`.

Scan reports are read from the Google Workspace mailbox (`src/lib/gmail.ts` scope-aware `accessToken`, `listMessages/getMessage/getAttachment`, `SCANS_MAILBOX` env; `src/lib/mailbox.ts` `processMail` + `checkScansMailbox`, VIN from subject → body → `readPdfRows`, `inbound_scans.gmail_message_id` dedupe, notifications `scan_received`/`scan_unmatched`; cron `/api/cron/mailbox` every five minutes in `vercel.json`; "Check the mailbox now" on `/scans`; settings `scans_mailbox_last_check`; local test route `POST /api/e2e/mailbox`); the Postmark route `/api/inbound/scan`, `SecretAddress` and the settings card are deleted. Reading is by a one-time Google sign-in as scans@ofjauto.com, not delegation (migration `20261103100000_scans_mailbox_oauth.sql`): `src/lib/google-mailbox.ts` (`consentUrl` with `gmail.readonly` only, `login_hint`, `access_type=offline`, `prompt=consent`; `connectWithCode` checks the profile address against `SCANS_MAILBOX` and hands a wrong account's permission back; `saveConnection` stores the refresh token AES-256-GCM sealed with a key from the client secret in `google_mailbox_tokens` (RLS on, no policies, no audit trigger); `mailboxAccessToken` writes `last_error` and tells the owner once, notification `scan_mailbox_problem`; `disconnectMailbox` revokes), routes `/api/google/mailbox/connect` (owner, state cookie `erp_mailbox_state`) and `/callback` (state check, `after()` runs the first read); Vercel variables `GOOGLE_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`, `GOOGLE_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`, `SCANS_MAILBOX`; the service account in `gmail.ts` is for the supplier LPO email only. test-w needs the dev server started with test values for the three variables; the e2e mailbox route's `connectTest` refuses when a real mailbox is connected (the database is shared). Lessons: a route file with JSX must be `route.tsx`; a path taken out of `PUBLIC_PREFIXES` redirects to login rather than 404; `String.replace` with `$&` in the replacement corrupts a patch (use split/join); the dev server kept stale `src/lib/pdf` modules until `.next` was deleted; a bash heredoc with an apostrophe fails whole, so long notes are written by the editor and appended.

DB triggers `quotation_lines_lock` (no insert/update/delete on lines of approved or superseded quotations except `line_total`, `customer_approved`, `updated_*`) and `quotations_lock` (money/header choice columns frozen). `replaceProformaAfterRevision` in `invoice-flow.ts` (called by `applyCustomerResponse` on `revisionOfApproved`): the old proforma cancelled, a new one from the approved lines, payments carried across. Lessons: Next memoizes identical GETs within one render, so a supabase select repeated after an insert comes back with the pre-insert answer — take the row from `.insert().select()` and put `.abortSignal(new AbortController().signal)` on a lookup that may repeat; a client component's string props can be asserted from `page.content()` (the flight payload carries them), which is how the driver message is tested.

Stage-box fix of 18 October, evening: `src/lib/stage-style.ts` is the ONE source for stage colours (done green / current amber / later grey, `stageStates` fact-driven: approval, report approved, quote approved, parts all received, qc passed, wash done, proforma paid; first undone = current); the job card bar takes `states`, the big boxes use `STAGE_BOX_CARD` with ! variants. Lessons: Card's own bg-white beats an appended background class (stylesheet order, not class order) so a tinted Card needs `!bg-*`; `bg-green-soft/50` reads as white; and a test that checks class NAMES can pass while the computed colour is still white - assert `getComputedStyle(el).backgroundColor` for colour proofs.

20 October 2026, part 2 (note `docs/20-oct-part2-note.md`; migrations `20261110190000_supplier_invoice_date_paid.sql`, `20261110210000_advance_payment_step.sql`; tests `test:waiting`, `test:invoice2`, `test:advance`, `test:commission`, `test:discount`, `test:instant`, `test:fixed`, `test:oldparts`): accounts' Waiting on you carries every money action (`src/lib/accounts-waiting.ts` `accountsWaitingExtra` → `WaitingPanel extra` with `always: true`; cash to confirm & bank with the button on the money page's deposit rows too, deposits asked, LPOs to approve, invoices to follow/check, recovery trips and months). Supplier invoices: number AND date compulsory (`supplier_invoice_date`), "to follow" = amber "Invoice missing" + daily Parts reminder (cron step 24, `invoice_reminder_at`, type `supplier_invoice_missing`); `markSupplierPaid` (owner/accounts) refuses without number+date+scan (`supplier_paid_at/by/reference`); several invoice files with thumbnails and × (`job_files.removed_at/by`, `/api/files/remove`, `JobFileUpload removable`+`caption`, `InvoiceFiles`). Lessons: the approved-quote lock silently refuses `deposit_mode/value` via the e2e sql route (`deposit_aed` alone passes); SQL-set discounts must keep stored totals consistent or `roundQuotation` refuses and the send dies silently; `Collapsible` mounts children only when open (a button, not `<details>`); the "quote" stage seed carries a pre-made token (assert `valid_until`, not token); `payments.customer_id` is NOT NULL without default.

Overnight 20 to 21 October 2026, part 2 (note `docs/overnight-21-oct-note.md`): the nine hand-off rules R1-R9 and the one map of every hand-over live in `docs/workflow-map.md` (2.1/2.2). 2.5: ONE supplier-invoice box everywhere - `src/lib/supplier-invoice.ts` (pure `invoiceNumberLooksReal`, `checkSupplierInvoice` with the 5% `needsAmountConfirm` gate, `dubaiToday`) + `src/components/SupplierInvoiceBox.tsx` (fields-only, inside the caller's form: invoice_number/date/amount, to_follow, amount_confirmed, scan_path; uploads kind `supplier_invoice` refId `poId ?? recv:<clientKey>`), used by `ReceivePanel` (now a real form; rows carry `poId`, `poTotals` prop; the amount check only when every ticked part sits on ONE LPO), `PoForms.tsx` ReceiveForm/SupplierInvoiceForm (`poTotal` prop) and enforced in `receiveTicked` (checks BEFORE receiving; explicit to_follow -> status + re-point `recv:` files to the first LPO), `receivePurchaseOrder` (pre-check) and `applySupplierInvoice` (`invoice_amount ?? amount`); test `npm run test:supinv`. 2.6/2.7: `npm run test:sim` (`tests/browser/sim-e2e.mjs`, PATHS=a..h) walks eight paths as every role (technician 800px) with R1-R9 checks and actor+next-screen screenshots in `tests/browser/shots/sim/`; it CAUGHT that `loadWaitingOnYou` and the dashboard never read `jobs.work_done_at`/`wash_sent_at`, so a finished job still said "In work, waiting on the technician" and the manager's confirm task never reached his Waiting list - both loaders now pass `workFinishedAt`/`washSentAt`. Sim lessons: the e2e sql route returns `date` columns as JS Dates (select `::text` to compare); "Quotation complete" does NOTHING but scroll while blockers exist, and its pending label is "Checking…", so tests wait on `quotations.completed_at`, never on the button; the completion dialogs are `data-parts-add/-leave`, `data-deposit-skip`, `data-low-profit-send`, `data-markup-continue`; the revision flow is `data-ask-revise` -> `[data-revise-ask] textarea` + `data-ask-revise-send`, owner `data-revision-allow`, then the enabled `form [data-revise-bar]`; `ChoiceButtons` render labels (getByText, not getByRole); the driver page is photo -> price -> SEND; the technician takes parts only after the desk's `[data-mark-ready]`, then ticks `[data-part-take]` + `[data-receive-pin]` + `[data-receive-parts]`; pricing a part in stock on an APPROVED job starts the Parts-revision ask by design (seed `part_items.cost_aed` + mark stray requests unavailable instead); deposit modes are `aed`/`percent` (not `fixed`), commissions need `commission_set_at` to list.

Security lockdown of 21 October 2026 (migrations `20261111200000_security_lockdown.sql` + `..210000_security_lockdown2.sql`, note `docs/security-21-oct-note.md`, after-table `docs/security-table-after.md`): the advisor warnings audited - RLS was already on for all 91 public tables with no anon policies (the rls-off finding was the recovery tables, fixed 19 Oct), and live anon probes read nothing; what remained was surface: anon's dormant grants on 40 tables, six SECURITY DEFINER views, PUBLIC execute on every function, and token tables reachable by authenticated. All removed: anon holds nothing (tables/sequences/functions + default privileges for postgres; supabase_admin's own default ACLs cannot be altered by postgres and only apply to platform objects), the six views are dropped with their eight call sites reading `customers`/`jobs` narrowly through `createAdminClient` (calendar form-data, dashboard, vehicles list+page, my-jobs, job-data, workshop, assign, search), functions execute only for authenticated+service_role (policies need authenticated EXECUTE - revoking PUBLIC without that grant breaks every has_role policy), and `short_links`/`payment_requests`/`payment_links`/`setup_links`/`quick_codes`/`google_mailbox_tokens` grant nothing to anon or authenticated (server-only; `staff_private` keeps its owner-only policies because team actions write it as the owner through `createClient`). `npm run test:security` (tests/security-check.mjs) enforces all of it plus live anon probes and runs with test:layout after every batch; `supabase/migration-template.sql` is the new-table pattern. test-perms B updated: since the 1.3 money-page rebuild the advisor sees Send first and "Waiting for accounts" only after the proforma is sent. Lessons: `revoke ... from anon` leaves function EXECUTE via the built-in PUBLIC grant (revoke from PUBLIC and grant authenticated+service_role explicitly, defaults too); trigger functions do not check EXECUTE at fire time, policy functions DO check it for the querying role; the two lockdown migrations applied with db-sql.mjs AFTER the code deploy because the live build still read the views until then.

pick-up = delivery (one TripActions component, driver page two steps loaded photo then delivered, markTripLoadedByDriver, a price>0 always completes); the inspection fee per car (jobs.inspection_fee_aed/_request_aed/_request_by, premium_brands/premium_brand_fee_aed settings, gate-in chips 750/1000/1500/2000+Other with data-inspection-fee on the chips DIV - Field swallows data-*, below-750 by non-owners becomes the owner's data-fee-request card via decideInspectionFee, the approval link snapshots amount+notices EN/AR from the job, locked at first_approval_at with the stale-form refusal, badge data-fee-badge);
