scan reports live (inbound_scans in the publication, LiveRefresh pollMs=20000 on my-jobs/job card/inspection/scans, ScanStepCard follows the server file list by the prev-key setState-in-render pattern - setState in an effect is refused by the React compiler, received line + data-flash-id=jobId); Lessons: $1 used as both uuid and text in one e2e-sql statement fails with inconsistent types deduced (pass two params); a controlled Other-box keeps its old figure, so a test clears it before typing (triple-click + Backspace); browserFor's BROWSER.newPage() has no cookies - new pages come from the CONTEXT; approval_requests rows are not made by the stage seed (first_approval_at only); quotations have no approved_amount column; the live publication is checked with pg_publication_tables, and adding a table needs only the guarded alter publication.

Evening batch of 21 October 2026 (note `docs/evening-21-oct-note.md`; migrations `20261113090000` to `20261114090000`; tests `test:commission1|commission2|owner3|advisor4|capitals|bodyshop6|pkgcost|packages8|pkgdisplay9|ppfcut10|tint11|warranty12|doc13`): one commission per job (a new version carries it; the old version keeps a tombstone, AED 0, `commission_status = 'superseded'`); `commission_payments` (CPV- vouchers with the signed voucher or slip, `quotations.commission_payment_id`, `/commissions`); the owner edits reports and approves his own asks directly; `jobs.advisor_id` + `jobAdvisorIds`/`notifyJobAdvisors` (car notices go to the advisor in charge and followers; `OWNER_QUIET_TYPES`); typed capitals are kept (`text-case.ts` converts nothing); bodyshop quotes start as estimates with `?packages=1`; `src/lib/package-cost.ts` fed by `packageCostingOf` (tint factor and double-layer hours from the tint spec); package snapshots are OBJECTS, read only through `toSnapshot`/`packageParts`/`packageDocOf`; `src/lib/ppf-cut.ts` (`cutOf`, `cutWords`, `lineIsPpf` - a plain line counts only when `isPpfFilmItem`, `ppfFinishOf`). Tint: `src/lib/tint.ts` (pure: rows, `SHADES`, `tintSummary`, `decorateTint`, `lineTintWords`, `aboveLimit`, `tintCostFactor`), `src/lib/tint-data.ts` (`tintSpecForJob`, `lastTintSpecFor`, `tintStageStarted`), `quotations.tint_spec/tint_ack_at/tint_ack_name`, `TintSpecPanel` → `TintSpecBox` through the editor's `tintBox` prop (LockedQuote too), `quotes/tint-actions.ts` (`saveTintSpec` logs `tint_changed` once sent), settings `tint_legal_max_darkness`/`tint_double_layer_hours`/`tint_presets`/`whatsapp_tint_update_template`, the PPF tablet's multipart `tint_photo` action. Warranty: `src/lib/warranty.ts` (pure terms, periods, `PPF_AREAS`, `warrantyStatusOf`, `fieldOf`), `src/lib/warranty-data.ts` (`warrantyCardOf` is the ONE card model; `ensureWarrantyToken`, `stageAsks`, `rowMissing`, `saveWarrantyEntry`), table `warranty_entries`, `jobs.warranty_token/warranty_confirmed_at/by/warranty_card_sent_at`, `aftercare_checks.reminded_12_at/reminded_20_at/activated_at/by/activation_note`; `setStageDone` refuses ppf/tint/detail without the details and QC without `warranty_confirmed_at`; `WarrantySection` + `WarrantyPanel` in the PDF template (tax invoice and certificate), `WarrantyCardPanel` on web pages, public `/warranty/[token]`, `/warranties`, `/settings/warranty` (keeps `ppf_warranty_years/text` in step), cron step 23 = reminders day 8/12/20 (`aftercareHourly({ jobIds })`, local route `/api/e2e/aftercare`). Documents: `LABEL_CELL`/`AMOUNT_CELL` on every totals row, `isNamelessTitle` in `sendBlockers`, `isHidden` = any line with `visible_to_customer = false` (plus the bank-charge fee), `INTERNAL_ONLY_LABEL`. Lessons: a production probe script must live under `tests/browser` (node resolves `mupdf` from the script's folder); flipping a test proforma to a tax invoice must change its number too or the next proforma number collides; `\b` inside a bash-quoted `node -e` patch lands as a backspace character - write regex patches with the Write tool and `String.raw`; a small react-pdf table needs `wrap={false}` or its rows continue on the next page without the heading.

Parts markup as % or AED, 22 October 2026 (migration `20261115090000_parts_markup_aed.sql`, test `npm run test:markup`): `quotation_lines.markup_mode` ('percent' | 'aed', default percent) and `markup_aed` (fixed AED per unit); `markup_percent` widened to numeric(14,6) (it capped at 9,999.99%). One price rule in `quotes.ts`: `costPlusMarkup` behind `linePrice`/`lineUnitPrice` (percent keeps the old cost × (1 + m) × qty rounding; aed = (cost + aed) × qty), `markupBoth` (both forms + the unit selling price, shown on every part line), `effectiveMarkupPercent` (minimum, floor and the "still at the minimum?" reminder read this), `markupFromSell` (a typed selling price kept in the line's own mode, six decimals for percent), `minimumMarkupAed`. No upper limit and no high-markup question any more (the save route's confirm, the `sendBlockers` markup blocker and the amber highlight are gone; settings `markup_confirm_percent`/`markup_warn_percent` are unused for now); the minimum still pulls an advisor's figure back up in either mode, the owner may go below (`markup_confirmed`, event `markup_below_minimum`). Editor: one "Markup (% or AED)" cell with the switch (switching keeps the selling price), the box, and "31.00% · AED 296.30" underneath; the Selling box is typed per unit; "Apply to all" has its own %/AED switch (AED is per unit, the minimum applied per line). test-h's two stale checks (the ORDER NOW wording, commission paid through vouchers) were brought up to date.


Parts import fix and safety lock, 22 October 2026 (migration `20261116090000_supplier_charges.sql`, note `docs/22-oct-import-lock-note.md`, tests `npm run test:import` (pure, every supplier PDF a permanent fixture) and `npm run test:importlock` (screens)): the Autovivo PDF ("Sl No. | Part No. | Description | Qty | Price | Total", Part No. empty) slid every value one column left. PDF tables are now read by their HEADINGS: `src/lib/pdf-cells.ts` (`readPdfCells(mupdf, buf)`, no server imports, so tests run it) keeps each cell's x/x2; `alignRowsToHeadings` places cells under the heading they sit beneath, with column edges learnt from the data (`learnAnchors`: text left edges, number right edges) and a right-anchored re-read when qty × price ≠ total; `pdfImport` = dealer blocks → headings → cells → plain lines, shared by `parseImportUpload` and the tests. ImportField `serial` (Sl No., exact headings only), `finishLines` (line numbers in the part-number column cleared, `kind: "charge"` for courier/freight words or an unnumbered priced line in a numbered table, `lineChecks` red qty×price≠total / amber qty > `IMPORT_MAX_QTY` 50 or line > `IMPORT_MAX_LINE_AED` 500,000), `checkTotals` by arithmetic (Total + VAT = Net Total) and single-cell "Net Amount: 2,518.00", `importLock` (the ONE rule, screen and `serverLock` in `applyImport`; a totals mismatch needs a typed reason stored in `price_imports.totals_override`), `companyInText`/`supplierMatches` pre-fill the supplier. Supplier charges: `supplier_charges` table + `src/lib/supplier-charges.ts` (`attachChargesToPo` in `createMainLpo`, `moveChargesToSupplierPo` in `splitMainLpo`, `releaseCharges` on refusal, both add to `total_cost_aed`); shown on `SupplierLpoCard` (`charges`), the LPO page, the LPO PDF rows, the Parts page (`SupplierChargesStrip`, `PartsTable extraCosts` in Our cost), the quotation's internal box (`EditorSettings.supplierCharges`, `quoteTotals(..., { supplierCharges })`), `profit.ts` (jobProfits otherCost, approved-quote profits) and the low-profit check; undo removes them. A part number of 1–3 digits is cleared on import when the supplier's paper has none. The fixture `tests/fixtures/supplier-autovivo-q2206129.pdf` is rebuilt in the supplier's layout by `make-supplier-autovivo.mjs` from `...expected.json` (the owner sent pictures only; the script never overwrites an original). J-00016 was re-imported through the screen as Omar (62 parts, courier 7,500, total 255,394). Lessons: a test car cannot take a real car's VIN (unique), so the VIN warning is ticked in tests; test-f's section C was brought up to the Net each / Line total columns on 22 October (9 columns on two lines, 11 on one; Supplier 14rem, part number 17.5rem, the two-line Availability 9.5rem and Part minimum 10rem so 1366 fits; a new check that the table never scrolls sideways; screenshots `tests/browser/shots/test-f-parts-<width>.png`).
