Security upgrade of 23 October 2026 (commits 4e4928e and 915721d, live as deployment dpl_DK5RWH8KGrviNWqSrobEjLRo2n1e; the one before it, dpl_B2vUznUJLMy17kFG5m3XNzN2YcuF on 16.4.0, is the Instant Rollback target; git tag `pre-next-16.4.2`; database backup backups/2026-10-23T03-41-17): Next.js 16.4.0 to 16.4.2 (GHSA-2464-fhr4-w9xw and GHSA-673q-mjm6-r5xr critical remote code execution, GHSA-p3m5-59mp-jv5r server-action denial of service; fixed from 16.4.1) with eslint-config-next 16.4.2, and SheetJS `xlsx` 0.18.5 to 0.20.3 (CVE-2023-30533, CVE-2024-22363) vendored as `vendor/xlsx-0.20.3.tgz` from cdn.sheetjs.com (npm has no fixed release; package.json reads `file:vendor/...`). Left by choice: the braces chain under @next/eslint-plugin-next (high, no fixed release exists, lint tooling only) and uuid under exceljs (moderate, only v4 is used). The live site reports its version in the browser as `window.next.version`. `tests/browser/live-probe.mjs` checks the live site without signing in (test documents made through a local server, removed after). test-w now skips every step that would touch a real connected mailbox: on 23 October a full run with the dummy Google secret disconnected scans@ofjauto.com (put back within two minutes; nothing reached Google). Cron fix (same day): Vercel calls the scheduled jobs on the deployment's own vercel.app address and does not follow redirects, and since 7 October the proxy sent every *.vercel.app request a 308 to erp.ofjauto.com, so neither /api/cron/mailbox nor /api/cron/housekeeping ever ran on schedule (the once-a-day mailbox checks were manual presses); the proxy now answers /api/cron/* first, on any host, and both routes keep their CRON_SECRET check (housekeeping got maxDuration 300 for its first catch-up run). Proof is scans_mailbox_last_check moving every five minutes by itself. Lessons: TaskStop ends the shell but NOT the node processes under it on Windows (a stopped dev server keeps its port and, with its log pipe gone, starts answering 500), so list node.exe processes after stopping; a fresh dev server times out first page loads, so compare test results only between warmed servers; never push a docs-only commit right after a risky deploy, because on the Hobby plan Instant Rollback only reaches the immediately previous deployment.

Batch of 23 October 2026, afternoon (staff testing notes; migrations `20261118090000` gate-in no money, `..100000` video posters, `..110000` background jobs; tests `test:gatein1|videos2|nofee3|jobs4`). 1. Gate-in: one search box finds a car by plate or VIN and a customer by name or mobile (`src/lib/gate-in-search.ts`: `gateInSearch`, `customersByPhone`, `gateInCustomer`, `phoneKey` = the last nine digits, admin client with a narrow select so the gate-in role gets name, mobile and that customer's cars only); three buttons Existing car / Existing customer, new car / New customer and car (`?mode=car|customer`); "+ New car for this customer" opens `/gate-in/new-car?customer=<id>` (`QuickCarForm customer`, hidden `customer_id`); a new customer's mobile already on record asks "This number belongs to <NAME> - add the car to <NAME> instead?" on blur (`checkCustomerPhone`) and again on the server (`createCustomerAndVehicle` returns `dup_customer_*` values; the form picks them up with the prev-value setState pattern); `phone_different=1` for a different person; the car's gate-in page sends the gate-in role no earlier visits, declined work or estimate amounts and no customer link; `declined_work_read` excludes gate_in. `ModelYearSelect` autofocuses the "Older or other" box only when the person picks it (an older car's VIN used to pull the cursor out of the VIN box). 2. Inspection videos reach the customer: `src/lib/report-videos.ts` (`reportVideos`, `videoLabel` "<item> - Bad", `reportVideosByToken`), public `/api/customer/videos?t=<report token>` (fresh addresses), `CustomerVideoList` (anchors `#v-<id>`) with the ONE player `VideoPlayer` exported from `CustomerVideos.tsx` (optional `poster`), a Videos section on `/report/[token]`; `inspection_media.poster_path` = a frame taken on the device at upload (`videoPoster` in `InspectionMedia`, uploaded through prepare and registered as `posterPath`); `VideoCards` in the PDF template (picture or dark card, a drawn play sign, "Watch video" linking to `/report/<token>#v-<id>`), placed with each item in the report PDF and the rest in a Videos block; `InspectionMedia` carries `data-media-where` (item key) for tests. Other customer links carry no videos (quote none; progress, handover, warranty photos only). 3. The inspection fee line is off the report PDF (and so the report link); it stays on the approval page and the internal gate-in PDF. 4. Background jobs: table `background_jobs` (server only, in the security test's SERVER_ONLY list), `src/lib/background-jobs.ts` (`recordJobRun`, `checkLateJobs`, `jobLate`, `lateWords`, `jobTime`), both cron routes record every run (housekeeping work in `runHousekeeping()`, `housekeepingWords` gives every count singular and plural words), lateness (mailbox > 15 min, hourly > 2 h) checked by each job and by `/api/notifications` through `after()` at most once a minute per server, one bell per episode (`late_notified_at`, notification `background_job_late`), Settings "Background jobs" box (owner). Lessons: Supabase `signOut()` defaults to the GLOBAL scope, so a test that presses the Sign out form (the first `<form>` on every app page is the Sign out form in the menu) signs that real person out on every device: submit a specific form through one of its fields (`el.form.requestSubmit()`); the technician form shows an item's photo and video buttons only after the item has an answer; a test that nulls a stored file path must remember the file to delete it from storage; MediaRecorder in headless Edge makes test videos (webm) without any fixture file; TaskStop leaves node processes running on Windows, so stop the server on 3101 by its process (scratchpad stop-3101.ps1 pattern) after each section.

Deposit proof fix of 26 October 2026 (migration `20261119090000_app_errors_and_receipt_numbers.sql`, test `npm run test:paymethods` = a deposit and a proforma payment by each of the five methods as Mathew from Waiting on you, 55 checks). `job_files.ref_id` is a uuid column, but the deposit panel tied its proof uploads to "pay:<key>" and the receiving panel to "recv:<key>" (with a "recvp-…" key), so every proof upload failed with "invalid input syntax for type uuid" (Mathew's RCT-00036 on Dubai E 55138 saved without its optional proof). Keys are now real uuids from `src/lib/uuid.ts` (`newUuid`, `isUuid`), files are tied to the key itself, and `/api/files/register` refuses a non-uuid reference in plain words. Staff never see database words: `src/lib/app-errors.ts` (`SAVE_FAILED` "Couldn't save – nothing was recorded. Try again or tell the owner.", `logAppError` writes the server-only `app_errors` table and the server log); `recordPaymentCore` logs insert failures and never reports a written receipt as failed (follow-ups are logged), `recordDepositPanel` likewise. Receipt numbers come from the `payments_receipt_number` trigger (the next after the highest RCT on record, under a transaction lock; the column default is gone), so a failed or refused save never uses a number and Clear test data restarts it by itself (`receipt_number_seq` is no longer used). A cheque still clearing no longer leaves "Deposit to record" or "Awaiting payment" on Waiting on you (it would be recorded twice): accounts get "Cheque <no> (RCT-…) to clear" with Cleared on the row (`inline.kind = "cheque_clear"`, `setChequeStatus` honours `return_to=/dashboard`), and a cleared deposit cheque tells Parts the LPO can go (`tellDepositCovered` in `payments.ts`). Lesson: a test must attach a proof for the methods that allow one, or a broken upload path stays invisible.

Batch of 26 October 2026 (receipt + Zoho phase 0; migrations `20261120090000_receipt_footer_note.sql`, `20261120100000_zoho_phase0.sql`; tests `test:receipt|zoho0`; plan `docs/zoho-plan.md`). A1: the receipt rebuilt to the owner's mock in `renderReceiptPdf` (`src/lib/pdf/invoice-pdf.tsx`, `ReceiptData` built by the route): the shared header, three `InfoBoxes` (received from with `formatPhone`, vehicle with plate or NO PLATE and VIN, paid by with method/reference/cheque/received), one FOR line, ONE framed Amount received box (`Amount` large + `amountInWords`), the three-cell summary (total · paid to date · balance remaining, or `BALANCE` + a drawn `CheckMark` + "Paid in full" — the font has NO ✓ glyph), a red VOIDED band, the footer note from the new setting `receipt_footer_note` (seeded; in `TEXT_KEYS` and the Settings form). Paid to date = every recorded payment on the job (deposits included); a pending cheque counts with its note and blocks Paid in full. The old qty/rate/VAT-0 table, TotalsBlock, "Received with thanks" and the words block are gone; `LinesTable`/`DocLine` imports left with them. One template for deposits and payments; `/r/` links unchanged. Lessons: test regexes need word boundaries ("Emirates" contains "rate") and must not match the footer note ("paid in full" is in it — assert `BALANCE Paid in full`); test-jobs4 asserts the red late line only while the test's stale time still stands (the LIVE five-minute cron writes the same shared row mid-test). B: Zoho Books phase 0 in `src/lib/zoho.ts` (mirrors google-mailbox: sealed refresh token in server-only `zoho_tokens`, consent `ZohoBooks.fullaccess.all` + offline + consent, state cookie `erp_zoho_state`, routes `/api/zoho/connect|callback`, page `/settings/zoho`); `zohoFetch` is the ONE door (sequential, 650 ms gap, one 429 retry, Zoho `code !== 0` thrown) and refuses any write to REAL org 942979945 while `settings.zoho_live_enabled` is off (`zoho_org_test` picked on the page, never the real id); reads `listOrganizations/Taxes/Accounts/BankAccounts`, `listPaymentModes` best-effort (the API may not expose modes); `mirrorRealIntoTest` creates missing taxes/accounts in TEST only. Env var NAMES: `ZOHO_CLIENT_ID`, `ZOHO_CLIENT_SECRET`; redirect URI `https://erp.ofjauto.com/api/zoho/callback`; Elite limits 10,000/day, 100/min, 10 concurrent. Connected 26 October 2026: OFJ TEST = 944122733 (settings.zoho_org_test); the mirror reports "already there: 58" (2 taxes, 56 accounts on both sides); the accountant's OFJ income accounts (Sales - Labour etc) are NOT created yet - the REAL chart is still Zoho's stock chart. The /api/zoho/inspect support route was removed the same day at the owner's order: no route may touch Zoho with just a shared secret - Zoho reads live only on the owner's /settings/zoho page (the one-off proof came through it before removal). LESSONS: Next's redirect() throws NEXT_REDIRECT on purpose - a try/catch around a redirecting call (redirect, a back() helper, requirePermission, notFound) swallows it and staff see "NEXT_REDIRECT"; build the words inside the try, redirect after it, unstable_rethrow(e) first in the catch (scan scratchpad scan-redirect-try.mjs found the one offender). A dev machine must NEVER load a page that refreshes a live-connected token with test env secrets (it writes last_error on the REAL row - same lesson as the scans mailbox): test-zoho0 skips /settings/zoho while a real connection exists.
Zoho phase 1 of 26 October 2026, evening (migration `20261121090000_zoho_phase1_outbox.sql`, note `docs/zoho-phase1-note.md`, plan updated in `docs/zoho-plan.md` §3/§6; tests `npm run test:zoho1` local 27 checks and `npm run test:zoho1live` 22 checks against OFJ TEST): customers, deposits, tax invoices and payments post one-way to OFJ TEST through `zoho_outbox` (server-only, unique (kind, record_id), zoho_result stores Zoho's figures next to the ERP's). Writers only ENQUEUE (`tryEnqueueZoho` in `src/lib/zoho-sync.ts`; hooks: recordPaymentCore post-insert (never a pending cheque), decideOverpayment approve, setChequeStatus cleared, convertProformaIfPaid, createChargeInvoice; a void/bounce/credit note AFTER a sync calls `flagZohoAttention` = red row "by hand"); the WORKER (`drainZohoOutbox`) runs only where the Zoho secret lives — the five-minute `/api/cron/zoho` (counts only, CRON_SECRET, in vercel.json) and the login-gated Sync now — so tests seed+enqueue locally on the shared DB, call the LIVE cron with CRON_SECRET and assert from zoho_outbox.zoho_result. Order contact→deposit→invoice→payment, per-row claim by attempts, lazy account resolve. Mapping = `settings.zoho_account_map` (NAMES: income by line type via quotation_lines.line_type/side + invoice section, deposit-to by method, bankCharges, advances), screen `/settings/zoho/mapping`, permission `zohoSync` (owner+accounts, roles.ts only — the tables are server-only so no SQL policy); missing name → FAILED "Mapping missing: <label>"; names absent from the chart are auto-created in OFJ TEST only. Idempotency ladder: stored id → GET by invoice_number (the LIST row has no tax_total — re-read the invoice in FULL before judging figures) / reference_number RCT scoped by customer_id (a number reused after Clear test data must never adopt someone else's document — figures mismatch = FAILED, never overwrite) → create (`ignore_auto_number_generation=true` keeps INV- numbers exactly). Totals to the fils: lines carry qty/rate/flat line discount/5% tax_id/`account_id` (accepted on ad-hoc lines though undocumented), entity-level discount before tax = discount_aed + warranty credit, the derived round-off is a taxed NEGATIVE "Round-off" LINE (Zoho's `adjustment` is after tax — wrong for VAT), one corrective pass then FAILED with both figures. Deposits are unapplied advances (empty invoices array IS accepted), applied at invoice sync via POST /invoices/{id}/credits — but only a payment's UNUSED amount (GET the payment first; Zoho refuses "exceeds the total payment"). Sync page `/zoho` (nav for owner+accounts): filter chips, green matching figures, red reason + Retry, open-in-Zoho links, owner's Backfill; failed rows join accounts' Waiting on you (amber, accounts-waiting.ts §6). background_jobs row 'zoho'; zoho_outbox in the security test's SERVER_ONLY. Backfill of the existing data ran 26 Oct: 15 posted, 0 failed. Lessons: the live-test invoices use run-unique TINV- numbers (nextDocumentNumber scans 'INV-%' only) and explicit TRCT- receipt numbers, because e2e cleanup leaves Zoho TEST populated and reused numbers adopt stale documents; a deposit detached from its job (job_id null) escapes the cleanup SQL's payments delete — it now also deletes by customer_id.
Zoho phase 1 adversarial review, same evening (migration `20261121120000_zoho_outbox_claim.sql`, commit f987202): a four-lens review workflow (money / idempotency / security / regressions, verifier per finding) confirmed 20 defects, all fixed. The rules that came out of it: zohoFetch retries a 5xx on READS ONLY (a 5xx can land after Zoho committed the write — a re-sent POST double-posts; the outbox retry is the safe recovery because every handler searches Zoho before creating); outbox rows are claimed in flight (`zoho_outbox.claimed_at`, taken when null or older than 10 minutes, cleared on every outcome — the attempts compare-and-swap alone cannot stop a drain that STARTS mid-handler); a labour line's 1-decimal quantity times its rate may not equal `amount_aed` (0.33 h stores qty 0.3), so a line whose qty × rate cannot land the exact amount (or would show a discount the ERP does not have) posts as qty 1 × amount with the hours in the description, and the corrective Round-off pass is capped at 1 AED (beyond that = FAILED, Round-off must stay rounding); contacts adopt only on a NAME match (phone_contains alone would merge two customers who share a mobile — gate-in allows that) and an "exists" create-refusal re-searches before making the "· C-xxxxx" suffixed contact; adopt-by-number also requires the Zoho invoice's customer_id to be OUR contact; the invoice balance check uses each payment's UNUSED amount and fails with the receipt's own words when credit ran short; a void landing mid-post is re-checked after create (red, never green); enqueueZoho/flagZohoAttention check every supabase result (supabase returns errors, it never throws — a swallowed insert error silently loses the enqueue); the backfill paginates with .range() past PostgREST's 1,000-row cap and upserts with ignoreDuplicates; a transient chart-of-accounts error leaves money rows PENDING with the reason (only mapping/connection problems fail rows); /api/e2e/zoho's write_to_real probe refuses with 409 once zoho_live_enabled is on (it would otherwise be a real write and smear last_error on the live token row from a dev machine); the sync page hides Sync now/Backfill/Retry under viewingAs; waiting rows read "<kind>: <reason>" because flagged rows DID post and need removal by hand, not re-posting. The cap then CAUGHT a live bug the unbounded pass had been hiding: Zoho honours ONE discount level - with discount_type entity_level the per-line discount field is IGNORED - so lines post at pre-discount figures and every discount (per line, quotation-wide, warranty) folds into the one entity discount, matching the ERP documents' own summed green Discount row (commit dd49cb8; test:zoho1live 22/22 with no corrective passes).
Zoho phase 2 of 27 October 2026 (migration `20261122090000_zoho_phase2.sql`, note `docs/zoho-phase2-note.md`; tests `npm run test:zoho2` local 19 checks through the real screens and `npm run test:zoho2live` 24 live checks, all green; backfill ran): credit notes, vendors, supplier bills, vendor payments and expenses join the same outbox (kinds creditnote/vendor/bill/billpayment/commission/recovery, KIND_ORDER contact→vendor→deposit→invoice→creditnote→payment→bill→billpayment→commission→recovery; id columns suppliers.zoho_vendor_id, purchase_orders.zoho_bill_id/zoho_billpayment_id, invoices.zoho_creditnote_id, commission_payments.zoho_expense_id, recovery_payouts.zoho_expense_id). zohoFetch gained the ONE multipart branch (`opts.upload` = {field, filename, contentType, data Buffer} → FormData, no content-type header, guard/queue/retry unchanged). Hooks: issueCreditNote enqueues creditnote+parent invoice (the phase 1 by-hand flag is gone), applySupplierInvoice + receiveTicked's PO loop enqueue bill, markSupplierPaid enqueues billpayment, payCommissions enqueues commission, markMonthPaid takes the upsert's returned id and enqueues recovery, the commissions proof route calls `reopenZoho` (synced→pending; the handler attaches what is missing and nothing else). The Zoho facts that cost a live round each: a UAE credit note REQUIRES the associated invoice (create with `?invoice_id=` — which also applies it) AND a matching `place_of_supply` (DU like our invoices); "reason_for_credit_debit_note" is compulsory ("others"); a bill line with the 5% tax on a vat_not_registered vendor is refused ("VAT or Exemption cannot be applied for this VAT Treatment") — so `vendorVatRegistered` demands a 15-digit supplier TRN in plain words (Parts > Suppliers, then Retry) and PUTs the vendor's tax_treatment+tax_reg_no idempotently so a late TRN just works; an "Our stock" LPO never bills (internal); bill attachments allow pdf/png/jpg/gif/bmp only (field `attachment`; expense receipts also xls/xlsx, field `receipt`) and re-adds are guarded by the bill's documents list / expense_receipt_name; job_files' column is `storage_path`, not path. Bills: one per supplier LPO with its invoice entered (bill_number = the SUPPLIER'S invoice number — unique per Zoho org, so a second LPO sharing it adopts on matching figures or fails in plain words), parts as ONE summary line at the invoice's net figures (invoice_unit_cost ?? unit_cost), each supplier_charge its own line on the courier account, price flags + "entered total wins" note in the notes, reference_number = the LPO number (also the vendor payment's idempotency key; payments settle the bill's Zoho balance in full from the mapped paidFrom and re-check 0 due). Expenses: no tax, reference = CPV number / "RECOV <month> <who>", adopt-by-reference refuses a different amount; the recovery receipt is built fresh with buildXlsx. Mapping gained purchases{parts,courier} + expense{commission,recovery} + paidFrom (+ reference-only advancesRefund); resolveZohoAccounts auto-creates them in TEST as account_type "expense" (works). The phase 1 invoice balance check now subtracts zohoCreditedTotal (credits applied in Zoho are invisible to the ERP's own balance). e2e cleanup's zoho_outbox lines also match record_ids of e2e suppliers/POs/commission_payments/recovery_payouts.
Zoho phase 2 adversarial review, same night (migration `20261122120000_zoho_id_protect.sql`, commit b0b92e8; test:zoho2 19/19 and test:zoho2live 24/24 re-run green): a four-lens workflow confirmed 22 defects, all fixed. The rules that came out of it: ONE supplier invoice belongs to ONE supplier — receiveTicked refuses a typed invoice across two suppliers in plain words (the amount smeared whole onto every vendor's bill, and the 5% cross-check is skipped whenever poIds.length > 1), and the received-branch update carries `.neq(supplier_invoice_status,'received')` so the panel never overwrites an entered invoice (the LPO page's own form corrects one, and a change AFTER the bill posted calls flagZohoAttention instead of re-posting); a bill counts what was DELIVERED (billed qty = received_qty ?? (fully received ? quantity : 0)); checkSupplierInvoice catches the exactly-+5% slip (the WITH-VAT total typed into the before-VAT box) with its own needsAmountConfirm wording; syncCreditNoteRow refuses to over-credit the parent (zohoCreditedTotal counted BEFORE create — hand-entered phase 1 credits carry Zoho's own numbers and are invisible to the number search); syncBillPaymentRow stores zoho_billpayment_id only AFTER the balance-0 check (an adopted foreign/partial payment must not lock creation out) and skips Our-stock LPOs like the bill handler (and the backfill excludes them); dependency waits go through `ensureQueued` (insert-only — enqueueZoho's failed→pending flip would reopen a FAILED parent every drain and wipe its reason to a blank "Waiting"); the phase 2 mapping groups resolve SOFTLY (purchasesError/expenseError/paidFromError on Resolved + needAccounts at use) so a missing bills/expenses mapping never fails phase 1 money rows; reopenZoho clears claimed_at and the drain's outcome writes carry the claim stamp (.eq claimed_at) so a proof uploaded mid-flight re-runs instead of being marked synced over; lookFor uses contact_name_CONTAINS in both ensureContact and ensureVendor (the exact-match filter could never find a "· Supplier"/"· C-xxxxx" suffixed contact again); a scan that exists but cannot be read FAILS with "press Retry" (and /api/files/register reopens the bill when a supplier_invoice scan lands late; an unsupported format says so in the result); markMonthPaid SUMS a re-paid month into the payout and reopens the expense row (the totals-differ refusal then names both figures) and surfaces the upsert error; the zoho_* id columns are protected by the `erp_protect_zoho_ids` trigger (refused for current_user = 'authenticated'; the sync writes as service_role); backfillZoho's message names the phase 2 counts; the live test restores the deliberately broken mapping in a finally. Lesson re-learned the hard way: check for a RUNNING background browser suite before killing the dev server's node processes (the layout check died mid-run on ECONNREFUSED and had to run again).

Zoho phase 3/4, 27 October 2026 (migration `20261123090000_zoho_phase34.sql`, note `docs/zoho-phase34-note.md`, runbook `docs/zoho-golive-runbook.md`, tests `npm run test:zoho3` 22 local / `test:zoho3live` 15 against OFJ TEST): suppliers carry `vat_registered` (default true; false = no TRN needed, vendor PUT to vat_not_registered, bills and vendor credits post flat with header tax_treatment and no tax_id); a part marked returned queues `vendorcredit` (number AND reference `RET-<id12>` — OFJ TEST has no auto-numbering for vendor credits, so the create names the number with `ignore_auto_number_generation`; applied to the bill via POST /vendorcredits/{id}/bills guarded by bills_credited; part_items.zoho_vendorcredit_id; scans stay in the ERP — Zoho vendor credits take no attachment endpoint); `recordCnRefund` on an issued credit note (accounts, once-only, cash/transfer, invoices.refund_* columns) queues `cnrefund` (waits for the CN in Zoho, refuses when its unapplied balance is short, POST /creditnotes/{id}/refunds from the mapped advancesRefund account, adopt by reference `RF-<CN number>`, invoices.zoho_refund_id, the outbox row's Open link is the CN). Daily reconciliation in `src/lib/zoho-reconcile.ts` (`compareDay` per Dubai day: invoices/CNs by issued_at, payments non-cheque by received_at + cheques by cleared_at with the per-mode map, bills by supplier_invoice_date at billErpFigures gross with Our-stock excluded, vendor payments by supplier_paid_at, expenses = commissions paid_on + recovery paid_at; the Zoho side lists with date= & per_page=200 paging and drops void/draft), `zoho_reconciliation` table (day PK, server-only), `reconcileYesterdayIfDue` on the live cron's first run after Dubai midnight (own catch), `reconcileMissingDays` cap 40, red transition = ONE bell (type `zoho_reconciliation`), page `/zoho/reconciliation` (re-check per day), cron `?reconcile=YYYY-MM-DD` branch; `/zoho/close` + `src/lib/zoho-close.ts` sums the STORED recon rows (no Zoho calls) with the Excel export `/api/export/zoho-close`. Go-live: `src/lib/zoho-golive.ts` six gates (accounts-only review tick via `tickGoLiveReview` — the owner cannot; mapping complete; mapped names read LIVE from the REAL chart + 5% tax; failed = 0; last 7 recon days green; start date), panel on Settings > Zoho Books, `flipGoLive` (owner only, never actingAs, type REAL, fresh all-green re-check server-side, `zoho_golive_log` + audit row + bell), `flipGoLiveBack` (reason ≥ 5 chars, logged); the zohoFetch write guard is now two-way (REAL refused unless live, OFJ TEST refused once live) and `beforeGoLiveStart`/`startDateSkip` gates EVERY handler by the document's own date (invoice issued_at, payment received/cleared, CN issued, bill supplier_invoice_date, billpayment supplier_paid_at, commission paid_on, recovery paid_at, vendorcredit disposition_at, cnrefund refund_paid_at) — pre-start documents return a skipped result, never a REAL post. Backfilled 23–27 Oct: 24 red is the real TRN-waiting bill; 26–27 red are the live suites' own documents sitting in Zoho TEST after cleanup — true differences, named exactly. LESSONS: part_items has NO `name` column (description) — the vendorcredit handler selected it and every credit died as "part no longer exists" until the first test run; a live-test day's recon baseline is red by itself, so live reconciliation proofs compare COUNTS against the baseline, and the suite's cleanup re-reconciles the day instead of deleting the row (red → red = no new morning bell); ChoiceButtons render `<label>`s, so tests click `label`, never `button`.
Zoho phase 3/4 adversarial review, same day (migration `20261123120000_zoho_org_switch.sql`, commit e8b47e7; test:zoho3 39/39, test:zoho3live 24/24): 19 confirmed findings, 14 distinct, all fixed. THE GO-LIVE SWITCH IS ONE SQL TRANSACTION: `public.zoho_switch_org(p_from, p_to, p_live, p_by)` (service_role only — test:security checks it) locks zoho_outbox, refuses while a row was claimed in the last 10 minutes, archives the queue under its org in server-only `zoho_outbox_archive` (PK org,kind,record_id), restores the target org's archived rows, clears EVERY ERP zoho_* id column (they are org-less: OFJ TEST ids sent to REAL would fail forever) and restores the target's ids from its synced rows (cnrefund from zoho_result.zoho_refund_id), then sets zoho_live_enabled; flipGoLive/flipGoLiveBack call it via admin.rpc and then enqueueBackfill (when live = the replay: only documents dated >= the start date, no bulk contacts/vendors). `zohoLiveNow()` in zoho.ts reads the switch fresh (getSettings is React cache() per request) for the write guard, the drain's start and a per-row check (a run that started before a flip releases its claim and stops). Cutover rules in zoho-sync.ts: `startDateSkip(date, kinds, recordId)` skips a pre-start doc only when the TEST archive holds it synced, else FAILS with DONE_BY_HAND words; `parentBeforeStart` fails a credit note/CN refund/bill payment whose parent predates go-live, posts a payment on a pre-start invoice unapplied with a note, and lets a vendor credit on a pre-start bill post unapplied; otherwise a vendor credit WAITS for its bill (ensureQueued) so it is always applied. Gate 6 = `startDateGate` (start date set AND <= today Dubai; setGoLiveStartDate is owner-only and refused once live); gate 5 = `reconGate` (each of the 7 calendar days before today has a row checked after its day ended, all green). Reconciliation: `reconOrgFor(day, live, start, testOrg)` (REAL only for days >= start once live), `reconcileDay(day)` picks the org itself and stores details.org/orgLabel, `reconDue(row, day, now, saturated)` (never while the drain filled its batch, never before 00:30 the next morning, re-check a row written before its day ended, red yesterday hourly), `firstDocumentDay` → backfill from the 1st of that month, billpayment amounts from the outbox (current org) or the archive, skipped billpayments not counted, bills allow 0.01 per bill line (Zoho's per-line VAT rounding). Vendor credits ONLY for `disposition = 'returned'` (return_status 'returned' alone = "back with Parts"; returnPart's returned stage now sets disposition/_at/_by/_note); an adopted vendor payment reads its own amount (GET /vendorpayments/{id}); syncBillRow refuses when the bill's VAT presence no longer matches the supplier's switch; changing a supplier's VAT switch flags their posted bills (flagBillsAfterVatChange). `markZohoDoneByHand` + the "Done by hand in Zoho…" fold on red rows (zoho_result.manual with by/name/at/note/reason). zoho-close pages every read with the exported `pageAll` (payments grouped in memory) and `validMonth` guards the page and export. recordCnRefund's update returns rows (a lost race says "already recorded"). Lessons: Mathew's real go-live review tick (27 Oct 09:16) sits in shared settings — tests snapshot real state and restore exactly, never reset it; a DB-level move on the shared database is tested inside BEGIN … ROLLBACK through pg (the switch simulation: TEST → a made-up org → TEST, never REAL); an outbox row created DURING a drain gets its first look on the next run; a red day re-checked while red rings no new bell, so a bell test marks the day green first and deletes only its own bells (title + since the run started).
Second Zoho phase 3/4 review round, same afternoon (migration `20261123150000_zoho_golive_once.sql`): 14 more confirmed findings, all go-live cutover. THE SWITCH RUNS ONCE: `zoho_switch_org(p_from, p_to, p_live, p_by, p_start)` refuses under its lock when `settings.zoho_went_live_at` is set or the books are already live, and on going live also deletes zoho_reconciliation rows from p_start (they were OFJ TEST comparisons), sets zoho_went_live_at and `zoho_golive_followup.pending`; "Back to OFJ TEST" is gone — `flipGoLiveBack` now STOPS posting (live false, wentLive true: drain skipped, zohoWriteOrg null, OFJ TEST refused for good by the guard) and `resumeGoLive` (owner, typed REAL) turns live on again; `finishGoLiveFollowup` re-runs the replay (`runGoLiveFollowup` clears the pending flag; red banners on the panel and /zoho while pending). `zohoBooksNow()` = {live, wentLive} fresh; `wentLiveOf(settings)`; `cutover()`/`reconOrgFor`/zoho-close treat "went live" (posting or stopped) as the cutover. HandlerOut gained `attention`: the document posted (zoho_id + result kept) but the row goes FAILED with by-hand words — used by an invoice whose payments predate go-live, a payment on a pre-go-live or hand-closed invoice, a vendor credit on a pre-go-live or hand-closed bill. `parentClosedByHand(kind, id)` makes children of a "Done by hand" parent fail with words instead of waiting forever; `reopenZoho` turns a manual row red instead of re-posting; `zohoPostedSomewhere(kinds, id)` → "current" | "opening" (archived synced in OFJ TEST after go-live) drives voidPayment, the cheque bounce and applySupplierInvoice (date changes count as changes; an opening-balance bill is never re-queued). enqueueBackfill once went live: `consider()` per kind, and every READY pre-start document not synced in the TEST archive becomes a failed by-hand row (`byHand` count). `purchase_orders.zoho_bill_taxed` snapshots the VAT standing at post time (billErpFigures and syncBillRow's VAT check read it; flagBillsAfterVatChange flags only bills posted under the other standing). The drain orders by updated_at (waited rows rotate to the back) and the cron counts a batch as busy only when it posted or failed something. Tests: the switch simulation in BEGIN … ROLLBACK also proves the once-only refusal (zoho_went_live_at set inside the transaction — never the live flag).
Third Zoho phase 3/4 review check, same evening (migration `20261123170000_zoho_switch_bill_taxed.sql`, commit 36f5da9 + the accounts cache after it; test:zoho3 47/47): `zohoPostedSomewhere(kinds, id, docStamp, isDay)` returns "opening" only for documents dated BEFORE the start date that have a Zoho id in the OFJ TEST archive (a post-start document awaiting its replay returns null and is re-queued); `flagZohoAttention(..., { handOnly })` stores zoho_result.hand_only — retryZohoRow refuses those rows, enqueueZoho and reopenZoho leave them red; markZohoDoneByHand on an invoice with no ERP Zoho id flags its synced payments/deposits (`flagPaymentsOfHandInvoice`); the switch nulls zoho_bill_taxed for bills dated >= p_start (whenever p_start is given); reached-OFJ-TEST = synced OR a zoho_id (a reopened row at the flip becomes a hand_only "change was waiting" row, never "in no book"); setZohoTestOrg refuses once went live and the org buttons hide. LESSON: OFJ TEST allows 1,000 Zoho API calls a DAY ("exceeded the maximum call rate limit of 1,000") — several live suites plus a 27-day reconciliation backfill in one day exhausted it (rows then wait with the reason; nothing breaks). Space live runs out, never backfill many days on a test day, and `resolveZohoAccounts` now caches per org+mapping for 30 minutes (`forgetZohoAccounts` on account/tax errors) because each five-minute run with a waiting row cost two calls.
Owner's answers of 27 October 2026 (migration `20261124090000_supplier_paid_method.sql`, test:zoho3 51/51): Stop/Resume approved. The go-live start date must be the 1st of a month (startDateGate and setGoLiveStartDate; the owner and the accountant pick it, likely 1 December, NOT 1 November). New go-live line `invoice_prefix`: Zoho's own invoice prefix (GET /settings/invoices on REAL, invoice_settings.prefix_string) must not start with INV- (seven lines now). The live Zoho suites stamp `settings.zoho_last_live_test_at`; the panel warns (data-golive-live-test-warning) when one ran in the last 7 days — no live tests in the week before go-live. Supplier payments: markSupplierPaid takes `method` (SUPPLIER_PAY_METHODS transfer default / card / cash / cheque), the optional reference and a `proof` file (job-files `supplier-payments/<poId>/…`; later via `SupplierProofUpload` → `/api/parts/supplier-proof`, which reopens the billpayment); purchase_orders.supplier_paid_method/_proof_path/_proof_at/_by; the mapping has `paidFromBy` per method (empty = paidFrom, resolved softly per method); syncBillPaymentRow posts payment_mode by method from that account and attaches the proof (to the vendor payment, else to the bill; the result's `proof` says which or why not).

Card and payment-link settlements, 27 October 2026 afternoon (migrations `20261125090000_card_settlements.sql`, `..093000` revoke; tests `npm run test:settle` 18 checks, `npm run test:settlelive` = the Zoho posting, run only when OFJ TEST's daily allowance has room): card/link payments go to Zoho at the FULL amount into their clearing account with NO bank charge (other methods unchanged); `card_settlements` (STL-xxxxx, server-only, in the security SERVER_ONLY list) + `payments.settlement_id/actual_fee_aed`; pure rules in `src/lib/card-settlement-rules.ts` (`feeOf` actual share once settled else the recorded estimate, `splitFee` proportional with the remainder on the largest, `checkSettlement`, `preTicked` = received before the settlement day, 6% second-look, 3 working days), server in `src/lib/card-settlements.ts` (`loadUnsettled`, `recordSettlement` all-or-nothing with the settlement_id guard, `clearingOpenAt`); screen `/card-settlements` (permission `cardSettlements` owner+accounts, ONE `SettlementForm`, "Upload settlement report – coming soon"); outbox kind `settlement` → `syncSettlementRow`: `/banktransactions` transfer_fund clearing→`depositTo.transfer` for what arrived + `createExpense` on the mapped bankCharges account paid through the clearing, reference "STL-x · statement ref", ids in `card_settlements.zoho_transfer_id/zoho_fee_id`; profit (`JobProfit.bankChargesActual`, "card fee estimated until the bank settles", the /profit "actual/est." mark) and so the bonus use the actual fee; accounts' Waiting on you gets one amber row per method past 3 working days; the daily reconciliation counts settlement fees as Expenses and shows the clearing balance at day end; the monthly close has the "Card and payment-link clearing settled" line. Lesson: a new table still receives the platform's default grants for authenticated, so a server-only table needs an explicit `revoke all ... from anon, authenticated`.

Long descriptions, 27 October 2026 afternoon (test `npm run test:longdesc`, 12 checks): the quote save route cut a line's title at 200 characters and its note at 1,000 SILENTLY (three lines on Q-00001 v1 of J-00016, all "Lathe charges - CYLINDER BLOCK BORING / ...", were stored cut; no full copy existed anywhere, so they need retyping). Now ONE limit in `src/lib/text-limits.ts` (`LINE_TEXT_MAX` 3,000, `tooLongWords`, `textCounter`) and ONE component `src/components/TextCounter.tsx` under the editor's name box and both note boxes; above the limit the editor keeps the text but saves nothing (its `patch` drops any waiting save of that field) and "Quotation complete" refuses; the save route and `createChargeInvoice` refuse instead of slicing. PDF section rows keep `wrap={false}` only below 600 characters, so a long row continues on the next page instead of being clipped; the editor's Note badge shows the whole note. Labour hours in a quantity column come from `hoursQty` in `src/lib/format.ts` (nothing at all for 0 hours, never "0.0 h"), used by the PDF template's `qtyText` and both invoice pages; `toInvoiceLine` reads the hours from the END of the details (after positions) and treats a services line with quantity 0 as labour, and `detailsWithoutHours` keeps the positions and notes.

New versions without dead clicks, 27 October 2026 afternoon (test `npm run test:version`, 8 checks; the dev server must be started with `APP_VERSION_OVERRIDE=e2e-v1`, the test writes and removes `public/version.json`): `next.config.ts` computes the version (`APP_VERSION_OVERRIDE` → `VERCEL_DEPLOYMENT_ID` → `VERCEL_GIT_COMMIT_SHA` → "local"/"dev"), bakes it into the pages as `NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_VERSION` and, in production builds, writes the static `public/version.json` (gitignored; the proxy matcher skips `version.json`, so a check costs no function call). `src/lib/app-version.ts` holds `isStaleVersionError` (missing chunks, failed dynamic imports, Next's UnrecognizedActionError "Server Action … was not found"), `onPageLeave` (pagehide, hidden tab, `erp:before-reload`), `flushTimers` and `reloadForNewVersion` (once a minute at most, `sessionStorage erp-version-reload-at`, the forms save first). ONE component `src/components/VersionGuard.tsx` in the root layout: reads /version.json when the tab comes back (at most once a minute) and every ten minutes, shows "A new version is available – tap to refresh" (never on customer pages), and reloads by itself on stale-version errors; `(app)/error.tsx` and `global-error.tsx` reload instead of "Something went wrong" for those errors. The quote editor and the inspection form push their half-second debounced saves into their saved queues on page leave (`waiting` ref + `flushTimers`); `FormDraft` (gate-in, QC) writes its waiting draft at once. Lesson: on Hobby there is no Skew Protection, so an old tab's chunks and server-action ids simply vanish at each deploy — this guard is the only protection.

Live updates instead of timed re-checks, 27 October 2026 afternoon (test `npm run test:realtime`, 6 checks; `test:live` still green): ONE helper `src/lib/live-connection.ts` (`startLive`: hands the login to Realtime, subscribes, runs `fallback` every `fallbackMs` (default 60 s, `FALLBACK_POLL_MS`) ONLY while the channel is down — error, time-out, closed or no answer in 15 s — and `onReconnect` once when it comes back; `hiddenTracker`: a hidden tab catches up on return only if something arrived meanwhile or it was away five minutes). `LiveRefresh` no longer re-renders every 30 s nor on the first subscribe; its `pollMs` now means "while the live connection is down", and `alwaysPollMs` is only for a person whose access rules hide the table from the live feed (the recovery list for advisors: `recovery_trips` is readable by owner and accounts only). The bell no longer calls `/api/notifications` every 30 s: INSERT and UPDATE (read on another device) arrive live, the minute check runs only while the feed is down or while viewing/acting as someone else (the feed carries the signed-in person's rows only). The bodyshop section tablet (no login, so no live feed) checks once a minute while its screen is on. The Zoho sync page never had a timer. Lesson: realtime respects RLS per subscriber, so before removing a timer check that every role on that screen can SELECT the tables it listens to.

28 October 2026 batch, Part A gaps (verification of the 27 Oct afternoon work). A1: `positionsWords` also gives FRONT AND REAR / LEFT AND RIGHT; `positionsWarningOf(l)` in `quotes.ts` is the ONE rule for the positions-vs-quantity warning (editor, all line types) and `completeQuotation`; `SectionLine.positions` prints its own line in the template; `invoice_lines.positions` and `work_lines.positions` (migration `20261126090000`) carry them to invoices, the work order, the technician's screen and QC labels; `PartsTable` rows have display-only `shownPositions`. A2: `recordSettlement` refuses payments received after the settlement day (`receivedAfter`, greyed on the form), `undoSettlement` + `UndoSettlement` (owner/accounts, reason, Zoho row re-run: unposted = skipped, posted = red by hand), `voidPayment` refuses a receipt inside a settlement, closed jobs' owner reports are rebuilt on settle/undo, `syncSettlementRow` waits until every payment of the settlement reached Zoho (and queues missing ones), the daily reconciliation has a "Card/link settlement transfers" line (`zohoClearingAccounts`, `/banktransactions` per clearing account, STL- references), the advisor bonus marks `cardFeeEstimated` rows and approval needs a "approve anyway" tick.
A3: every path that turns typed text into a line description keeps it whole or refuses in words (`lineTextOrError`/`tooLongWords`, limit 3,000): Parts table rows, splits, imports (supplier charges), "+ Add a part" (the save route; positions default to [] - a missing list used to crash the insert), the customer name for a part, services and part-list names, the PartPicker custom box, the inspection parts list, tool orders, the technician's additional-work remark and parts (`CountedTextarea` = textarea + `TextCounter` with maxLength), the quote's note to the customer (counter, nothing saved above the limit) and the PPF cut note, bodyshop finding remarks; Zoho invoice/credit-note lines keep the full text in the description when the name (100) is shorter; PDF rows (LPO, parts check, sections) may break across pages above 600 characters; the battery sticker shrinks a long name instead of cutting it; the import preview wraps.
A4: `reloadIfStale(e)` in `app-version.ts` is the ONE check for catches around server actions (Parts table save and menu actions, damage note, tint box, panel marks, record deposit, deposit request, trip buttons): an old-version error reloads the page instead of "no connection"; `src/lib/pending-draft.ts` (`writePending`/`readPending`/`clearPending`, 12 h) keeps an autosaving box's unsaved typing in the browser until the server confirms it and re-saves it after a reload (Parts table rows `parts:<jobId>:<rowKey>`, damage note, tint spec, panel marks); BodyshopFindings sends a waiting remark with `keepalive` on page leave; `snapshotForms`/`restoreForms`/`forgetFormSnapshot` (sessionStorage `erp-version-forms`, never passwords/PINs/files/hidden fields, `data-no-draft` opts out) make EVERY form keep its typing across a version reload: whole page just before the reload (replaces), a submitted form for 15 s (merges), restored once after the reload into fields still at their start; `STALE_EVENT` shows the bar when a reload already happened a moment ago; the bar now also shows on /u and /k.
A5 (note `docs/vercel-28-oct-note.md`): `FALLBACK_POLL_MS` 120 s and nothing may poll faster (`Math.max`), `MIN_REFRESH_GAP_MS` 10 s in `LiveRefresh` (first change at once, a burst folded), page `pollMs` overrides removed, recovery advisors and the bell's view-as at 120 s; the quote editor's lock heartbeat only while visible (45 s) and the save route skips the `quotations` write when this person's lock is under 30 s old; the editor's refresh after saves at most every 10 s; the editor's and the inspection form's retry loops back off and pause while hidden; `PollRefresh` 15 s, visible only, and the /u page drops it once the checklist is complete; `CustomerVideos` slows 8→60 s, stops while hidden, "tap to check again" after 20 min; section tablets every 120 s. Payment proofs upload straight to storage (`PaymentForm` uses `JobFileUpload` kind payment_proof tied to the form's uuid client key; `recordPayment` re-points the row) - the 1 MB server-action limit would have refused a normal phone photo; `experimental.serverActions.bodySizeLimit` is now "4mb" for the forms that still send a file. The car picture is copied inside storage (`copy` with `destinationBucket`, the old download/upload as fallback). Short links answer with `s-maxage=86400`; the proxy matcher skips `api/cron/`, `fonts/`, `[mdqir]/` and font files; `vercel.json` `ignoreCommand` skips builds for docs/tests/scripts/supabase/notes-only pushes. Lesson: `notifications.id` is an identity bigint - a test that inserts its own uuid id fails silently through the e2e sql helper and looks like a broken live feed.


B1 of 28 October 2026 (migrations `20261127090000_bodyshop_release.sql`, `..093000_bodyshop_release_backfill.sql`; test `npm run test:b1`, 34 checks): planning follows the job's SIDES. `src/lib/job-sides.ts` (pure): `kindsOfLine` (package snapshots give ppf/tint/other, film/tint details, bodyshop-finding labour = accident), `lineSideOf`, `sidesOfJob` (the department is the truth; a "both" job narrows to its approved work), `sidesOfDepartment`, `sideReleased`/`sidesToRelease` (bodyshop = `jobs.plan_bodyshop_released_at` OR `bodyshop_started_at`; mechanical = `plan_released_at`, which a bodyshop-only release also writes), `releaseWords`, `SIDE_LABEL`/`SIDE_MANAGER`. Planning: `loadPlanning` has `sides`, `releaseNow`, `hoursBySide`, one circle per side (Workshop / Bodyshop, both on a mixed car, numbered 2), `waitingOn` = `PlanningOn` (`parts | workshop | bodyshop | both_sides | advisor`, `planningOnOf(job, live, sides)`; the dashboard and Waiting on you pass `workSidesForJobs`); `NextStep.side` sends a one-side release only to that side's manager. `releaseChecks` + `releaseToWorkshop` (first ticked = lead, work order without bodyshop lines) + `releaseToBodyshop` (stages first via `startBodyshopWork(..., { start, keepStatus })`, then `assignPerson` per person; a mixed car whose mechanical side is still unreleased stays in planning; existing stages get `extendBodyshopStages`); the second side of a mixed car can be released in any open status; `managerConfirmWork` refuses a mixed car until the bodyshop is released and its stages are done (one QC for the whole car); `setStageDone` on a mixed car never moves the job to Ready; gate-out checks the release sign-off on "both" cars too; `startWorkAction` ("Start the work now") is hidden and refused while the car is in planning; `quote-respond` clears the bodyshop stamps with the planning reset and adds newly approved bodyshop work to running stages; `ensureWorkLines` keeps bodyshop lines off the work order but never retires lines already on it; `approvedKinds` falls back to "other" for a bodyshop job with plain labour or services. ONE people source + ONE picker: `technicianLoads` (technicians, team leaders, managers with a team; side, team, `isLeader`, `section`, `onBreak`; bodyshop assignments and open clockings count), `src/lib/staff-picker.ts` (`pickablePeople`, `workSidesOf`, `workSidesForJobs`, `checkPickable` = the server check used by assignJob, both releases, addTechnician, addTechnicianToJob, issueStock; `bodyshopOnCar`), `groupByTeam`/`isLeader`/`sideOfPerson` (team first, else department)/`SECTION_OF_TEAM`/`TEAM_OF_SECTION` in `teams.ts`, `src/components/StaffPicker.tsx` (modes tick / tap / view / chips; `data-staff-picker`, `data-team`, `data-side`, `data-pick-person`, `data-leader`, `data-pick-busy`). Used by PlanningCard (one form per side, `data-release-form`, `data-release`, `data-release-waiting`, `data-circle`), AssignBoard (keyed per car), the job card's Assign card (mechanics + road test before the inspection on any car with a mechanical side), `TechniciansCard` ("On the car": mechanics and the bodyshop team, `mySide` limits a side manager to their side, `removeBodyshopPersonFromJob`), the work order, the bodyshop job page (a leader sees their own team), My section (chips), Workshop load (view), the stock issue list; `TechnicianPicker`/`TechnicianChecks` are deleted. QC fail also tells the bodyshop people on the car. `requireStaff` loads `staff.team`. Small fixes: `knownWordsUp` in `text-case.ts` (whole known words of 3+ characters, never led/mot/aux/vat/abs/ids) at request save (gate-in, amend, loose items); four "Ppf" rows fixed; `noPartsNeeded(steps)` in `parts-steps.ts` (`PAST_QUOTE` statuses) gives "No parts needed ✓" unless requests are open. Found by the simulation: `settings.payment_gateway_domains` held one comma-joined entry, so every pasted N-Genius link was refused - the save and `validateGatewayUrl` now split on commas and spaces too, and the row is tidy. Test seed `stage: "bodyshop-plan"` (`mixed`) at fixed ids e350-e353; pw-lib `pickPerson(scope, id)` answers the busy question. Lessons: a supabase-js batch insert whose rows have different keys sends NULL for every missing key (an omitted `is_active` failed the whole batch), so give every row the same keys; the sim's a22 owner check was stale since `owner_report` joined `OWNER_QUIET_TYPES`.

B2 of 28 October 2026, team leaders (migrations `20261127100000_team_leaders.sql`, `..110000_team_leaders_review.sql`; test `npm run test:b2`, 31 checks). `src/lib/team-scope.ts` is the ONE rule (pure): `bodyshopScopeOf(staff)` = all (owner, a bodyshop-side manager) / team (a team leader: their `team` and its section) / none; `mayActOnSection`, `mayPickPerson`, `mayEnterProduct` (film and tint = PPF team, ceramic = detailing), `maySignAftercare` (PPF leader, managers), `isBodyshopManagerScope`. Server checks with it: `assignAction`/`clockAction` (`personRefusal`: open job + own team), `stageDoneAction` (managers: QC and electrical only), `handOverStageAction` (THE stage hand-over: the stage's own team or a manager, at least one `job_files` bodyshop_photo whose `ref_id` is the stage, the leader's own PIN, owner without; stamps `bodyshop_stages.handover_pin_at/handover_by`), removed items, refit ticks, customer update photos/after photo/`updateSentAction` (managers or `bodyshopQuote`), warranty rows by product (`saveRows`; a team stage's warranty form only SAVES - `data-stage-warranty-save` - the sign-off is the hand-over; QC keeps `stageDoneWarrantyAction`), rolls/film/tint/ceramic, `signOffAftercare`, `electricalDoneAction` (only the job's active electrical stage), `signOff`/`notReady` (only a needed key, an open car at ready/pending_payment; Not ready reopens the key's own team stage - the manager may fall back to the last stage - and that team is told), `/api/inspection/body` (`isBodyshopAssessor`: leaders read, never edit or check), `/api/parts/price-memory` (`viewPartCosts`), `/api/files/register` (a stored path is registered once; a team leader adds bodyshop photos only), the gate-in PDF (no fee for leaders, technicians, QC), the booked car's deposit figure (money roles). Database: RESTRICTIVE select policies `<table>_no_team_leader` on services, stock_movements, declined_work, gate_outs, jobs, job_events, customers, customer_contacts, advisor_bonus_*, price_imports, price_import_lines, supplier_discount_changes, approval_requests, stock_items; `settings_no_team_leader_money` (price/cost/rate/aed/profit/margin/markup/discount/commission/bonus/target/charge/fee/salary/recovery_rates/recovery_types/film_types/package keys); `job_files_read` storage policy excludes team_leader; `npm run test:security` now probes a real leader's login inside a rolled-back transaction (an advisor sees 197 services and 18 jobs, a leader 0). Leader screens read through the admin client; the inspection page reads the job card through it for a leader; `LiveRefresh alwaysPollMs` (2 min) for leaders on /bodyshop, /bodyshop/mine, /bodyshop/[jobId] because their login cannot read `jobs` live. New tables (server-only, audit + no-delete + stamp triggers, in SERVER_ONLY): `bodyshop_problems` (`raiseProblemAction` with the browser's uuid as id - a repeat submit never notifies twice, the photo is the raiser's own bodyshop photo tied by `ref_id`; `answerProblemAction` once only; notifications `bodyshop_problem` to the bodyshop managers + `jobAdvisorIds`, `bodyshop_problem_answered` to the raiser, both locked) and `ceramic_usage` (`recordCeramicUsage`: exterior/interior, product, batch, bottle share from `ceramic_cars_*_per_bottle` at `ceramic_bottle_price_aed`; `jobCeramicUsage`; `jobFilmCost().ceramic` feeds the owner's REAL PROFIT and the package cost history). ONE component each: `StageHandover` (photos via `JobFileUpload` + PIN, `data-stage-handover-open/-send`, `data-handover-pin`) and `ProblemButton` (`data-problem-open/-dialog/-text/-send`); both close when the page returns with the server's answer (useSearchParams prev-value pattern) and on Escape. My team (`/bodyshop/mine`, rewritten): "My team now" (StaffPicker view: clocked on / on a car clocked off / on break / free), 10-day checks due for the PPF leader, ONE keyed car list under four headings Overdue / Ready for us / In progress / Waiting (`data-mine-group`, each car `data-in-group`, so a regroup never closes an open window), per car the team chips, Clock me on/off (`data-clock-me`, only for a bodyshop person), the hand-over (or "Warranty details first" when the stage's details are missing), Problem, Materials. The bodyshop car page: Problems card (`#problems`, answer box for managers), Materials card by product (`data-ceramic-form`), the 10-day check card (`#aftercare`, PPF leader/managers sign with PIN; an owner-activated check counts as finished). Notices: `involvedIds(jobId, sections, advisor, withLeaders)` sends car-wide news to managers + the advisors in charge only; leaders get their own stages (ready for you, heads-up), the release sign-off their key gives, due-out tomorrow when their sign-off is missing, a late notice only for their running stage, problem answered. A team leader lands on /bodyshop/mine ("My team" in the menu, no Home). Tests updated: test-i (owner signs the strip stage, the PPF leader saves details then hands over with photo + PIN, customer updates by the owner, "My team"), test-warranty12 (save + hand-over), layout-check gained a `team-leader` role. Lesson: a review-confirmed early-release hole - `signOff`/`notReady` had no job-state check and their bound actions reach every viewer's browser through the panel's props, so any server action passed to a client component must check the state itself.


B3 discount pop-up, 28 October 2026 (migration `20261127120000_discount_set_by.sql`, tests `npm run test:b3` 22 checks and `npm run test:popups` = `ROLE=popups` layout check): the labour discount amount is stored and shown EXACTLY as typed (AED 5,611 stays 5,611.00); the % is information only. `discountAgainstLimit(totals, limit)` in `quotes.ts` is the ONE wording ("AED x[ + round-off AED y] = n.n% of labour"), used by `ownerApprovalReasons` (reason "discount AED 5,611.00 = 46.8% of labour (limit 15%)"), the editor's status line and the owner's box. `quotations.discount_set_by/at` are stamped by the save route only when the discount kind, value or scope REALLY changes (Apply with the same figures changes nothing) and are carried by `createNextVersion` and `copyEstimate`. The owner's own discount is never a reason (`ownerApprovalReasons(..., { discountByOwner })`) and approves nothing else: it writes no `owner_approved_*`; on a quotation waiting for him the save route re-checks with `approvalReasonsNow(bundle, settings, true)` (quote-data.ts, the ONE computation used by `sendQuotation`, the quote page and the route) and either keeps it waiting with the reasons left (a large total) or sends it back to draft telling the advisors ("Owner set the discount on …: complete and send it"). An approval covers only what the owner saw: `approvalCovers(owner_approval_reason, reasons)` in quotes.ts (the same words, or a total or discount that has only gone down; the discount compared as the amount itself and the labour it comes off, so the round-off chosen at Send is never "more") is used by `sendQuotation` (a send not covered goes back to the owner and clears the old approval), the quote page (`data-owner-approved` only while it stands, else `data-owner-approval-stale`) and the editor (`approvalStands`). While a quotation waits, `refreshQuoteTotals` keeps `owner_approval_reason` current after any change (nothing left = back to draft) and now reads `discount_scope` (the stored totals ignored "labour and services" before). `decideQuoteApproval` refuses unless the quotation is still `pending_owner` and the box's hidden `seen` reason equals the stored one. The words follow WHO gave it (`data-discount-by` owner/advisor, `data-discount-approved`): "given by the owner (no limit)", "by <name> · approved by the owner", or "by <name> · limit x% · …"; an unknown giver counts as not the owner on screen as on the server, and the editor re-syncs its discount figures from the quotation when `discount_set_at` changes (prev-value pattern), so it never credits the viewer with someone else's change. ONE `OwnerApprovalBox` (`src/app/(app)/quotes/OwnerApprovalBox.tsx`, `discountByOf(q)`, `data-owner-box`, `data-owner-box-reason`) shown whenever the quotation waits, on the quotation page and the estimate page; `SendQuoteControl isOwner` reads "Approve and send" for a waiting quotation. The ONE number box (`NumericInput`) reads money as the screens print it: "5,611" and "12,500.00" are thousands (`THOUSANDS`, never "0,500"), "2,5" is still 2.5, one dot at most and no comma after it. Every question window's box has `max-h-[calc(100dvh-2rem)] overflow-y-auto`. The dialog: the %/AED toggle is `inline-flex shrink-0` with whole buttons, the amount box has a fixed width, the row wraps, Escape closes it. Every pop-up is now in the layout check: `tests/browser/popup-check.mjs` (`runPopups()`, run by `layout-check.mjs` with the full check or alone with `ROLE=popups`) opens 26 pop-ups (quotation line menu, discount % and AED, Add a part from the quotation and from the Parts desk, Add a package, Send the quotation, the customer's approve sheet, Send the deposit request, Record the deposit, Send the proforma, Record full payment, Send to the wash, the Parts menu, Split into separate parts, Remove: not needed, Delete the ticked parts, Copy list for supplier, estimate menu, notifications panel, search results, recovery Change price, bodyshop release sign-off and Not ready, stage hand-over, Problem – needs manager; the customer's Pay now only when a gateway link exists) at 1920/1366/1024/800 and fails when the box leaves the window, a fixed box's bottom cannot be scrolled, something is clipped by an overflow ancestor, a label is wider than its button, or controls overlap; screenshots `tests/browser/shots/layout/popup-<name>-<width>.png`. A full-screen backdrop is measured by the box inside it (taller than the screen without scrolling = fault), and `npm run test:popups` runs alone (`pathToFileURL`: the project path has a space). Found and fixed by it: the bell panel ran off the left edge at 800 px (now fixed to the window's right edge), "Transmission and driveline" overflowed its PartPicker category button. An independent four-lens review (21 confirmed findings, about 12 distinct) found the approval holes above, the comma bug and the checker gaps; test-b3 (22 checks) proves each one through the screens. Not in it: the Zoho settings forms (inline, and the page must never be loaded from a dev machine while the real connection is live). test-discount follows the new wording and any limit (the owner set 15% on 23 October). Lesson: an inline pop-up (a `<details>` in the page flow) scrolls with the page, so only a FIXED box whose bottom leaves the window is a fault.

Bank fees, 29 October 2026 (batch "card settlement fees + report upload + photo viewer + inspection report"). Section 1 (migration `20261128090000_bank_fee_rules.sql`, test `npm run test:fees1`, 13 checks): `src/lib/bank-fees.ts` is THE fee rule (pure): `roundHalfUp` (to the fils, half up, never floating-point down: 31.605 → 31.61), `bankFeesFrom(settings)` → per method `{ msfPercent, txnFee, vatPercent, terminals }`, `feesFor(amount, rule)` → `{ msf, txn, vat, cost, total, net }` (VAT on the rounded fees; the job's cost = MSF + fee, the VAT is reclaimable input VAT), `feeRuleWords`, `terminalList`, `methodOfTerminal`. Network International: card machine MSF 2.15% before VAT and no fee (the old 2.26% was with VAT), payment link MSF 1.75% + AED 1.00 per transaction; settings `bank_charge_<method>_percent` (MSF before VAT), `bank_fee_card_txn_aed`/`bank_fee_link_txn_aed`, `bank_fee_vat_percent`, `bank_terminal_ids_card`/`_link`; `bankFeesOf`, `expectedFeeRule`, `expectedFeeCost`, `bankFeeValuesOf` in settings.ts. Payments store the estimate apart (`payments.fee_msf_aed/fee_txn_aed/fee_vat_aed`, `bank_charge_aed` = MSF + fee) and later the actuals (`actual_msf_aed/actual_txn_fee_aed/actual_vat_aed`). The quotation's hidden fee line and the editor's live figure use the same rule (`quoteTotals` opts `bankChargeFixed`, editor `bankFixedCard/Link`, `data-bank-fee-label`). ONE settings box `BankFeeSettings` + `saveBankFeesAction` (permission `cardSettlements`, owner + accounts) on /card-settlements#fees and behind the money list's fold; Settings only links to it (the five old inputs and `updateBankCharges` are gone). Forms that show the fee (PaymentForm, RecordDeposit, ReadySendControl, WaitingPanel) take `charges: BankFees`.
Section 2 (test `npm run test:fees2`, 4 checks; note `docs/bank-fees-29-oct-note.md` with the before/after list): job profit, the advisor bonus, the daily target and the profit page count the fee WITHOUT VAT. `jobProfits` takes the estimate before any payment from the newest approved quotation's `expected_payment_method` applied to the invoice total (`expectedFeeCost`); the approved quotation's hidden fee line is frozen at approval (the lock trigger also freezes `expected_payment_method`) so it only counts without an approved quotation. `approvedQuoteProfits` (exported) and `approvedNotInvoicedProfit` pass `feeOpts(settings, method)` to `quoteTotals`, and `quoteTotals` treats a given `bankChargePercent` as the answer (0 for cash), never falling back to the stored line. The one-off local route `POST /api/e2e/fee-recalc` (`{ dryRun, days }`, e2e secret) recalculated open jobs and jobs gated out in the last 35 days: RCT-00036 → 176.00 + VAT 8.80, J-00017 and J-00460 one dirham less profit each, five draft quotations refreshed. The e2e stage seed takes `method` (the expected payment method, set on the draft before approval locks it).
Section 3 (migration `20261128100000_payment_auth_code.sql`, test `npm run test:fees3`, 11 checks): every card-machine and payment-link payment carries `payments.auth_code` (6 letters or digits, check constraint, index), the key for matching settlements. `src/lib/auth-code.ts` (pure: `needsAuthCode`, `cleanAuthCode`, `AUTH_CODE_RE`, `authCodeWhere`, `authCodeProblem`) and ONE box `src/components/AuthCodeInput.tsx` (`data-auth-code`, capitals, required) in `PaymentForm` and `RecordDeposit` (both send `auth_code`; transfer keeps its reference box). `recordPaymentCore` takes `authCode`: required when staff record card/link (the N-Genius Pay-now path, actor id null, passes the gateway's `authResponse.authorizationCode` when it sends one), refused when the same code sits on another live receipt of that method within 60 days ("Auth code 756117 is already on RCT-00036"). Shown as "Auth …" on the job's money page and the invoice page, and as "Auth code" on the receipt PDF. The migration copied RCT-00036's reference 756117 into the column. Tests that pay by card/link type their own codes (never a real one: a real code is a duplicate).
Section 4 (migrations `20261128110000_settlement_figures.sql`, `..113000_settlement_sum_difference.sql`; tests `npm run test:settle` 30 checks, `npm run test:fees4` 7): the settlement row as the accountant asked - Ticked total · NET RECEIVED · MSF / BANK FEE · TRANSACTION FEE · VAT 5% (pre-filled as 5% of the fees by `vatOnFees`, editable) · Statement reference · Note · Record (`SettlementForm`: `data-settle-net/-msf/-txn/-vat/-note`, `data-settle-adds` green ✓, `data-settle-diff` red "AED X difference", `data-settle-override` "Save anyway" needing a note of 5+ characters; fees typed with a minus count as plain amounts). Pure rules in `card-settlement-rules.ts` (kept import-free for the tests): `checkFigures` (ticked = net + MSF + fee + VAT to the fils; `difference`, `fees`, `cost`, the 6% second look) and `splitFigures` (exact per payment from a report, else MSF by amount, fee per transaction equally, VAT by fees; remainders on the largest). `recordSettlement` takes `net/msf/txn/vat/override/exact/reportId`, stores `card_settlements.msf_aed/txn_fee_aed/vat_aed/difference_aed/difference_note/source/report_id` (`fee_aed` = all the bank kept; the sum rule is now received + fee + difference = gross, and the parts add up to fee_aed), writes each payment's `actual_msf_aed/actual_txn_fee_aed/actual_vat_aed` with `actual_fee_aed` = MSF + fee (the job's cost), logs a saved difference in `audit_log`, and the history shows the parts, "From the bank report" and the red difference. The clean-up SQL clears the new actual columns.
Section 5 (migration `20261128120000_settlement_reports.sql`; tests `npm run test:mtr` = the reader on its own, every fixture as PDF, CSV and Excel, and `npm run test:fees5`, 16 checks): ONE reader `src/lib/settlement-report.ts` (pure: `reportNumber` reads "-175.00", "175.00-", "(175.00)", "1,470.00"; `reportDate`; `headingColumns` finds the TRANSACTION DETAILS headings DTOF TRN · TRMNL ID · CARD NUMBER · TRXN TYPE · ACQ_REF_NBR · AUTH CODE · TRAN AMT · MSF AMT · TXN FEE · VAT AMT · NET AMT · TRXN DESP and their spreadsheet synonyms; `rowsUnderHeadings` places PDF cells under the headings, joining a heading printed on two lines; `parseReportRows` (CSV/Excel) and `parseReportPdf` (from `readPdfCells`); the header FROM/TO/MERCHANT NO/CHAIN ID/DATE; the summary POS transactions, commission, fee transactions, VAT, adjustments, chargebacks, suspended, rebates, BNPL/instalment fees, net payable; `methodOfLine` = the terminal ID from Settings, else TRXN DESP "E/…" card machine, "M/…" payment link; red flags for a non-zero adjustment/chargeback/suspended, a refund or other non-sale line, a line that does not add up, lines that do not add up to the net payable; `matchLines` = auth + amount + date ±3 days, else candidates to pick by hand). Server `src/lib/settlement-import.ts` (`uploadSettlementReport` keeps the file in the private bucket `settlement-reports` and the reading in the server-only table `settlement_reports`; `reviewPaymentsFor`, `matchablePayments`, `reviewMatches`; `recordReport` needs every sale line matched or "not ours" and the red flags ticked, then one `recordSettlement` per method with `exact` per-payment figures and `reportId`). Screen: `ReportUpload` + `ReportReview` (`data-report-line`, the match row `data-report-line-match` with `data-report-matched`/`data-report-pick`, `data-report-check`, `data-report-flag`, `data-report-flags-seen`, `data-report-record`) on /card-settlements#report (`?report=<id>`). The two real reports did not arrive as files: `tests/fixtures/make-ni-mtr.mjs` rebuilds them masked from `ni-mtr.expected.json` (the owner's figures) as PDF, CSV and Excel and never overwrites an original. The real RCT-00036 is matched by the test but never recorded (the payout date is not known); the card machine payment of 24 October (auth 219769) is not in the ERP. Lesson: a `\s` written through `node -e` in bash lands as `s` (the regex then strips every letter s) - write regexes with the Write/Edit tools.
Section 6 (live proof `npm run test:settlelive`, 12 checks against OFJ TEST): `syncSettlementRow` posts, for a settlement with its parts (`msf_aed` set), the transfer clearing → bank for the NET and ONE itemised expense paid from the clearing account (`createExpense` `lines`/`expectTotal`; body `is_inclusive_tax: false`, `tax_treatment: "vat_registered"`, `place_of_supply: "DU"` - Zoho refuses a taxed UAE expense without them) with one line per payment and part: MSF on `bankCharges`, the fee per transaction on `bankTxnFees` (new mapping key, box "Bank transaction fee" on Settings > Zoho Books > Mapping, empty = the bank charges account), each with the 5% `taxId` so the VAT is INPUT VAT; the result checks Zoho's total (= MSF + fee + VAT) and its tax (total − sub_total = the ERP VAT) to the fils, else a red row with both figures; a saved difference stays in the clearing account and the result says so; settlements from before (no parts) keep the old single-fee expense. Proof: AED 10,000 by payment link → transfer 9,815.20, expense 184.80 = MSF 175.00 + fee 1.00 + input VAT 8.80, adopted (not doubled) on a re-run.
Section 7 (test `npm run test:viewer7`, 14 checks at 390 px touch, a tablet and a computer): THE viewer is `Lightbox` (src/components/Lightbox.tsx), upgraded for staff and customers alike: full screen on black, ✕ (`data-lightbox-close`), Escape, swipe down and the phone's back button close it (one `history.pushState({ erpViewer })` per opening, `popstate` closes; the close handler lives in a ref so re-renders never push again); pinch and double-tap (or double-click / wheel / + -) zoom the PHOTO with `touch-action: none` on the stage (`data-lightbox-zoom`), a zoomed photo drags, a pinch never ends as a swipe or a tap (`gesture.pinched`); swipe left/right, arrows and the keyboard move on; "3 / 12" (`data-lightbox-count`) and the item's name in capitals (`data-lightbox-label`); videos play in it muted (`data-lightbox-video`, a failed one offers a page reload); the page's small picture (`thumb`) shows until the sharp `url` has loaded; `setPointerCapture` is tried, never required. `src/components/PhotoViewer.tsx`: `ViewerThumb` (data-viewer-item/-src/-thumb/-kind/-label/-detail/-poster, optional `onOpen`), `openViewerAt(el, override)` gathers EVERY `[data-viewer-item]` of the page in order (so swiping runs through one item's photos, then the next item's) and `ViewerHost` (once, in the root layout) opens the viewer. Used by `MediaGallery` (gate-in photos, staff and the approval page; `thumbs` prop), `CustomerMedia` (`thumbUrl`, `label`), the customer video tile `VideoPlayer` (renews an address older than 40 minutes, then opens the viewer; `label`), the progress, handover and warranty pages. Thumbnails: `src/lib/media-urls.ts` `thumbsFor(bucket, paths, width)` signs Supabase-resized pictures (`transform: { width, quality: 70 }`: a 1.4 MB photo becomes ~30 KB); 480 px for grids, 960 px across a phone. The quotation and estimate links carry no photos. test-videos2 now plays the report videos in the viewer.
Section 8 (test `npm run test:report8`, 37 checks; note `docs/inspection-report-29-oct-note.md`): the inspection report grouped by part, ONE layout for the customer's link, the PDF and the staff preview. `src/lib/report-layout.ts` (pure `buildReportLayout` -> `ReportLayout`: `attention` worst first - rank 0 Dangerous / tyre Replace now (red, the only red), 1 Bad / Required / Empty / battery Replace (dark), 2 Average / Low / Service due / Replace soon / Old tyre / battery Weak (amber); `dangers`; `note`; `sections` BRAKES -> TYRES -> BATTERY -> every other checklist section with a finding, measurement or photo (checklist order from `inspections.checklist`) -> ROAD TEST -> BODY, each with `chip` (worst or `ALL_GOOD`), `lines`, `tables` (brakes Front/Rear pads + discs "thickness · minimum · condition"; tyres `TYRE_ROWS` FL FR RL RR Spare with Tread · Made · Condition), `findings`, `good` (OK items with a photo or note), `mediaKeys` (a photo shows once); `passed` = plain OK items only, minus items shown in a block's numbers; `placedKeys`; `TONE_COLOURS`). `src/lib/report-data.ts` `loadReportData(card, bundle, roadTest, settings, { thumbs })` (the layout, head boxes with mileage, media per place labelled by `videoLabel`, `extraVideos` for videos of unplaced items). ONE web component `src/components/InspectionReport.tsx` (`reportBoxes`, `ReportChip`; hooks `data-report-attention`, `data-attention-line`, `data-report-note`, `data-report-section`, `data-report-table`, `data-finding`, `data-report-photo`, `data-report-good`, `data-report-passed`, `data-chip`; photos are `ViewerThumb`s, videos `CustomerVideoList` so `#v-<id>` anchors stay) used by `/report/[token]` (the whole report on the page inside `CustomerFront`; three boxes get three columns) and the staff fold on `/jobs/[id]/inspection` (`withHead`, `data-report-preview`). `renderReportPdf` draws the same layout: blocks up to `KEEP_WHOLE` 320 pt never split, longer ones continue only between findings and photo rows (title, numbers and first finding together, photo rows `wrap={false}`), four photos a place then "+n more photos on the online report", passed in `balancedColumns`, a last page under 20% full (`pdfLastPageFill` in `pdf-read.ts`) redrawn TIGHT and kept only when shorter. New: `inspection_limits.tyre_old_years` (default 5, Settings "Warn on tyres older than (years)"; "Older" years count as old) and the optional `battery_cca` measurement. test-s, test-body7 and test-videos2 read the new layout. Lessons: react-pdf's `lineHeight` on a Text in a flex row doubles the row height (leave it out); a text column beside a chip goes in a `View` with flex 1; `loading="lazy"` pictures stay blank in a full-page screenshot unless switched to eager first; a PDF order check must use left-edge title lines, because the attention list repeats section names on the right.

The layout check (`tests/browser/layout-check.mjs` + the rules in `layout-rules.mjs`) runs every role's menu pages, the seeded car's staff pages and the customer links at 1920×1080, 1440×900, 1366×768, 1280×800, 1024×768, 800×1280, 768×1024, 390×844 and 800×1280 at 130% text (touch below 1440), failing on sideways scroll, overlaps, right-edge cut-offs, text on text, clipped text, broken words (a compound may wrap at its own hyphen; never a code, number or date, never a single letter), anything out of its card and touch targets under 40 px; `ROLE=<role>|customer|popups`, `PART=n/m`; the pop-up check runs at 1440, 1280, 1024, 800, 768 and 390 and re-opens a pop-up that closed when the window changed. The customer's invoice page prints `invoices.notes`. Every customer document labels the job number "Reference" (quotation, estimate, invoice, inspection report, warranty certificate; web pages and PDFs). Uploads of 31 October 2026 (night batch section 10 finished; note `docs/uploads-31-oct-note.md` with the full upload list; tests `npm run test:uploads` (20 checks: the door, the offline queue on the inspection screen, a sign-out before sending, a refused file) and `npm run test:uploads-video` (a 200 MB video on slow 3G with the signal cut for a minute, about 70 minutes; no other browser suite may run meanwhile - their clean-up wipes its car)). THE upload door is `/api/uploads/grant|prepare|register` (public paths in the proxy; they check a permit or the session themselves), one `TargetSpec` per place in `src/lib/upload-targets/` (`gate_in`, `inspection`, `job_file`, `supplier_proof`, `commission_proof`, `credit_note`, `loose_item`; shared shapes in `common.ts`, the route helpers in `src/lib/upload-door.ts`): the browser names each file with its own uuid, so the object path is the same on every try (prepare answers `exists` for a stored one; register is idempotent per path and returns the first row), refusals carry `permanent`, `takenAt` (the browser's capture time, clamped to 14 days) becomes `taken_at`. A sending permit (`src/lib/upload-grant.ts`: HMAC with a key from SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY, 12 h, `mintGrant`/`readGrant`/`readGrantAnyTime`, via login/link/pin) made while the person may add files there lets a queued file arrive after a sign-out, the tablet idle lock or the /u link's end, under the person who took it; a late inspection file taken before the submit/approval is still accepted; the PIN-only section tablet gets one from /api/tablet/bodyshop after its PIN step; an expired pin permit is a permanent refusal. THE queue is `src/lib/send-queue.ts` (IndexedDB `erp-send-queue` version 2: store `files` = each file's small state with a `scope` index, store `bytes` = its blob and poster written ONCE at the pick - every later change rewrites the state only (`updateRecord` in one transaction; "missing" vs "failed"); every tab runs two lanes (files up to 6 MB, and bigger), each taking the next due file after every send, with a per-file Web Lock `erp-send:<id>` (ifAvailable) so no two tabs send the same file; `heldBack` keeps a file back in memory when its state could not be written (no tight loop); the runner starts once per page life from the root layout's `SendQueueHost` on staff pages (its pill `data-send-queue-pill`/`-panel`/`-row`/`-retry`/`-remove`, above the bottom bars below the nav breakpoint); backoff 5 s to 5 min, everything at once on `online`; BroadcastChannel `erp-send-queue` carries the events to every tab; a file the device will not keep stays in that page's memory; a too-big refusal (413) is permanent; a file removed while sending stays removed).
