Who: the (app) layout's meta `erp-staff-id` names the signed-in person; permits are cached per person (`forgetGrants` on the sign-in pages), every file carries its taker (`staffId`, sent as `takenBy`; a tablet's PIN permit names its person), a permit is renewed only once it has run out (the first one proves when the file was taken), and the door (`senderOf`) makes a file wait with "Taken by another person" while someone else is signed in, and lets the taker's own expired permit prove the time once they sign in again. A database hiccup in any place's checks answers 503 "Couldn't check right now" (`checkLater`, `rowExists`), never a refusal for good. Screens use `useSendQueue({ target, scopeId, grantBody, match, onSent })` from `src/lib/use-send-queue.ts` (pending files with a preview made on the device, `pendingWords`); `JobFileUpload` (`onPending`, `single`, `form` keys kept across a reload), `InspectionMedia` (`useFilesOnDevice`, `useSendGuard` hold Submit/Approve/QC while files are on the device), the gate-in tiles, videos, car picture, panel photos and damage-found-after photos are on it; `uploadFile`/`prepareUpload`/`registerMedia` are gone from upload-client. Resumable (TUS) sends are ON above 6 MB (`NEXT_PUBLIC_RESUMABLE_UPLOADS=0` switches them off); a failed send carries storage's answer text (`body`) so "already exists" and "too big" are told apart. The old routes (`/api/media/*`, `/api/files/*`, `/api/inspection-media/prepare|register`, `/api/parts/supplier-proof`, `/api/commissions/proof`, the converted forms' file fields) stay for tabs opened before the change. Fixed on the way: found-after damage photos for every staff role (and late ones taken before the car left), inspection uploads on the owner's direct edit / bodyshop findings after approval / road tests after approval, the X following the same rule, a late QC post-scan becoming the open round's post-scan, a late payment proof joining its payment. The storage project limit was 50 MB per file on live (four inspection videos up to 88.7 MB were refused since 29 October): raised to 500 MB on live and test on 31 October through the Management API (PATCH /v1/projects/<ref>/config/storage fileSizeLimit), with the owner's OK. Lessons: Chromium's network emulation throttles upload bodies too (2 MB at 400 kbit/s took 41 s), so a slow-3G proof is real; the inspection form is locked until the scan step (`scan_read_at`), so upload tests set it; pw-lib answers every dialog itself, so a test must not add its own handler.

Payments any time, 1 November 2026 (batch "payments anytime + LPO approvals + intake manifold", section 1; migration `20261202090000_payments_anytime.sql`, note `docs/payments-anytime-1-nov-note.md`, test `npm run test:payanytime` = every stage × owner and accounts, a car and a customer with no job, allocation, the proforma taking every advance, the over-payment note, the advisor's link, search Pay; `ONLY=B` skips the stage round). Cause of J-00969: before the proforma (made at Ready) the only way to record money was a closed grey fold at the bottom of the money page while "What to do now" said "Nothing to do yet"; the job card had only "Payments and invoice". `src/lib/customer-money.ts` is THE money of a job and a customer: `jobMoneyOf` (live invoice figures, or the approved total less every advance), `paymentChoices` (the job, its proforma/invoice, jobs that left owing, the customer's account), `unallocatedOf`/`customerCredit`, `allocatePayment` (one step, logged, `settleJob`, a posted Zoho advance re-run by `reopenZoho("deposit")`). An UNALLOCATED ADVANCE is a payment with `job_id` and `invoice_id` null, `notes` = what it is for, `payments.vehicle_id` the car; `allocated_at/by` when it moves. `src/lib/payment-form.ts` `paymentFromForm` is THE reading of a payment form (money page, invoice page and the window); `recordPaymentCore` takes `unallocated` and `overNote`: more than what is left is allowed with a note (logged, the owner told), no more pending_owner for new payments; every receipt gets `payments.token` and the receipt PDF opens with `?p=<token>`. The window (`recordPaymentAnywhere` returns the receipt and the WhatsApp text, `searchPayers`, `loadPaymentChoices` in `invoices/payment-anywhere-actions.ts`) answers in place; `AllocatePayment` + `UnallocatedList` on the customer, car, Invoices and payments pages and as "Customer credit, not allocated" on the job card and money page; accounts' Waiting on you lists each unallocated payment; the customer panel shows "Credit AED X (not allocated)". The advisor's "Send payment link" (any amount) is `PaymentLinkSend` + `jobs/[id]/payment-link-actions.ts` on the /p/ page (`payment_requests.kind = 'advance'`, setting `whatsapp_payment_request_template`). `createChargeInvoice` (declined work) takes the job's advances. Dormant until the migration through `paymentsAnytimeReady()`. Lessons: a short link answers 200 with its preview page, so tests read the /p/ page by its token; the hidden top-bar search box comes first in the page, so tests pick `[data-search-input]:visible`.

Estimate back to the advisor, 1 November 2026 (E-00055; migration `20261202100000_quote_handback.sql`, note `docs/estimate-handback-1-nov-note.md`, test `npm run test:handback`). Cause: the Estimates list filtered advisors to `created_by = me`, and the owner's revision made v2 with `created_by = owner`; approval notices also went to the version's creator. THE rule of who is in charge is `src/lib/quote-lead.ts`: `startersOf(numbers)` (the creator of the LOWEST version of a number) and `quoteLeadIds(q)` (the starter, plus `jobAdvisorIds` for a job's quotation) - never `q.created_by` for "the advisor" (expiry, follow-up, customer opened/answered, sent by someone else, parts desk prices, revision decisions all use it). Handback: pure `src/lib/quote-handback.ts` (`snapshotOf`, `afterOf`, `handbackSummary` "Markup changed on 2 parts, total 966.00 → 987.00", `handbackWords`, `handbackStands` = while status draft); server `keepBaseline` (the owner's first change in the save route keeps `quotations.handback_baseline`; a version the owner made compares with its parent instead), `handBack(id, "approved" | "edited", by)` from `decideQuoteApproval`, `completeQuotation` by the owner, the save route's owner-discount path and `refreshQuoteTotals` when an owner change ends `pending_owner` (never twice: the save route block only runs while still pending); notification `quote_handed_back` (locked hand-over), `handbacksFor(staff)` lines in `WaitingPanel` (`data-waiting-job="quote:<id>"`), `HandbackBanner` (`data-handback-banner`) on the estimate and job quote pages. ONE list `src/components/QuoteListSections.tsx` (`bucketOf`; sections owner/ready/sent/opened/noreply/draft/accepted/carried/declined/closed; `data-list-section`, `data-mine`, `data-started-by`, `data-waiting-on`, whole-row `data-quote-list-open`) on `/estimates` (every advisor sees every estimate, own first) and the new `/quotes` (open jobs' quotations, menu "Quotations"); `QuoteVersionSelect` versions take `by` ("v2 · edited by Omar"). Estimate manage rights (menu, edit, send on the estimate page) follow the starter. Applied on live 2 November 2026 (`handbackReady()` switched it on).

LPO approvals + intake manifold, 1 November 2026 (migration `20261202110000_lpo_refusals_intake.sql`, applied on live 2 November 2026, note `docs/lpo-approvals-1-nov-note.md`, test `npm run test:lpo`, 29 checks). One LPO line can be refused on its own: `refusePoLine` in `src/lib/lpo.ts` + `refusePoLineAction` (owner or head accountant, reason required) sets `part_items.refused_at/by/reason/refused_po_id`, order_status none, the line inactive, the total re-counted (`retotalPo`), the supplier's charges released when it has no line left, the last line refused cancels the LPO; dormant behind `refusalsReady()`. A refused part, or one marked not needed before ordering, gets "Replace (another supplier)" (`replaceRefusedPart` in parts/actions.ts: new row with `replaced_part_id`, the original's `replacement_part_id`); priced, it takes over the approval (`approvedOriginalOf` → To order, no syncLineForPart, the original back to none), `missingPricedParts` treats it as covered and `buildInvoiceDraft`'s partOf follows the replacement for the cost. `addPartsToOpenLpo(jobId, by)` (called after a completed part save and from `applyCustomerResponse` for additional work) puts every priced To order part on no LPO onto the main LPO while it waits ("1 part added to LPO-…" to the owner and the head accountants) or makes a NEW main LPO when the main one is approved; `refreshPendingLpoLine` keeps a waiting LPO's line in step with the part; `loadJobLpos.toOrder` never includes a not-needed part. Part numbers: `isLineNumber`/`realPartNumber`/`NO_PART_NUMBER` in parts-rows.ts (1–3 digits are line numbers: refused when newly typed, never printed on LPOs, the supplier copy or lists; the table's "None" button stores NO NUMBER). PartsTable rows: `refused`, `replaces`, `replaceable`, `notNeeded`, "Not saved yet" while a save waits; `SendLpoButton` names unfinished lines (`data-lpo-unfinished`, `data-lpo-unfinished-ask/-back/-send`). `lpoApprovalsWaiting()` in accounts-waiting.ts is THE pending-LPO list for the owner's and accounts' Waiting on you. Intake manifold: `DEFAULT_CHECKLIST` after Valve cover, `ChecklistItem.hint` + `ITEM_HINTS`/`hintOf` (`data-item-hint`), and `BUILT_IN_PARTS` Engine (Intake manifold, Intake manifold gasket). Lessons: an unhinted purchase_orders → jobs embed fails silently (read job numbers apart); `Collapsible` mounts the report preview only when opened, so tests click it first.

Departments per line, 1 November 2026 night (migration `20261202120000_line_teams.sql`, applied on live 2 November 2026, note `docs/departments-per-line-1-nov-note.md`, tests `npm run test:teams` 21 checks + `tests/unit/line-teams.test.mjs`). `src/lib/line-teams.ts` is THE team rule (pure): `LINE_TEAMS` (mechanical, electrical, dismantle_refit, paint, ppf, detailing, outside; each with its side, staff teams and bodyshop stages), `defaultTeamOf(line, svc, department)` (service outside_work/bodyshop_kind, then the words: PDR → outside, programming/coding/reset/radar → electrical, paint, remove and refit, detailing; the department keeps it on its side), `teamOfLine` (the chosen `quotation_lines.team` wins), `teamsOf` (in `WORK_ORDER`), `sidesOfTeams`, `teamsWords` ("Bodyshop + Electrical"), `staffTeamsFor(teams, side, present)` (a team nobody is in falls back to the side), `teamWithCar`, `realRequests` (gate-in: 3+ letters). `src/lib/line-teams-data.ts`: `teamsReady`, `attachTeams` (reads the chosen teams by quotation), `servicesFor`. `sidesOfJob` on a "both" job follows the teams; `approvedKinds` adds `team:<key>` kinds on bodyshop/both jobs and `stageKeysFor` turns them into stages (strip+refit, paint, ppf, detail, electrical, then qc + handover); the bodyshop board's planned stages do the same; `ensureWorkLines` on a "both" job keeps only mechanical-side team lines. `staff-picker.ts`: `workTeamsOf` ({ teams, sides }), `lineTeamsOfJob`, `presentTeams`, `fitsTeams`; `pickablePeople(settings, sides, teams)` and `checkPickable(ids, sides, teams)` (the release check passes `plan.teams`). `loadPlanning` returns `teams` with each leader (workshop manager for mechanical/electrical, the bodyshop manager for dismantle & refit, the team leaders) and the Advisor circle is `jobs.advisor_id` (read from the job when the caller's row lacks it). Job card: `TeamsCard` (`#teams`, `data-department-choice`, `data-team-line`/`data-team`, `data-line-team` select, `data-team-misfits`, `data-team-with-car`) with `jobs/team-actions.ts` (`setJobDepartment`, `setLineTeam`; owner, advisors, managers; logged); `StageTrack workLabel`; `PlanningCard` `data-planning-teams`/`data-planning-team`. The line lock trigger lets `team` change on approved quotations. Lessons: `gated_in_by` is a protected stamp, so a test that updates it with other columns loses the whole update silently; the staff picker asks "already on a car?" for busy people, so tests pick with `pickPerson`.

Supplier invoices tab, 1 November 2026 night (migration `20261202130000_supplier_invoice_query.sql`, applied on live 2 November 2026, note `docs/supplier-invoices-1-nov-note.md`, tests `npm run test:supinvoices` 28 checks + `tests/unit/supplier-invoice-rows.test.mjs`). `src/lib/supplier-invoice-rows.ts` (pure): `groupInvoices` makes ONE row per (supplier, invoice number without punctuation) over its LPOs (a to-follow LPO is its own "waiting" row), with shares per LPO/car, before VAT/VAT (vat_registered)/total, `match` (matches / price_changed / quantity_different / not_linked), `status` (waiting, queried, new, checked, zoho, paid), `duplicateOf` (two LPOs with the same typed amount that is not their own share, or the same supplier+date+amount or share under another number); `filterInvoices`, `unpaidBySupplier`, `invoiceNoticeTitle`, `invWords` (INV never twice). `src/lib/supplier-invoices.ts`: `loadSupplierInvoices({ jobId?, poIds? })` (a job's split invoices bring their other LPOs), `invoiceHref`, `notifyInvoiceReceived` (type `supplier_invoice_received`, accounts), `supplierInvoicesToCheckCount` (the accounts tile), `siQueryReady`. Page `/supplier-invoices` (owner, accounts, Parts; menu item; `data-si-row`=key, `data-si-state`, `data-si-open`, `data-si-opened`, `data-si-car`, `data-si-prices`, `data-si-check`, `data-si-query`, `data-si-duplicate`, `data-si-match`, `data-si-totals`), actions `checkInvoiceAction` (checks every LPO of the invoice, queues the bills) / `queryInvoiceAction` (dormant until the migration), export `/api/export/supplier-invoices`. THE BILL IS QUEUED AT ACCOUNTS' CHECK now: receiveTicked and applySupplierInvoice no longer auto-check or queue (applySupplierInvoice queues only a correction of an already-checked invoice), decideInvoiceCheck checks and queues only when the owner or accounts decide the last difference; Parts cannot change a checked invoice. Job card `data-job-supplier-invoices` (each car's share), LPO page `data-lpo-invoice-link`, accounts' Waiting on you `si:<key>` rows. Lessons: the dashboard's nav link and the tile share the href, so tests pick the tile by `data-tile`; a row's duplicate words name the other invoice, so tests pick rows by key, never by text; inserting a job needs `gated_in_by`.

Capitals, 1 November 2026 night (migration `20261202140000_capitals.sql`, applied on live 2 November 2026, note `docs/capitals-1-nov-note.md`, test `npm run test:capitals` 15 checks, rewritten: the 21 October "typed letters are kept" rule is gone for customer line text). Live before apply: the save route raises title/details/customer_note (`capsText`), `syncPartLine` titles, `buildInvoiceDraft` descriptions; the quote PDF and the invoice PDF print parts through `partLineName` (no separate side line for parts); `company.ts`/`pay-page.ts`/`template.tsx` print the legal name as the bank account name, on its own bank line. The migration's locks let a letter-case-only change through (`lower(old) = lower(new)`) and the trigger `*_caps` sorts before `*_lock`. Lessons: a new trigger function needs `revoke all ... from public, anon` + `grant execute ... to authenticated, service_role` or test:security fails; a no-break-space name moves whole to the next line when its line is full, so it gets its own line.

Early charges + Pending charges, 1 November 2026 night (migration `20261202150000_charges.sql`, applied on live 2 November 2026, note `docs/charges-1-nov-note.md`, test `npm run test:charges` 30 checks). ONE table of lines `charge_lines` (job_id for early charges, entry_id for `charge_entries` = pending charges; kind recovery/fuel/other, cost_aed, supplier_trn, bill_path, charge_aed, free + free_status, agreed_at + approval_request_id, quotation_line_id, invoice_id) and ONE form `ChargeLinesEditor` (hidden JSON field `lines`, `data-charge-row/-kind/-provider/-recovery-type/-trips/-litres/-description/-cost/-charge/-free`). `src/lib/charge-rules.ts` (pure: `chargeTitle`, `chargeNet`, `chargeWordsEn/Ar` "…: AED 630.00 including VAT (AED 600 + VAT)", `CHARGES_AGREE_EN/AR`, `chargeTotals`, `chargeProblem`, `pendingDays`, `pendingChargeNote`), `src/lib/charges.ts` (server: `chargesReady`, `loadCharges`, `addCharges` (owner's free approved, others pending_owner; cost above charge tells the owner; after first_approval_at → `pushIntoQuotation` through `quoteTargetFor`), `checkInCharges`, `agreeChargesOnApproval` (called by /api/approve), `chargesToInvoice` + `markChargesInvoiced` (buildInvoiceDraft's DraftLine.charge_line_id, ensureProforma, createChargeInvoice which declineAllWork now calls with agreed charges even at no fee), `chargeCostsNotInvoiced`, pending: `createChargeEntry` (own PROFORMA with job_id NULL, never on a job's bill), `convertEntryIfPaid` (tax invoice, payments moved; force with a reason), `afterPendingChargePayment` (recordPaymentCore calls it for an invoice payment with no job), `cancelChargeEntry`, `loadChargeEntries`, `pendingChargesFor`). Job card `ChargesCard` (`#charges`, `data-charge-line`, bills through JobFileUpload kind supplier_invoice refId=line id), `jobs/charge-actions.ts`; check-in `data-charges-incurred`/`data-charge-incurred`/`data-charges-agree`; quote editor fee button follows `jobs.inspection_fee_choice` (none → hidden). `/pending-charges` (+ actions, export `/api/export/pending-charges`, menu badge, `data-pc-*`), `paymentChoices` offers pending proformas, `PendingChargeNote` on the gate-in, the quotation page and the customer panel (`pendingCharges`), WaitingPanel `pc:<id>` reminders after `pending_charges_reminder_days`. Fixed: `spendFormKey` (JobFileUpload) renews the payment form's key from the Record payment window (its "recorded" screen unmounts the form, so a second payment to the same place in the same tab used to be refused as already used); CustomerFront stacks the tax number under the unbroken legal name on phones. Lessons: an `until` on a row must wait for the field a later step fills (the quotation link, the proforma id), not the row; the form guard swallows a tap within five seconds of the last one, so tests pause before a confirm-type button; e2e cleanup now removes `charge_*` rows and job-less invoices of test customers (`E2E PENDING%` names join cust_ids).

Supplier price changes, 1 November 2026 night (migration `20261202160000_supplier_price_changes.sql`, applied on live 2 November 2026, note `docs/supplier-price-changes-1-nov-note.md`, test `npm run test:pricechanges` 15 checks; test-p section 2 follows the new rule). `src/lib/price-change.ts` (pure: `lineChange`, `changeWords` "Approved 250.00 · invoiced 260.00 (+10.00 each, +20.00 total)", `autoLimit` = max(percent of the LPO, AED), `priceDecision`, `profitChangeWords`), `src/lib/price-change-server.ts` (`priceLimits(settings)`, `applyInvoicePrices(poId, Map lineId→price, by, settings)`: invoice_unit_cost, invoice_check match / accepted (a price down, or up within the limit) / mismatch (up above it: the owner's OK or Query with supplier), part final_cost_aed = the invoice price, job event, owner notified). Used by `applySupplierInvoice` (LPO page, `price__<lineId>`) and `receiveTicked` (the receiving panel's `price__part__<partId>` = `data-invoice-price`, prefilled with the approved price from `ReceiveRow.unitCost`). `decideInvoiceCheck` on a mismatch is the owner's (buttons OK / Query with supplier, `data-price-ok`/`data-price-query`); `checkInvoiceAction` refuses while any line waits. LPO page `data-price-change`; job card `data-job-price-change`; `/reports/supplier-prices` (tab in ReportTabs). Settings defaults `supplier_price_auto_percent` 5, `supplier_price_auto_aed` 100. Lesson: `URLSearchParams` writes spaces as "+", so a test reading the answer from the address replaces "+" before decoding.

Confirm + undo for money actions, 1 November 2026 night (B3; no migration; note `docs/confirm-undo-1-nov-note.md`; test `npm run test:undo` 34 checks, unit `tests/unit/money-undo.test.mjs`). THE question is `src/components/ConfirmSubmit.tsx` (props label/title/lines/live/confirmLabel/name/value/tone/hook/attrs; `live` reads the form's fields at the tap, radios by their label text, `proof: true` gives "proof attached ✓"/"no proof attached"; the form's own validity is checked first; hooks `data-confirm-open`, `data-confirm-money`, `data-confirm-text`, `data-confirm-yes-money`, `data-confirm-cancel`) on Paid to supplier, the supplier reversal, LPO approve (`DecideForm`), credit note, credit-note refund, card settlement (`SettlementForm`, `ReportReview`), early tax invoice (money page) and the pending-charge tax invoice; `PaymentForm` asks for EVERY payment (`data-confirm-full`/`data-confirm-yes`, "Record payment of AED x · against PRO-… · Cash"; `openConfirm` checks validity first); the deposit window already asked. Undo: `src/lib/money-undo.ts` (pure `DEFAULT_UNDO_MINUTES` 10, `undoSecondsLeft`, `undoLabel`, `mayUndo` = the one who did it or the owner, `heldForUndo` for outbox kinds payment/deposit/billpayment - the drain skips them during the window, setting `money_undo_minutes` on Settings > Money via `saveMoneyLimits`, which also holds the supplier price limits), `src/components/UndoButton.tsx` (black bar "Payment recorded · Undo (9:58)", drawn in the browser only via useSyncExternalStore, `action` or `onUndo`+`busy`; `data-undo-bar`, `data-undo-money`) in the Record payment window (`undoPaymentFromWindow`, `data-payment-undone`), the deposit window (`data-deposit-undone`), the payment rows of the money and invoice pages (`undoPaymentAction(id, back)`) and the LPO (`undoSupplierPaid`). `undoPayment` in payments.ts voids with "Undone within N minutes by <name>" and deletes the pending outbox row; refused when the payment made the tax invoice. After the window: `voidPayment` (owner or head accountant) and `reverseSupplierPaid` (owner or accounts, reason) now REOPEN the Zoho row instead of a red by-hand row, and the worker takes the payment back in Zoho: `reverseZohoPayment` (DELETE /customerpayments/{id}; "already removed" when gone) in `syncPaymentRow`, and `syncBillPaymentRow` deletes the vendor payment of an LPO no longer paid and clears `zoho_billpayment_id`; the REAL book's opening balances stay by hand. The Zoho removal itself is not proven on the test server (no Zoho connection there; tests never call the live cron). pw-lib `pressRecord(scope)` (Record + Yes) and `pressYes(page)`. Lessons: `created_at` is a protected stamp (an update moving it is silently refused), so a test of "after the window" sets a one-minute window and waits; textContent joins the bar's two parts without a space.

Spell check while typing, 1 November 2026 night (no migration; note `docs/spell-while-typing-1-nov-note.md`; test `npm run test:spell5` rewritten). STANDING RULE REPLACED: word boxes are checked by OUR spell check, not the browser's (it never knew the workshop's words). THE component is `src/components/SpellHost.tsx`, mounted once in the (app) layout: every `input`/`textarea` with `data-spell` (and not readOnly/disabled) gets a dotted red underline drawn on a fixed, see-through overlay copied from the box's own font/padding/scroll (`data-spell-overlay`, `data-spell-word`), a tap or arrow key on a word opens `data-spell-menu` with `data-spell-suggest` (case kept by `matchCase`), `data-spell-ignore`, `data-spell-remove` for mash and `data-spell-add` (owner and service advisors); the page learns good/bad words per tab and only asks `/api/spell` ({ text } -> { flagged, canAdd }) about unseen words, 700 ms after typing stops; a suggestion is written with the native value setter plus an input event, so React's onChange sees it. `Input`/`Textarea` in ui.tsx set `data-spell` themselves and `spellCheck={false}`; `spell={false}` turns ours off for one box; never checked: `noSpellName(name)` in `src/lib/spell-rules.ts` (name/phone/plate/vin/part_number/address/amount/price/qty/ref/code/trn/... field names), `textCase="upper"`, type search, numeric boxes, `lang="ar"`/`dir="rtl"`. Raw boxes carry `data-spell=""` by hand (the quote editor's NameBox, the my-jobs note, the declined-all reason, the custom part name). The shared dictionary in `src/lib/spell.ts` (`loadShared`, five minutes per server, paged past 1,000 rows): spell_words, known_words, tyre brands/models, premium brands, recovery companies, makes, models, customers, contacts, staff, suppliers, part_catalogue and services names; `rememberSpellWord` makes an added word count at once on that server. The send window has no spelling panel any more (`SpellCheckPanel`, `spell-docs.ts` and the `spell`/`checkSpelling` props are deleted): sending is never asked about spelling.

Small fixes C1-C4, 1 November 2026 night (no migration; test `npm run test:c12` 9 checks, unit `tests/unit/month-card.test.mjs`; lists `docs/c4-lists-2-nov-note.md`). C1: `retrySupplierRows(supplierId)` in zoho-sync.ts puts the supplier's FAILED vendor/bill/billpayment outbox rows (not hand_only, not manual) back to pending; `updateSupplier` calls it when the TRN changes or the VAT switch moves and says "n Zoho items try again now."; a VAT-registered supplier with no TRN shows the red `data-no-trn` badge on Parts > Suppliers and a red notice on its LPOs (`SupplierRow.vat_registered` in parts-data). C2: `test_mode_enabled` defaults to FALSE (a missing row is off), `clearTestDataAction` reads the row fresh and needs DELETE typed (`data-clear-confirm`); the card stays hidden while off. C3: `src/lib/month-card.ts` (`monthCard`, `aedWhole` never "−0"): a month whose target so far is 0 (it starts on a Sunday) reads "Month just started" over an empty bar, a missing target "No daily target set"; ProfitPanel and AdvisorPanel use it. C4: 14 open jobs approved with the old fee wording (charged inclusive), and no customer place shows "+ VAT" alone.

Stale browser tests, 2 November 2026 (note `docs/stale-tests-2-nov-note.md`): test-m, test-zoho2, test-h, test-i, test-tint11, test-zoho3 and test-oldparts are green again. Every one was the test, except one real bug in tonight's C1: `retrySupplierRows` re-queued a POSTED bill that `flagBillsAfterVatChange` had just turned red; it now touches only rows with no `zoho_id`. Points to remember: the car card's link sits under its content (read `[data-car-card]`, not the link); the internal box's rows are `[data-profit-line="<key>"]` with `[data-profit-detail]`; the gate-out needs `[data-mileage-out]` and a cluster photo in `[data-final-mileage]` before it closes a job; the gate-in/gate-out record is `/jobs/[id]/gate-in`; pasted payment links must be on a gateway domain; the test database copy has no `zoho_went_live_at` row (live has it as "") - upsert it inside a rolled-back test. test-t is still stale (deposit question of 16/20 Oct, live services data, name wrapping of 20 Oct) and waits for its own rewrite. Lesson again: a backslash in a bash heredoc or a single-quoted node -e patch is lost - regex patches go through the Edit tool or a Write-tool script with String.raw.

Price calculation and nothing below cost, 2 November 2026 (section 1; migration `20261203090000_below_cost.sql`, applied on live 2 November 2026 evening; note `docs/2-nov-s1-note.md`; tests `npm run test:s1-price` (sections A-H) + `tests/unit/below-cost.test.mjs`). Q-00039's "Peter added FRONT RH HEAD LIGHT, AED 5,502.73" was the part's COST, printed with no label: the list 12,228.30 was already net and the Parts table's supplier pick took Gargash's 55% standard discount off again. No markup is ever subtracted. THE price rule stays `costPlusMarkup` behind `linePrice`/`lineTotal`/`lineUnitPrice`/`markupBoth`; its one straggler was the early charge (charges.ts `chargeQuoteLine`, the ONE way a charge becomes a quotation line: an Other line with a cost takes the markup that lands on the charge, `markupFromSell`). Nothing below cost, pure in quotes.ts: `costOfLine(l, opts)` = what the internal box counts for the line (lineCost; on a package-type line its installer hours × `bodyshopRate`, a plain PPF line's `plainPpfFilm`, a package's `packageLineCost` with the costing); `belowCostOf(l, { packageCosting, bodyshopRate, headerDiscount })` judges the line's total LESS its share of the header (labour / package) discount (`headerDiscountShares`: by price, remainder on the largest), null for labour, hidden, complimentary, unchosen, left-off or unpriced lines, `approved` only while the price is no lower and the cost no higher than the owner's stamp; `belowCostAll(lines, q, opts)`; `worseBelowCost`; THE change check `belowCostChangesIn(before, after, opts)` (every line a change makes worse, so a labour line under an AED discount can take a package below cost); `needsPackageCosting`; `NOT_PRICE_KEYS`/`priceSideKeys` (a cost - unit_cost, installer hours, package items, hours estimate, title - and plain words are never refused; everything else is price-side). `sendBlockers` (+ `packageCosting`, `bodyshopRate`) stops unapproved lines; `floorProblem` leaves below-cost lines to it. Server: `src/lib/below-cost.ts` (`BELOW_COST_Q_COLS`, `headerOf`, `belowCostOptsFor` = the ONE place for the costing + bodyshop rate, `linesForBelowCost`, `unapprovedBelowCost`, `stampBelowCost` writing `quotation_lines.below_cost_reason/_by/_at/_sell_aed/_cost_aed` + event `below_cost_approved`, `flagBelowCost` = event `below_cost` + notification `quote_below_cost`, never to the one who made the change). The save route reads the lines once (`belowLines`) and gates with `belowChanges` + `belowCostGate(hits)` on addLine, a price-side patchLine and a header discount change: an advisor gets 400 "Not saved … only the owner can sell below cost", the owner 409 `ask: "below_cost"` (`lineId`, `more`) then `below_cost_reason` (5+ characters) stamps every line he saw at its after-discount figures; everyone is refused before the migration. A cost-only patch, a removed line and the film rolls are saved and flagged, never refused. `belowCostApprove {id, reason}`; `belowCostAsk {id}` answers `asked: true`, or `asked: false, notBelow: true` when the SAVED line is not below cost. Editor: an advisor's change is judged against what the quotation holds (`basisLines` = the server copy plus `sentFields`, minus refused fields), never his own unsaved figure; a price-side figure below cost is held per line (`belowHeld` {fields, words}, `data-below-cost-held`, `data-below-cost-put-back`), only the held fields' timers stop, plain words and costs still go, and the next change is judged together with the held figure and sent with it once it clears. `askOwner` reads the answer ("The owner was asked." only on `asked`, else the saved figure is put back with `data-below-cost-info`); the owner's windows queue (`belowAsks`; patch, add, header or line approval; "Change the price/discount" puts back what the quotation holds); the merge takes `below_cost_*` from the server row and re-applies held figures; `belowMap` feeds `belowCostNote`; the discount window takes `check` (`data-discount-below-cost`, `data-discount-apply`: blocked for an advisor, the owner is asked why). The estimate page costs packages with make, model AND variant, like the quote page and the server. Revisions: parts syncPartLine inserts on version 2+ take `markupForAddedPart` (a twin by compact part number or `sidelessName` - left/right words only, front and rear stay different parts, FL/FR = front, RL/RR = rear - else the most used PERCENTAGE markup among parts with a real cost (`REAL_COST_MIN_AED` 10; AED markups and placeholder costs never teach one), a tie going to the LOWER, else the minimum). `createNextVersion` brings every copied part line to its part (`syncChangedParts` with the pure `partLineChanges` {gone, money, words}: cost, quantity, type, brand, side; never the name), adds missing priced parts and the early charges waiting for a revision (`chargesWaitingForRevision`, `pushWaitingCharges`), returns `synced` and flags them unless `quietBelow`. `revisionPreview` shows added, changed (with `changes` words, `data-revision-changes`), taken-off and charge rows (`data-revision-row="charge"`). `askOwnerForRevision` writes `revision_request_parts` on every new request (null for an advisor's or a charge's) and adds a part only to a waiting PARTS request that already names its parts; `withdrawStaleRevisions` touches only kind parts, status approved, no newer version; `requestPartsGone` decides "nothing left" (box `allGone`, `data-revision-empty`); `requestPartsCleared` empties the list on allow, decline and use. `decideRevision` needs a note when red, closes only when every listed part is gone (telling the requester), makes the version with `quietBelow`, stamps ONLY the red rows he saw below cost (by part or charge line, at the previewed figures) and flags the rest. `changeWords` reads "… – cost AED x (list AED y less z%)"; `cheapCostWarning` flags a cost under half of the same part on the job or the last price for its number; the bell starts "CHECK THE PRICE · ". Lessons: the React Compiler skipped the whole editor ("Existing memoization could not be preserved") after a new JSX handler called `setDirty` with a copying updater; keep new handlers' setters plain. `String.raw` in a patch script still interpolates `${}`. A heredoc with an en dash and apostrophes failed whole again; patch scripts go through the Write tool. A test can put a line on an approved quotation by setting its status to draft and back (the header lock does not freeze `status`).

Placeholder costs, 2 November 2026 (section 2; migration `20261203100000_part_costs.sql`, applied on live 2 November 2026 evening; note `docs/2-nov-s2-note.md` with the 2.4 list; tests `npm run test:s2-costs` (sections A-H) + `tests/unit/part-costs.test.mjs`). Q-00039's grill moulding at AED 1.00 from "OFJ Additional" (87,000% markup): THE cost check is pure `src/lib/part-cost-check.ts` (`partCostCheck` = ask_cheap under `askBelow` 5 unless `boughtSimilar` within 20%, far_below under 50% of the last price BOUGHT or of a same-job twin via S1's `cheapCostWarning`; `costNeedsAnswer`, `discountTwiceNote` for the Gargash slip, `markupTooHigh`/`markupTooHighWords` over 500%, `lpoLineProblem` THE LPO gate, `costLimitsOf` from settings `part_cost_ask_below_aed`/`part_cost_far_below_percent`/`part_cost_similar_percent`/`part_markup_warn_percent` edited in Settings > Money; the dead `markup_warn/confirm_percent` boxes are gone); the server side is `src/lib/part-cost-history.ts` (`boughtPrices` = LPO lines at the invoice price, ordered/received parts, the stock list, by compact part number, cached a minute per server, never placeholders; `costChecksFor(parts, jobParts)` → `CostCheck {ask, hold, confirmed, note}`; `costCheckForLine` for estimate parts; `costCheckValues` → RowValues; `costReferencesFor` is now S1's revision-box finder too; `pricesToFix` = 2.4). `parts-rows.ts`: `isPlaceholderSupplier` ("OFJ…", TBC, < 3 letters; never Our stock), `isPlaceholderPartNumber`, THE display rule `realPartNumber` (line numbers, OFJ, NO NUMBER → null) and THE write rule `cleanPartNumber` (stored stand-ins read as NO NUMBER), `partNumberProblem` (one refusal wording), RowValues `cost_ask/_for/_kind/_hold`, `stock_item`, `stock_words`, `cost_note`; `rowMissing` adds "Not a supplier", "Pick the stock item" and "Real price?" (only while `cost_ask_hold` = "1", i.e. `costConfirmReady()`), so Pricing done, SendLpoButton, syncLineForPart, tellAdvisorIfComplete and the counts follow. `autoSavePartRow` (RowSave `stockItemId`, `costConfirmed`) computes the check before writing, stores `part_items.cost_confirmed_*`, logs `part_cost_confirmed`, forces Our stock → in stock at the stock item's cost, and returns `server` values the row merges; the table shows `data-cost-ask` (+ `data-cost-confirm` "Yes, real price"), `data-cost-note`, `data-stock-words`/`data-stock-pick`/`data-stock-change`, and hides stand-ins and the "Our stock" row from the supplier lists. ONE `StockItemPicker` (FloatingMenu: `data-stock-picker`, `data-stock-item`, `data-stock-add`, `data-stock-new-*`; actions `stockItemsForPicker`, `stockItemQuick`) on the Parts row ("From our stock…") and the estimate desk (`data-desk-stock-open`; `quotation_lines.stock_item_id` carried at gate-in); `quote-respond` records the "out" movement and `final_cost_aed` = the stock average at approval. LPO gate: `lpoProblemsFor`/`lpoProblemWords` in `lpo.ts` used by `createMainLpo`, `addPartsToOpenLpo`, `refreshPendingLpoLine` (approvers told) and `decidePurchaseOrder` approve ("Not approved: 1 line has no real price …"); `splitMainLpo` never makes an "Our stock"/stand-in supplier row; dead `createPurchaseOrder` deleted. Quotation: `sendBlockers` takes `costAsks` (from `loadQuoteChecks`/`costAsksOf`) and blocks stand-in suppliers (job parts and estimate `supplier_text`); the editor's `data-markup-high`, `completeQuotation` ask `high_markup` (`data-high-markup-ask/-fix/-send`, event `high_markup_sent`, notification `high_markup`); the advisor's own price and `priceEstimatePart` ask the same question (save route 409 `ask: "cost_check"` → `data-est-cost-ask`; the desk's `data-desk-cost-ask`/`data-desk-cost-confirm` with `ReleaseFormLock`, now shared from `forms.tsx`). "Prices to fix (n)" card on /parts (`PricesToFix`, `data-prices-to-fix`, `data-price-to-fix`) + the Parts Waiting line. 2.5: `realPartNumber` on the parts check PDF, QC labels, PoForms, /parts, question, handover, approvals, staff invoice page, editor estimate line; `cleanPartNumber`/refusal at every write (estimate desk, carry-over, invoice draft, stock items, addPartToStock, exchangePart, readRow); the migration turns stored stand-ins into NO NUMBER (parts, unlocked estimate lines; LPO and invoice lines kept as stored). Not built: an owner "Correct cost" for an invoiced part (J-00609 INV-00002 keeps 1.00). Lessons: FormGuard swallows a tap within five seconds, so an in-form question needs `ReleaseFormLock` (keyed by what it asks) or its Yes is lost; the purchase list is cached a minute, so a test that seeds history waits 62 s; a supplier row that is a stand-in must be filtered from the list AND from the row's matching (`supplier` lookup) or the select points at a hidden option; a bash heredoc patch full of escaped template literals failed whole once more, so long patch scripts go through the Write tool. Review fixes (3 November 2026; migration `20261203101000_stock_test_leftover.sql` switches off the live test stock item "Rear brake discs · E2E-DISC-1", guarded, applied on live 3 November 2026; test-s2-costs sections I-M, `ONLY=` filter): a stock cost exempts a part only when it came from real purchases (`deliveredStockItems`: stock movements in/return with a po or a part above AED 1; `boughtPrices` counts only those stock items; `costCheckForLine` takes the real `stockItemId`), so a cost typed in the picker or on the stock page is asked like any typed cost; `stockItemQuick` gives a matching item with no cost the typed cost and returns `note` when an existing cost is kept (`data-stock-note`, "Use it"); the picker's `need` and the row's `stock_on_hand` show amber when the shelf has fewer than the part needs (`data-stock-short`, never a block) and `receiveFromShelf` in `stock.ts` is THE shelf path (approval in quote-respond, a replacement priced From our stock - now received at once, never To order) and tells Parts (`stock_low`) when the count is short. Ordered/received parts are "settled" (`isSettledStatus`; RowValues `settled` = rowMissing skips stand-in, stock item and cost question; `costChecksFor` judges `final_cost_aed` when set, PartForCheck carries `order_status/final_cost_aed`; `settledCostProblem` is THE rule for them) and never block a revision: `sendBlockers` and `costAsksOf(checks, parts)` apply the stand-in and cost blockers only while `order_status` = none; `confirmEstimateUnchanged` passes `costAsks`. An approved part not ordered yet (`TableRow.approvedNotOrdered`) answers its question on the locked row through `confirmPartCost` (`data-cost-confirm-approved`; joins a waiting LPO). `lpoLineProblem` returns `LpoProblem {words, fix: confirm|price|stock}` and `lpoProblemAdvice` is the one wording (a question Parts can answer never says Remove: not needed); the LPO page marks each line (`data-lpo-line-problem`) and `DecideForm problems` shows them in the Approve box with Approve off (`data-lpo-problems`). "Correct the cost…" (`TableRow.settledFix`, `data-settled-fix`/`data-correct-cost`, ONE `CorrectCostDialog` → `correctSettledCost`: from our stock = item linked, one "out" movement, final cost = average, Our stock; bought from a supplier = real supplier + cost (asked when cheap), an LPO addressed to a stand-in with no invoice entered is re-addressed with all its lines; a proforma line's internal cost follows; a tax invoice refuses; event `part_cost_corrected`). `loadJobLpos.toOrder` never holds a shelf part; `exchangePart` re-prices (open row, no copied stand-in or placeholder cost) when the original came off the shelf or had no real price; Our-stock rows priced before the rule (no item) are never unpriced (listed in Prices to fix, `data-stock-pick-hint` under Pricing done), and a new Our stock without a picked item is refused on save; Parts' Waiting line counts only parts not ordered yet; the estimate desk and the advisor's price read the stored `cost_confirmed_aed`; `belowShareWords`/`cheapCostWarning(…, share)` follow the far-below setting.

Payment hand-over, 2 November 2026 (section 3; migration `20261203110000_payment_reports.sql`, applied on live 3 November 2026, backup `backups/2026-11-02T21-55-08`; note `docs/2-nov-s3-note.md`; tests `npm run test:s3-payreport` (sections A-H, `ONLY=`) + `tests/unit/payment-reports.test.mjs`; test-payanytime B2/V/D2/H2 and test-m section 3 follow the new rules). The J-00969 cause: a real accounts login always had Record payment in the job card's money tile; View as hides every Record payment button and the window host (`!staff.viewingAs`), and accounts have no Jobs/Cars menu, so the owner only ever saw his own. Now `RecordPaymentButton readOnly` draws the same button greyed with `VIEW_AS_PAY_WORDS` ("View as is read-only – use Act as to record", `data-record-payment-readonly`) on the job card, money page, car page, customer page and Invoices; accounts reach the job card from `MoneyRow` (`data-money-row-job-card`; the row is now a div with the money-page link inside), their Waiting rows (`data-waiting-job-card`), the window's search rows (`data-payer-job-card`) and the reports card. THE window search is `payerRows(q, settings)` in `customer-money.ts` (a job hit = that job; ONE car rule for every hit: a car shows its open jobs, then its jobs that left still owing (balance above zero; a gone job with no total never), else the car alone; a typed job number is that job; a customer hit = each of their cars (`vehicles.customer_id` and their jobs' cars) by the same rule, "No car on record" only when they have none; car picture through `thumbsFor`, `jobMoneyOf` balance, `moneyWords`), rendered with `CarThumb` (`data-payer-hit`, `data-payer-job`, `data-payer-car`, `data-payer-who`, `data-payer-balance`). Plates: `src/lib/plate-search.ts` (`cleanNeedle`, `plateParts`, `vehicleOrFilter`: "GG 13706", "gg13706", "Dubai GG 13706" match the code and the number kept apart) used by the window and `/api/search`. Advisors: `payment_reports` (RLS read for owner/accounts/service_advisor, writes through the server only, in `supabase_realtime`), job file kind `payment_report` (JOB_FILE_KINDS, FILE_KINDS, the check constraint, `kindAllowed` lets advisors send it), setting `payment_report_reminder_hours` (4, Settings > Money). Pure rules `src/lib/payment-report-rules.ts` (`reportLine` = the owner's words "J-00969 · DUBAI GG 13706 · MERCEDES-BENZ S 580 · <customer> · AED x · Card · from Anas", `checkReportInput`, `reportLate` by `workingHoursBetween`, `reminderHoursOf`, `reportClosedWords`); server `src/lib/payment-reports.ts` (`loadReports` with photos/thumbs, `createPaymentReport` = the photo must be on the server under the report's browser-made id, a resend replaces a queried report and may carry its photo, a second waiting report asks once; `reportToRecord`, `closeReportWithPayment`, `afterReceiptForReports` called by `recordPaymentCore` (`PaymentInput.reportId`; a receipt recorded another way answers the report THE pure rule `pickReportForReceipt` picks: same job, method and amount, waiting or queried, never one whose auth code or reference says it is a different payment - codes compared without spaces/dashes/case, references agree when one sits inside the other, a matching code first, waiting before queried; recordPaymentCore passes the receipt's auth code and reference, a cheque's number as the reference), `queryReport`, `withdrawReport`, `reopenReportsOfPayment(id, actor, { word: "undone" | "voided", reason })` from `undoPayment` AND `voidPayment` (the voided receipt's client_key becomes `undone:<id>`/`voided:<id>` so the report can be recorded again; the advisor's bell "Receipt voided · …"), `paymentReportsHourly` = cron step 27 with `reminded_at`, `reportWaitingItems` inside `WaitingPanel` (accounts/owner: waiting reports with inline kind `payment_report`; an advisor: his queried ones), `waitingReportByJob` for `moneyState({ reported })` = "Payment reported by Anas, checking"); actions `jobs/[id]/payment-report-actions.ts`; ONE component file `src/components/PaymentReport.tsx` (`PaymentReportButton` "Payment received – send to accounts" with `data-payment-report-open`/`-dialog`, `data-report-method`, `data-report-amount`, `data-report-send`, `data-report-sent`, `data-report-second`; `PaymentReportQuery` with the `payment_query` reasons; `PaymentReportLines` amber `data-payment-reported`, red `data-payment-queried` with Send again/Withdraw, green `data-payment-report-recorded`; `PaymentReportActions` on Waiting rows) and `PaymentReportsCard` (`data-payment-reports`, where the dead `CounterPaymentsCard` was; `loadCounterPaymentsToConfirm` is gone). The Record payment window opens on `{ reportId }` (Record buttons, and `?pay-report=<id>` from the bell, read with `useSearchParams` - the host now sits in a Suspense - then dropped by `replaceState`): `data-report-banner` with the slip and Query, `PaymentForm` `prefill`/`fixedKey` (= the report id, the receipt's client key)/`initialProofs`, hidden `report_id`; `paymentFromForm` refuses a report not waiting or on another car and uses the advisor's slip as the proof. `AuthCodeInput optional` for the advisor (accounts type it when they record). The money page's "Customer says paid" section lists the reports (the old one-line form only before the migration); the Invoices filter is "Customer says paid / reported". Side fix: the job card's Send payment link phone `/[^\d]/g`. Lessons: `spendFormKey` must be given the SPENT key, never the current one (spending the current key renews it, the effect sees a new key and spends again: "Maximum update depth exceeded"); `next dev` refuses `--env-file` inside NODE_OPTIONS here, so the dev test server was started with a small wrapper that reads `.env.test.local` itself; a tab that did not start View as drops it on its next page (ModeGuard), so a test sets the cookie again before each View-as page; a window opened from a list line that its own success removes (Send again on a queried report) must live outside that line (`PaymentReportLines` holds it), or the live refresh unmounts it with its "Sent ✓"; a test waits for the LAST write of a server step (the undo's bell, the replaced report's status), never the first. Review fixes of 3 November 2026 (an independent review, 13 confirmed findings): the job card's big money button opens the waiting report itself (`RecordPaymentButton reportId`, label "Record the reported payment"), and a window opened on a report offers ONLY that report's car (`loadPaymentChoices` filters the targets) plus `data-record-other-payment` "Record a different payment instead (this report keeps waiting)" (the always-rule); on the money page the page's own form is folded under "Record a different payment (not the one Anas reported)" while a report waits, and every other Record button there says "Record a different payment"; View as accounts shows the greyed button in every money-page branch (car gone owing, proforma not sent, sent and unpaid, deposit) instead of "Waiting for accounts"; THE advisor-button rule is `stillOwes(jobMoney)` in payment-report-rules.ts on both pages (open or gone); ONE `SlipTile` (PaymentReport.tsx) draws a slip everywhere by `slipKind(contentType, path)` - a photo in the viewer, a PDF as a tile opening a new tab (`data-slip-kind="pdf"`), a video in the viewer - and `loadReports` asks image transforms for photos only; `closeReportWithPayment` also closes a QUERIED report ("the query is settled, nothing to send again"), and Send again after money was recorded on the car since the query asks once ("RCT-… was recorded on this car after the query … Send it as well?"); the same window key with a different amount or method (a copied tab) is refused with `renewKey` (the dialog spends the key; the stray slip registered under it after the first report is taken off), the old "same reporter and job" repeat answer now also needs the same amount and method; auth codes and references print through `ReportMethodWords` (never broken across lines). `popup-check.mjs` now opens the advisor's window, the Record payment window on a report and the Query window (204 shots). Lessons: `recordPaymentCore` refuses the same amount and method on one invoice within a minute even with different auth codes, so a test of two equal card payments waits that minute out; `popup-check` cleans up only at its end, so a layout run with `NO_SEED=1` must be followed by the clean-up SQL before it, or leftover rows on the fixed e2e car leave four pop-ups unreached.

Invoices and payments: every job with money, 2 November 2026 (section 4, built 3 November; no migration; note `docs/2-nov-s4-note.md`; tests `npm run test:s4-moneylist` 41 checks + `tests/unit/money-list.test.mjs`). Cause: the list was built from invoices only (`loadMoneyRows` read payments by live invoice id; an advance has `invoice_id` null; invoice-less rows were only jobs at Ready) and the tiles came from other queries than the lists they linked to, so J-00969's AED 30,000 advance was in Collected today and in no row ("Part paid · 0", "Awaiting payment AED 0 · 0 cars"). THE money of a job is `jobMoneyFrom` in `customer-money.ts` (live tax invoice else proforma, never a deposit document, via `liveDocOf`; before it the approved draft total less every cleared recorded payment on the job), batched by `jobMoneyMany(ids, settings, pre?)`; `jobMoneyOf` is `jobMoneyMany([id])`, and `paymentChoices`/`payerRows` use the batch too. The draft is `draftFrom(inputs, settings)` (pure) over `loadDraftInputs(jobIds)` (batched: jobs, `approvedQuotationsMany` in `quote-data.ts` which also carries `rounded_total_aed`, quotation lines, parts, `chargesToInvoiceMany` in `charges.ts`); `buildInvoiceDraft(jobId)` is the two together. Proven identical to the one-job reading over every TEST job (40, with two approved versions of one number, a rounded total, returned and replaced parts, hidden and left-off lines, an agreed charge, a fee, a free comeback: 0 differences); the local route `POST /api/e2e/money-check { mode: "batch" }` keeps proving one-at-a-time = all-at-once. `src/lib/page-all.ts` holds `pageAll` (moved out of zoho-sync, re-exported there), `chunks` (150 ids per `in(...)`) and `pageAllIn`. THE list is `loadMoneyList(settings, { scope: "now" | "range" | "search" })` in `money-data.ts` (no 600 cap): job rows for open jobs with any payment, an approved quotation, a live proforma/tax invoice (deposit documents too), a waiting payment report or a Ready/pending_payment/in_delivery status; any job with an advance and no invoice; closed jobs whose live document still owes after its credit notes, or that were paid in full in the last `RECENT_PAID_DAYS` (30) days (`recentPaidOnly`: in Paid/Tax invoices/Released, in no tile, never on the dashboard; `RECENT_PAID_HINT` under the chips, `data-money-list-hint`); in the "now" scope only, any job with money collected today or a tax invoice issued today (a date range keeps to its dates); plus credit rows (`unallocatedOf`, one per customer, `href /customers/<id>#money`) and pending-charge rows (`loadChargeEntries`, `/pending-charges?open=`). The pure module `src/lib/money-list.ts` holds `MoneyRowData` (kind job/credit/charge, `key`, `href`, `total|null`, `paid`, `balance|null`, `pendingCheque`, `advance`, `paidToday`/`paidTodayCount`, `taxToday`), `MONEY_FILTERS` (12 chips incl. `advance`, `today`, `tax_today`, `credit`), `inFilter` (awaiting = every balance due on approved work, credit never; part = paid > 0 and balance > 0, advances included), `awaitingGroupOf` + `AWAITING_GROUPS` (due_now / to_send / wip_advance / wip_nothing), `moneyTilesOf` (the tiles FROM the rows), `collectedAt`/`countsAsCollected` (rule B: money on its received day, a cheque on its CLEARING day, like the Zoho reconciliation; compared as instants), `rowMatches`, `paidSoFarParts` ("credit note AED x off"), `chipCountOf` (every chip counts rows except "Tax invoices today", which counts invoices like its tile and its heading "2 tax invoices on 1 car"; the row shows every number in `data-money-tax-today-line`), `recentlyPaid`, and THE credit-note rule `creditTakenOff` (an issued credit note's total less what of it was refunded in money). `loadMoneyTiles(rows)` adds only the month (same rule, read straight) and the LPO/supplier-invoice counts; `MoneyTilesRow` links Awaiting → `?show=awaiting` ("N cars · AED X due now · AED Y work in progress"), Collected today → `?show=today` ("N payments · this month"), Tax invoices today → `?show=tax_today` (deposit tax invoices excluded), with `data-tile-amount`/`data-tile-count`. `/invoices`: tiles and chips from the SAME now-list; dates = history (`scope: "range"`, every job with a document in the range); a search adds older jobs (`searchMatches`: job or any document number, plate or VIN via `vehicleOrFilter`, customer name or mobile; the credit and charge rows of the customers and cars it found) and EVERY row it found is kept (`foundKeys`), only the other rows must `rowMatches`; the list heading sums it (`data-money-list-sum`); LiveRefresh on /invoices and the accounts dashboard never listens to `quotations` (the editor's lock heartbeat would re-render them every minute; approvals and declines write `jobs`). ONE `PaidSoFar` component (`data-paid-so-far`, `data-balance-left`) on `MoneyRow`, the job card's money box and the customer page; `MoneyRow` carries `data-money-row` (= key), `data-money-kind`, `data-money-total`, `data-money-paid`, `data-money-balance`, `data-money-today`, `data-money-today-count`, `data-money-tax-today`, "Today AED d" in green. `moneyState` takes an optional `advance { paid, left }` (no live document: chip "Advance AED X · AED Y to pay", amber; `advanceWords`), and a car that left with its document still owing reads "Left owing · AED X due" (amber) instead of a green "Released"; the job card and the money page pass it, and the money page's Paid so far / Balance due / cheque clearing before the proforma read `jobMoneyOf` (they counted uncleared cheques and missed deposit documents). Credit notes (review of 3 November): `invoiceBalance(inv, payments, credited)` returns `credited`; `creditsOnDocs(docIds)` in `invoice-data.ts` reads them (with `refund_amount_aed`); used by `jobMoneyFrom`/`jobMoneyMany` (`JobMoney.credited`), the list's closed-document check, the money page (`data-credit-note-off`), `/invoices/[id]`, `settleJob` and `recordPaymentCore`'s balance; the customer's invoice page/PDF and Pay-now links are unchanged. The accounts dashboard uses the same list without pending-charge rows (the owner's rule of 31 October) and without `recentPaidOnly` rows: Awaiting payment in the four groups (`data-awaiting-group`, `data-advances-link` to `?show=advance`) under a heading of the job rows' sum, one `data-pending-charges-line` ("Plus n pending charges (cars not in the workshop) · AED x", link to /pending-charges; `MoneyListTiles.awaitingChargeCount/awaitingChargeAed`, the tile says "N cars + n pending charges"), "Other money: paid, today, customer credit"; `depositsNotInvoiced` is gone (its rows are the advance rows; test-t's `data-deposit-not-invoiced` check is stale with the rest of test-t); test-charges' E check is back to "not on the dashboard" plus the one line. Lessons: `next dev` refuses `--env-file` in NODE_OPTIONS, so a scratch wrapper loads `.env.test.local` and starts `next dev -p 3101` without it; a fixed e2e id must stay hex (`…e9g1` is not a uuid); the stage seed has ONE car, so a test that needs several money situations inserts its own e9xx customers/cars/jobs/quotations (lines while the quotation is draft, then approved) and documents numbered `E2E-PRO-…`/`E2E-INV-…`, never in the real PRO-/INV- runs (`nextDocumentNumber` reads the highest `PRO-%`/`INV-%`).

Auth code optional, made-up codes flagged, filled from the bank report, 2 November 2026 (section 5, built 3 November; no migration; note `docs/2-nov-s5-note.md`; tests `npm run test:s5-authcode` 49 checks after the review (sections A-I, `ONLY=`), `tests/unit/auth-code.test.mjs`, `test:fees3` rewritten, `test:mtr` now runs through `tests/unit/register.mjs`). RCT-00040 (J-00969, AED 30,000 by link) carried "111111" because the box was required. THE rule is `src/lib/auth-code.ts` (pure): `FAKE_AUTH_CODES` (one fixed list: one character ×6, digit runs up/down, a few patterns; `authMissingFilter()` asks the database for exactly these + null), `isFakeAuthCode`, `realAuthCode` (null for empty, malformed or made up - read EVERYWHERE a code is shown, matched or printed), `authCodeMissing(p)` (card/link with no real code), `authMissingWhy`, `authCodeProblem` (only a wrong length is refused now, "leave it empty"), `authCodeNote` (amber words for a made-up code), `authConfirmWords` ("auth X" / "auth code missing (flagged)"), `AUTH_MISSING_WORDS`, `AUTH_OPTIONAL_HINT` "Optional – helps match the bank report". `AuthCodeInput` is optional by default (label "Auth code (optional)", `data-auth-optional-hint`, `data-auth-code-note`; `required` only in the Add box) in PaymentForm, RecordDeposit (refuses a wrong length before its question; `data-deposit-confirm-text` names the code) and the advisors' `PaymentReport` dialog. `recordPaymentCore` stores `realAuthCode` (a made-up code is stored as NULL, the history note says "auth code missing (111111 typed, not a real code)"), the duplicate rule is `authCodeTwin(code, method, exceptId)` and runs only for a real code. `checkReportInput` stores a made-up advisor code as null, `loadReports` and `PaymentForm prefill` read `realAuthCode`, `pickReportForReceipt` treats a made-up code as none. `addAuthCode(paymentId, code, actor, { kind: "hand" } | { kind: "report", reportId, fileName, line })` in payments.ts (owner/accounts, recorded or pending_owner card/link still missing, made up refused by hand, twin refused, compare-and-swap on the old value, explicit `audit_log` row with `auth_code_added/was/number/job_id/source/by` + `job_events` "RCT-…: auth code X added by hand|from the bank report <file>, line n by …"; nothing else moves), action `addAuthCodeAction` in `invoices/actions.ts`. ONE `src/components/AuthCodeMissing.tsx` (amber badge `data-auth-missing=<RCT>`, `data-auth-add`, `data-auth-add-form`, `data-auth-add-save`, `data-auth-add-error`, `data-auth-added`; calls the action directly, not as a form action, because React resets a form after its action and blanks the typed code) on the money page row, the invoice page row and `MoneyRow`'s strip (`data-money-auth-missing`, `canAddAuth` from the invoices page and AccountsDashboard). Money list: `MoneyRowData.authMissing` (`AuthMissingPayment`), chip `auth_missing` "Auth code missing" (counts rows; the heading adds "n payments without an auth code"); `loadMoneyList` reads the missing-code payments once (by job, by pending-charge invoice, by customer credit) and keeps a job on the list without dates until the code is added (`recentPaidOnly` = off the dashboard, in no tile). The receipt PDF prints only `realAuthCode`; card settlements show "auth missing" (`UnsettledPayment.codeMissing`, `data-settle-auth-missing`); the accounts export has an "Auth code" column ("missing"). Bank report (`settlement-report.ts`, now importing the pure auth rule): `MatchPayment.codeMissing/settledIn`, `LineMatch.status` matched / settled / suggested / unmatched with `fillCode`, `daysApart`; `suggestionStands(line, payment)` (a real line code, codeMissing, same amount to the fils, same method or none on the line, ±3 days) is THE rule the server re-checks; exactly one fit = suggested ("Suggested match: RCT-… · AED … · payment link · n days apart · its auth code is missing"), two or more = unmatched with them first; an exact match on a payment settled by hand = "Already settled in STL-… ✓". `reviewPaymentsFor` adds the settled payments within the report's days ±4 (`matchablePayments(null, settledBetween)` with the STL number); `fillAuthFromReport` + `confirmSuggestionAction` (one tap `data-report-confirm-suggested`, the box `data-report-suggested`, words above the button so it stays in sight at 800 px); a settled line's pick is `settled:<id>` (`data-report-settled`, handled, never settled twice); a missing-code payment picked by hand gets the line's code at Record (`data-report-will-fill`); `recordReport(reportId, input, actor)` returns `settledBefore` and `codesSaved` and stamps a report whose payments were all settled before as recorded with nothing new. RCT-00040 is flagged by the rule on deploy - no live data change. Lessons: unit tests that used 111111/222222 as "real" codes had to move to real-looking ones (4X7Q21, B0A60C); a test that waits for `message=` in the address after a second payment on the same page resolves at once (the first payment's message is still there) - wait for the row, then open the page afresh; two equal deposits by the same method within a minute are refused as a double tap, so tests vary the amounts. Review fixes (3 November 2026; test-s5-authcode sections G, H, I; note's "Review fixes"): suggestions are ONE TO ONE - `matchLines` works out before its loop which missing-code payments fit which lines whose code no payment carries (`fitsOf`/`linesOf`, so the line order never decides) and suggests only when the line fits exactly one payment AND that payment fits no other line ("RCT-… has its auth code missing and fits this line and line 2 alike: pick it by hand"); `fillAuthFromReport` re-runs `matchLines` and accepts Confirm only while that line is still `suggested` to that payment. A fitting payment settled by hand stays in the line's candidates and `ReportReview` offers it as `settled:<id>` ("· already settled in STL-…"); picked by hand the line shows `data-report-settled-pick` with the select still open (`settledAuto` = only an exact settled match or a confirmed suggestion hides it), and `recordReport` accepts a settled pick whose code is missing when `suggestionStands`, counting it as handled and saving the bank's code (fills now also run in the nothing-new branch; `carried(code)` checks open AND settled payments). `reviewPaymentsFor` of a RECORDED report = its own settlements' payments + `settledAround` minus those, never the open list (its "already settled" lines no longer read "Not ours"). Dates: `reportDate` returns only real calendar days (`dayOf`; d/m/y, or m/d/y when the "month" is above 12), Excel is read with `REPORT_XLSX_READ`/`REPORT_SHEET_ROWS` (cellDates + dateNF yyyy-mm-dd, shared with test:mtr) because a real date cell printed "10/24/26" became "2026-24-10" and `settledAround`'s `addDay` threw "Invalid time value" (now `isDay` filters first; `ReportReview`'s `day()` shows an impossible stored date as it is). The Add box: `authCodeRefusal(method, code)` is THE red refusal of a made-up code (server `addAuthCode` by hand and `AuthCodeInput required`, `data-auth-code-refused`; no amber note there; Save off). `UnallocatedList` flags card/link credit (`AuthCodeMissing`, or `data-auth-shown` "Auth X" once added). On money list rows (`MoneyRow`'s strip, `answerWindow`) the answer also opens `AuthAddedWindow` (module store in AuthCodeMissing.tsx, mounted once in the (app) layout for recordPayments and not viewing as; `data-auth-added-window`, `data-auth-added-message`, `data-auth-added-ok`), because the save takes the strip, and under the chip the row, away; `popup-check.mjs` opens it ("Auth code saved (money list)"). Lesson: a test settlement must never be numbered "STL-…" - `nextNumber` reads the highest STL- number, so "STL-E5…" made every later settlement "STL-00001" (duplicate twice = "Couldn't save"); test settlements are "E2E-STL-…".

Section 8 of 2 November 2026, built and review-fixed 3 November (migration `20261203150000_quote_charge_costs.sql`, applied on live 3 November 2026; note `docs/2-nov-s8-note.md`; tests `tests/unit/charge-items.test.mjs` (72 checks) in `npm run test:unit`, and `npm run test:s8-charges`).

Cause: J-00969's Charges section said "No recovery, fuel or other charges" because `ChargesCard` read only `charge_lines`, and Q-00039's recovery was a quotation line.

`src/lib/charge-items.ts` (pure) is THE list and THE cost rule.
- `chargeItems({ rows, costRows, versions, lines, owners })` lists every charge once:
  - job-card rows;
  - each quotation number's charge lines from `shownVersions` (newest approved, plus lines new on the version being prepared), traced to their first version by `rootLineOf` through `revised_from_line_id`.
  - A pushed job-card charge is one item, with `whereWords` ("on Q-00039 · approved").
  - `isQuoteCharge` leaves out Consumables, the Inspection fee line, the bank charge, a recovery the customer arranges and unchosen lines.
  - Internal lines read "Internal – not charged".
- Each item has:
  - `billed`: what "charged" counts. True for a job-card charge on no quotation; otherwise only once its quotation version is approved (`notBilledWords`).
  - `notCharged`: taken off its quotation, or the quotation declined or cancelled. Charge 0, "Not charged", never asks 8.2.
  - `who` ('ours' | 'company' | 'any', from `whoOfLine`/`whoOfRow`/`isOurProvider`).
  - `ownerKnows` ('approved' = the line's below-cost stamp covers it, 'told' = the save sent the notice, 'owner' = he entered it; `belowCostWords`). The screen never says "the owner is told" when nobody told him.
- `chargeCostsOnce(items, trips, { open })`:
  - job-card charges count at once;
  - quotation charges count once approved, using the bill else the estimate;
  - recovery: each trip (with `company`, null = our truck) goes to the charges of whoever did it (our trucks, the same company by `sameName`, an unnamed company, else 'any'). A group with trips costs its priced trips plus the per-trip estimate for trips still to price (all the trips it names while `open`; only the recorded trips once the job is closed, so it never stays provisional for good). A group with no trip keeps its own cost. A trip of nobody counts on its own once priced.
  - returns `tripKeys` (items costed from trips), `standIn`/`standInTrips`, `fromCharges`, and `recoveryCostWords` ("Recovery cost AED 720.00: AED 460.00 from 2 priced trips + AED 260.00 estimated for 1 trip still to price").
- `readOnlyWords(status)`: an approved quotation changes only with a revision the owner allows.
- `recoveryAlready` / `recoveryWords` / `recoveryAlreadyWords` give 8.2.
- `chargeLineCosts` gives the actual cost per shown line.

In `charges.ts`:
- `loadChargeItemsMany` / `loadJobChargeItems` return `JobCharges` {items, trips, costs, costReady, lineIds = every version's line of a listed charge}. They read the trips' company, `jobs.is_open`, the owners' ids and the charge lines' below-cost stamps.
- `recoveryElsewhere(jobId, exceptNumber)`.
- `saveQuoteChargeCost` writes the one `charge_lines` row with `origin = 'quotation'`, keyed to the line's FIRST version (`quotation_line_id`; unique index `charge_lines_quote_cost_one`; the locked quotation line keeps its estimate). It is refused only for a recovery in `costs.tripKeys`.
- `loadCharges` skips origin 'quotation' rows.
- `quoteChargeCostsReady()` keeps the cost box dormant.
- The bill is a `supplier_invoice` job file with `ref_id` = the first-version line id.

8.2 (a second recovery):
- `addCharges` refuses a recovery while one is on the car unless `ChargeInput.different_trip` (ChargeLinesEditor `recoveryAlready`, `data-charge-recovery-warning`/`-different`, a required tick).
- The save route refuses a plain recovery `addLine` unless `different_trip` (editor `EditorSettings.recoveryElsewhere`, `data-recovery-elsewhere`, `data-recovery-different`).
- `startQuotation` decides the automatic recovery line AFTER `quotationsFromCarriedEstimates`.
- `leaveOutRepeatedRecovery` (quotes/actions.ts) leaves an estimate's recovery off a quotation of a car that already has one. It logs `recovery_not_copied`, shown by the editor through `EditorSettings.recoveryLeftOut` (`data-recovery-left-out`).

Profit:
- `jobProfits` never counts a charge's invoice line again (by `quotation_line_id`, or for check-in charges by description), nor a hidden charge line.
- `recoveryCost`/`otherCost` come from the rule; a declined car keeps its charges' costs.
- `approvedNotInvoicedProfit` and `approvedQuoteProfits` apply `chargeAdjustment` after `quoteTotals`, which is unchanged.

The card:
- `ChargesCard` takes items/costs and `mayOpenQuote` (= `seesQuote`: the quotation's number is a link only for owner and advisors, words for accounts).
- `QuoteChargeCostForm` is an ActionForm.
- Hooks: `data-charge-source`, `data-charge-where`, `data-charge-read-only=<status>`, `data-charge-open-quote`/`data-charge-quote-number`, `data-charge-below-cost=<ownerKnows|none>`, `data-charge-not-billed`, `data-charge-cost-open/-input/-save`, `data-charges-trips`.
- It hides trip prices from advisors.
- Charge action answers come back as `charge_message`/`charge_error` and show inside the section.

Lessons:
- A `node -e "…"` patch in bash loses backticks (template literals): patch JSX template strings with the Edit tool.
- `String.replace` on a short JSX anchor like `{!disabled ? (` hits the FIRST block in a big file, so anchor on a line unique to the target block.
- A trip must be matched to the charge it IS (by who did it), never by count alone: an own-truck delivery would otherwise erase an outside tow-in's bill.

Removing an ordered part, 3 November 2026 (2 Nov list, section 7; migration `20261203140000_part_endings.sql`, applied on live 3 November 2026; note `docs/2-nov-s7-note.md`; tests `tests/unit/part-endings.test.mjs` in `npm run test:unit` and `npm run test:s7-remove`). Cause of J-00969 (grill on LPO-00037-1): the Parts table's Return to supplier could never succeed (setDisposition wanted a credit note the window never asked for), the window closed before the answer and partNotNeeded had already half-saved, and no step touched order status, the LPO line/total, final cost or the quotation. THE rule is pure `src/lib/part-endings.ts` (`endingsFor` = Cancelled with the supplier only when nothing arrived, Return and Keep in stock only when delivered, our shelf = Keep in stock only, refused with the technician or while exchanging; `isExchanging` = exchange_status needed/ordered ONLY: a part replaced by another supplier's part takes the endings; `endingOf` reads `disposition` 'cancelled'/'return_pending'/'returned'/'stock' and, before the migration, disposition NULL + disposition_at set (return_status 'to_return' = return pending); `storedDisposition`, `stateOfChoice`, `endingWords` "Removed – … by …", `LINE_ENDING_LABEL`, `poAfterEnding` (no line left = cancelled; charges leave a never-ordered LPO, an ordered one keeps them and its chargeNote names "Waived – remove"), `supplierMessage` (with `notDelivered`: a part only partly delivered asks the supplier to cancel the rest whatever its ending), `notDeliveredQty`, `returnCreditOf` (THE credit of a return: typed credit note amount → the invoice price on its LPO line → the LPO price → the part's cost; used by the Zoho vendor credit and "to be credited"), `daysWaiting`). THE function is `endOrderedPart` in `src/lib/part-endings-server.ts` (`partEndingsReady()`): read and check first (the part, its LPO line and LPO, an active replacement, its invoice lines); claim the part (is_active false, removed_*, not_needed_*, disposition*, final_cost_aed 0, order_status none or received) with guards on its state; cancel or cut its LPO line; re-total the LPO (notes line, status, charges); a live proforma's line cost of the part to 0 (the tax invoice it becomes copies 0); Keep in stock through `addPartToStock` or a 'return' movement; any failed step puts the earlier ones back, `logAppError("end_part")` and answers SAVE_FAILED; afterwards (only logged) a job event, the vendorcredit enqueue for 'returned', a tax invoice already out = the owner's `owner_approval_needed` "Cost correction needed" (never changed from here), the quotation through `revisePartOnQuotation` in quote-versions.ts on the number that CHARGES the part (never another quotation of the job; the shared `revisionTargetOf` that `quoteTargetFor` also uses returns target/asked/tax/none: draft = line retired; sent = next version; approved = owner asked, or next version when already allowed; tax invoice out = owner told, advisor and Parts get the credit-note words, never "revise"; skipped when an active replacement covers the line), `finishPartsPlanning` and the managers' notice (href `/jobs/<id>/work`: they cannot open Parts). Also `recordCreditNote`, `creditNotesAwaited`, `endedLinesOf`, `cancelledSupplierLpos`. Screens: PartsTable `EndDialog` (`data-end-dialog`, `data-end-option` + `data-allowed`, `data-end-why`, `data-end-save`, `data-end-error`, `data-end-done`, `data-end-message`, `data-end-supplier-copy`; it stays open while saving), rows `ended`/`creditAwaited`/`endings` (`data-part-ended`; `data-credit-awaited` is a tag, "Record the credit note" is in the ⋯ menu; no Put back), action `endPartFromParts`; `EndingForm endings` (`data-ending-form`, `data-ending-choice`, `data-ending-save`) and `CreditNoteForm` (`data-credit-note-number`) on Returns and leftovers (`data-credit-notes-awaited`, `data-credit-note-awaited`, anchors `#cn-<id>`) and the handover page: `dispositionAction`/`creditNoteAction` RETURN an `EndingAnswer` (no redirect), the forms submit through `useEndingSave` (error inside the card `data-ending-error`, ReleaseFormLock + WakeSubmit so the next tap works) and a saved ending opens the page's `EndingResultHost` window (`data-ending-result`, `-message`, `-copy`, `-close`) because the card leaves the list; `SupplierLpoCard po.ended` (`data-lpo-line-ended`, `data-lpo-line-cancelled`, `data-lpo-total`, `data-lpo-returns`, status cancelled) and the Parts page's `data-cancelled-lpos`; `removeSupplierCharge` takes a charge off an LPO left with no part line (not invoiced, paid or in Zoho) and cancels an LPO left with nothing: the strip's "Waived – remove" (`ChargeRow.waivable`, `data-charge-waive`, `data-charge-message`). Rules elsewhere:
- the invoice draft no longer drops a line whose part is 'returned' (only a customer-approved revision takes a line off) and reads ended parts for their 0 cost;
- `requestPartsGone`/`withdrawStaleRevisions` never treat a part the approved quotation still charges as "nothing left";
- the supplier-invoice price helpers (`applyInvoicePrices`, `decideInvoiceCheck`) and `receivePurchaseOrder` skip ended parts;
- `putBackPart`, `autoSavePartRow` and `replaceRefusedPart` refuse them;
- `partsWithNoHome` includes fit_pending (the owner's Approve was unreachable);
- `recordStockMovement` and `addPartToStock` check their writes;
- the Zoho backfill/go-live replay finds returned parts by disposition alone (an ending makes them inactive).
Lessons:
- a test forces a failed save with a temporary trigger on one fixed row through the e2e sql route (revoke execute from public, drop it in finally);
- the tidy triggers sentence-case part descriptions and title-case supplier names, so tests match names case-insensitively;
- a save from a list card that the success removes needs a page-level window (a module store read with useSyncExternalStore), and its errors stay in the card;
- FormGuard dims the tapped submit button for five seconds, and ReleaseFormLock clears only the lock, so an answer shown in place must also wake the button;
- a window that stays open after its save while its own row greys out (`opacity-60`) must be portalled to `document.body` (`EndDialog` is): inside the row it turned see-through, the rows below painted over it and Close could not be tapped (found by test-s7 C at integration);
- `app_errors`' time column is `at`, not `created_at`; the pop-up check opens the ending's result window ("Ending saved (Returns and leftovers)", parts `…e79x`).

Sections 9 and 10 of 2 November 2026 (built 3 November, review fixes the same day; migrations `20261203160000_part_units.sql` and `20261203170000_estimate_pricing_done.sql`, applied on live 3 November 2026, read through `partUnitsReady`/`estimatePricingReady`; note `docs/2-nov-s9-10-note.md`; tests `npm run test:s9-qty|test:s10-pricing|test:s9-s10` plus unit tests `part-units` and `pricing-done`).

E-00058: the estimate desk had its own form with "×1" as plain text, and there was no unit anywhere. ONE parts form now: `PartsTable` takes `estimate={id, number, preparing}` and its `Row` saves through `saveEstimatePartRow` (estimate-actions.ts maps RowValues onto the line; priced_at only when `rowState` is priced). The desk-only extras are `EstimateLineExtras` (Price is right ✓ sets the row's `advisor_price` to "checked" at once, `markChecked`; tell the advisor). `loadEstimateDesk`/`estimateBarMode`/`estimateAdvisors`/`estimateSentStamps` live in estimate-parts-data.ts.

Units: pure `src/lib/part-units.ts`:
- `cleanUnit`, `defaultUnitFor`, `checkQuantity`, `quantityChangeWords` ("Advisor asked 1, Parts changed to 8 L"), `qtyWithUnit`/`unitSuffix`/`perUnitWords`/`perUnitMark`, `partKindOf`/`lastQuantity`/`qtyMemoryWords`.
- Review additions: `requestUnitOf(label, stored)` is THE request unit (a stored "pc" never hides litres; used by `valuesOfRequest`, `autoSavePartRow`, `ensurePartRequests` and the page's askedUnit). `stepQuantity` makes − and + step from the number (6.5 → 7.5 L). `askerOfRequest` is THE asker: an inspection request or `additional:` work is the technician's, whoever approved it; otherwise the maker, else `added_by_role`. It is used by the job parts page and by `askerOfPart`.

Rows and documents:
- ONE `QtyUnitBox`.
- `TableRow.qtyChangedBy/savedQty` name who changed a quantity on the row.
- `part_items.unit` and `quotation_lines.unit/asked_quantity/quantity_changed_*/list_price_aed/supplier_discount_percent` are read through `attachPartLineExtras`, `pricingPartSelect`, `attachInvoiceUnits`, `loadPurchaseOrder` and `partUnitsOf` (parts-data.ts, for the LPO cards' "× 8 L · AED 165.00/L" and their Copy text).
- `partLineName(…, {quantity, unit})` and `qtyText(q, hours, unit)` print "· 8 L" and "8 L".

The quantity rule on the server is `src/lib/part-quantity.ts` (`logPartQuantity`, `setJobPartQuantity`, which is lenient before the migration).
- The save route refuses a quantity, or a unit-only change, that breaks `checkQuantity`.
