- `autoSavePartRow` checks the quantity against the unit the part will really have: typed, saved, its request's or its name's.
- `src/lib/part-memory.ts` `lastQuantitiesFor` reads in 150-id pieces and pages.
- A litre part prints one sticker.
- `estimateLineRows` (gate-in) carries a half-typed estimate price as an unfinished part with no priced_at and no quotation line.

Section 10: pure `src/lib/pricing-done.ts`:
- `pricingProgress` gives "3 of 4 priced" (`count` includes removed lines).
- `pricingDoneTitle(ref, p, again, auto)`: All parts priced / Parts priced n of m / Prices updated / Parts changes / No parts left / Parts not available.
- `pricingDoneBody` (with `auto`).
- `awaitingSend` = `holdPerPartNotices`: before approval until the first send, or once reopened; past approval only when reopened. A car past approval never sent holds nothing.
- `jobPricingTodo`/`pricingTodoOf`: to_price while a request is open or a part is unpriced, also after a send ("Send what you have" leaves those lines the desk's) and on approved cars. to_send while `awaitingSend` and (a part priced since the send, or `parts_pricing_reopened_at`).
- `estimateDeskTodo`/`estimateLineMoment`: per line by `estimatePartState`; with_desk is to price whatever was sent.

`parts_pricing_reopened_at` means "changes wait for a send":
- Edit sets it.
- `reopenPricingFor` sets it on a new part: a sent pricing, or any car past approval in the pricing phase; never in planning; returns the stamp.
- `holdForSend(job, by)` (parts-pricing.ts) is THE gate for every advisor per-part notice: not available, quantity, removed, split, changed after the quotation went out. It stamps the mark, so the car stays listed. /parts and the Parts dashboard also read jobs carrying the mark.

THE send is `src/lib/pricing-send.ts`:
- `sendJobPricing` is used by `pricingDone`. It folds `FOLDED_EVENTS`. It answers "Already sent … Nothing changed since." when sent and not reopened, and closes quietly after Edit with nothing changed.
- `releaseHeldPricing` sends what is still held, by itself ("Parts changes on J-…"), from `applyCustomerResponse` and cron step 28 (`releaseHeldPricingHourly`, report key `pricingReleased`).

The estimate send:
- `estimatePricingDone` stamps `quotations.parts_priced_at`. Until that column exists, the send is read from the "parts_pricing_done" quotation event (`estimateSentStamps`). It answers "Already sent…" when nothing changed.
- `loadEstimatesToPrice` reads open estimates page by page, then their lines through `pageAllIn`.

ONE `PricingDoneBar` (useActionState):
- Hooks: `data-pricing-bar/-progress/-done/-open-list/-open-line/-go/-go-next/-send-anyway/-sent/-sent-open/-locked/-edit/-editing/-jump-strip/-show-list`.
- The answer shows in every mode until Edit.
- Go to folds the list into one line and scrolls the row above the bar.

`/parts`, the Parts tile, Parts' Waiting (`pricingToSendItems`) and `loadQuoteSummaries` follow the send.

Lessons:
- A `.select()` built from a template loses its type, so cast it.
- A status-line chip must stay `h-5`, or test-f's same-height check fails.
- A stored column default of 'pc' hides the name-based unit, so a request's "pc" is read as "no answer".
- A rule that holds notifications must be the same rule that puts the car on a to-do list, or a held notice is lost.
- A send button whose stamp column may not exist yet needs another record of the send (the history event), or the button never shows its answer and sends twice.

Zoho on manual, 3 November 2026 (section 11; migration `20261203180000_zoho_manual_sync.sql`, applied on live 3 November 2026, note `docs/2-nov-s11-note.md`, tests `npm run test:s11-zoho-manual` + `tests/unit/zoho-sync-rules.test.mjs`). The setting `zoho_sync_mode` is 'manual' or 'automatic'; a missing row is MANUAL. `zohoBooksNow()` returns `auto`; `syncModeOf` reads the value. The five-minute cron calls `zohoCronTick` (`src/lib/zoho-manual.ts`). It drains only when automatic (`drainZohoOutbox(limit, { requireAuto: true })`; a switch to manual mid-run stops it). Otherwise it records "Manual: N items waiting for Sync to Zoho" (plus "· M waiting on something else"), so the job is never late. Both modes keep the read-only reconciliation (only where Zoho is configured). ONE row runner, `processOutboxRow` in zoho-sync.ts: the undo hold, the claim, the switch re-read, the handler, and the outcome written under the claim stamp. The outcome is posted, removed (`result.reversed`) or skipped (`result.skipped`) via `doneOutcome`, else failed or waiting. When Zoho's call allowance is used up (`isRateLimitError`), the row waits and the run stops. The runner serves the cron's drain and `runOutboxRows(ids, { deadlineMs, fake })`, which sends exactly the named rows in order (Retry and Sync to Zoho never sweep other rows).

Pure rules in `src/lib/zoho-sync-rules.ts`:
- words and order: `KIND_WORDS`/`kindWord`/`kindCount`, `resultWords` (it also reads removals: "Removed from Zoho: RCT-…"), `POSTING_ORDER`/`sortForPosting`;
- THE effect rule `rowEffect` (new / again = already in Zoho / removal = taken back out of Zoho / nothing = voided, cancelled or internal before it was sent) and `effectOfRow`;
- `buildPreview` with `PREVIEW_GROUPS`: the groups and Money in/out count FIRST POSTS only; `removals` (with its total), `again` and `nothing` are separate lists; held for undo, being sent, `RUN_CAP_TEST_ROWS` 120 on OFJ TEST;
- THE "a press can move it" rule `sendsOnPress` + `SENDS_ON_PRESS_FILTER`: never tried, or waiting only on Zoho's allowance or `ACCOUNTS_WAIT_WORDS`;
- `stuckWords`, `retryRefusal` (hand_only and manual rows), `reminderDue`/`reminderWords`;
- `reconDue` (moved here, re-exported by zoho-reconcile), `reconDaysDue` (`RECON_LOOKBACK_DAYS` 10, `RECON_DAYS_PER_RUN` 3), `reconRunWords`, `dayWaitsForSync`;
- `runWords`/`tallyItems` (posted, removed, skipped, failed, waiting), `mergeItems`.

ONE document lookup: `outboxDocs(rows, { billFigures })` in `src/lib/zoho-docs.ts`. It gives name, amount, effect and effectWords; a bill's amount is the `billErpFigures` gross; the outbox row's `zoho_id` means "already in Zoho" unless the document is voided.

`zoho-manual.ts` holds:
- `sendState`: the block words, and `testStub`;
- `loadSyncPreview` (selects `zoho_id`);
- `startSyncRun`: only previewed rows that still wait; one running run through a partial unique index;
- `runSyncChunk`: 45 s per chunk, ids filtered to the run's `outbox_ids`;
- `finishSyncRun`;
- `retryOneRow`: refuses with `retryRefusal`;
- `loadRecentRuns`/`loadRun`;
- `waitingCounts`: `total` = rows a press would send, plus `stuck`/`stuckWords`. It is THE number for the bell, the menu badge (layout.tsx counts with `SENDS_ON_PRESS_FILTER`), the button and Waiting on you;
- `zohoWaitingReminder`: housekeeping step 29; "told today" = a `zoho_waiting` notification since the Dubai day began; no bell when only stuck rows remain;
- `zohoWaitingExtra`: Waiting on you for accounts and the owner;
- `setSyncMode`: owner only, never viewing or acting; upsert with the label; an audit_log row; a `zoho_sync_mode` bell.

The reconciliation: `reconcileDueDays` runs after every Sync to Zoho and on every cron tick. It covers every day of the last ten that has no row, or whose row was checked before the day ended, and that no longer waits for Sync to Zoho. It goes oldest first, three per run, and stops at the first error. So a Saturday held back over a Sunday off is compared after Monday's sync. `firstUnsentAt` uses `SENDS_ON_PRESS_FILTER`. Accounts are read with `resolveZohoAccounts(..., { create: false })`.

Screens:
- `ZohoSyncButton`: hooks `data-zoho-sync-open`/`-now`, `-window`, `data-zoho-preview`, `-dest`, `-group` with `data-count`/`data-total`, `-row` with `data-zoho-preview-effect`, `data-zoho-preview-removals` (rows `data-zoho-preview-removal`), `data-zoho-preview-again`, `data-zoho-preview-nothing`, `-held`, `data-zoho-sync-confirm`, `-results`, and `data-zoho-sync-item` (posted / removed / skipped / failed / waiting) with `data-outbox-id`, `-retry`. It watches `useSearchParams` for `sync=1`, so each arrival opens it, including a bell tapped while already on /zoho. It drops the parameter with `history.replaceState(null, …)` one tick later (`setTimeout` 0): Next skips its router sync when given its own `__NA` state, and Next installs its replaceState patch in the app router's own effect, which on the first load runs AFTER the child's effect (a replaceState in the effect itself bypassed Next, the router kept `?sync=1` and a later bell tap on /zoho opened nothing; found by test-s11 at integration).
- `ZohoSyncModeCard` (`data-zoho-mode-card`, `data-zoho-mode-switch`) sits on /zoho and on Settings > Zoho Books.
- Last syncs (`data-zoho-run`) and `/zoho?run=<id>` (`data-zoho-run-view`, `data-zoho-run-now`). Retry is hidden with `data-zoho-retry-refused` where `retryRefusal` says so.
- The /zoho list filters in the database: Failed and Waiting show every row; All and Posted show the newest 400, and All also shows every red row. Chips count in the database (`data-zoho-rows-capped` when cut). Rows carry `id="z-<outbox id>"`.
- The page sets `maxDuration` 120.

`zoho_sync_runs` (with `removed`/`skipped` counts) is server-only, read through `syncRunsReady()` (applied on live 3 November 2026).

TEST stand-in: an outbox row with `zoho_result.e2e_stub` ('post' | 'fail' | 'fail_once' | 'wait' | 'skip' | 'reverse', plus `e2e_words`) is "sent" by `e2eFake()` only under `isTestEnvironment()`. e2e actions: `manual_cron`, `manual_reminder` ({ nowMs }), `manual_mode_as` ({ role, mode }).

Lessons:
- A manual run must never call `recordJobRun('zoho')`: it would hide a dead cron.
- `outboxDocs` reads a supplier credit's amount through section 7's `returnCreditOf` (the handler's own rule), so the preview never shows a returned part at the 0 cost an ending leaves on it.
- The live Zoho suites drained through the live cron, and on manual they no longer drain.
- A preview total must count only the money that FIRST reaches Zoho: reopened, voided and internal rows get their own lines.
- A count that drives a reminder must count only what the reminded action can clear.
- `window.history.replaceState(window.history.state, …)` leaves `useSearchParams` stale in Next 16.
- A child component's mount effect runs before Next's app router patches `history.replaceState`: a URL tidy-up on first load must wait a tick, or Next never hears of it.

Parts accounts, 3 November 2026 (section 12; migration `20261203190000_parts_accounts.sql` adds `suppliers.contact_name` + setting `supplier_credit_wait_days`, applied on live 3 November 2026, read through `supplierContactReady`; note `docs/2-nov-s12-note.md`; tests `tests/unit/parts-accounts.test.mjs` (170 checks) and `npm run test:s12-parts-accounts`). Page `/parts-accounts` (permission `viewPartsAccounts` = owner + accounts, roles.ts only; menu "Parts accounts"; every read through the admin client). THE rules are pure, in `src/lib/parts-accounts-rules.ts` (imports only pure modules: `format`, `part-endings`, `part-state`, `part-units` included):
- `partLines`: `partStep` is the furthest step, the customer's documents first. The four dots are each their OWN fact, never the ladder position (`partIsOrdered`, `partFullyHere`, `partIsFitted`, invoiced = a tax-invoice sale). `late` = bought, past the expected day, not all here, no ending, whatever document the part is on. The status words come from `lineWords`: plain dates through `formatDate`, plus "· not received yet / partly received / not ordered yet" for a part sold before it is here. `partEnding` gives the endings: refused, then section 7's four (below), then removed, then `partState`.
- Section 7's endings (integrated 3 November): `endingStateOf(p)` = section 7's `endingOf` (cancelled / return_pending / returned / stock, also the before-migration storage: disposition empty + disposition_at set). Section 7 writes every ending with `removed_at` set and the part INACTIVE, so `loadPartsBase` keeps inactive parts with an ending (and asks for `disposition_at.not.is.null` too) and `partEnding`/`partIsBought`/`returnStateOf` read the ending BEFORE "removed". Words are section 7's `ENDING_LABEL`. Every ending costs 0 on the line (`partCostCounts`; "Cancelled: nothing to pay", "Returned: credit due", "Moved to stock"); cancelled is never bought; returned and return_pending are not in Parts bought (`isBoughtLine`). Returns and credits: returned = on file (else waiting), return_pending = `waiting_credit` (red after `supplier_credit_wait_days`, "Record the credit note" links to `/parts/no-home#cn-<id>`), cancelled = nothing unless its LPO was paid before the cancellation (`cancelledAfterPayment` → `credit_due`, amber, in Problems as credit_missing and in the supplier's credits due), stock = not listed. `expectedCredit(p, line)` = section 7's `returnCreditOf` (typed credit note amount → invoice price on the part's LPO line → LPO price → the part's cost; a cancelled part counts what was ordered). `stale_invoice_cost` counts an ended part's cost as 0, so a part ended after its tax invoice stays red until the invoice is corrected. The credit note columns (`credit_note_number`, `credit_note_date`, `credit_note_amount_aed`) are read only behind `partEndingsReady()`.
- Units (section 9, integrated 3 November): `qtyWithUnit` is section 9's `qtyWithUnit` through its `cleanUnit` (one display rule); a part's unit is `part_items.unit` behind `partUnitsReady()`, else its request's stored unit.
- `partSupplierOf`: a part on a MAIN LPO still waiting ("2 suppliers") keeps its own supplier.
- `salesByPart` + `replacementChain` over `everyPart(base)` = parts + `linkParts` (originals read only to follow their replacement, never listed). A tax invoice line keeps the ORIGINAL part id.
- `lpoRows` uses `lpoStep` and `poStatus`; the invoice match comes from THE `groupInvoices` rows.
- `supplierBalances`.
- `returnStateOf` is THE one Returns reader (`waiting_credit`, `credit_due`, `credit_on_file`, `to_return` = scanned back with no ending, `no_ending` = bought with nothing decided). A never-bought part returns null. `creditNoteOf` is the ONE credit-note function: section 7's `creditNumber` wins, else the number typed on Returns and leftovers (`disposition_note` of a returned part; `typed`), else the scan.
- `partsProblems`: nine rules. Rule 1 `not_charged` never fires on a live proforma (a car released on credit is charged). The others: not_on_quote, no_supplier_invoice, price_differs, placeholder_cost (open cars from `pricesToFix`, gone cars from `settledCostProblem`), stale_invoice_cost, duplicate_invoice, credit_missing, supplier_no_trn.
- Free comebacks: an invoice with `warranty_credit_aed` sells its parts at 0 (`notCharged: "warranty"`).
- `isBoughtLine` / `isChargedLine` are THE rules of the totals AND the chips their boxes open (Parts bought → quick `bought`).
- Also there: `filterLines`/`filterLpos`/`filterInvoiceRows`/`filterBalances`/`filterReturns`/`filterProblems`, `searchHit` (also compares without spaces), `partsAccountsTotals`, `PA_TABS`/`PA_QUICK`/`PA_STATUS`.

The reads are in `src/lib/parts-accounts.ts`: `loadPartsBase` (cached per request, `pageAll`/`pageAllIn` everywhere, reads `warranty_credit_aed`, reads replaced originals into `linkParts`) and `partsAccountsView`.

Supplier invoices (`src/lib/supplier-invoice-rows.ts`):
- Paid LPO by LPO: `InvoiceShare.paidAt`; `InvoiceRow.partlyPaid`/`paidAmount`/`unpaidAmount` (with VAT, they add up to `total`); `unpaidShares(r)` = the LPOs still to pay.
- `unpaidBySupplier` counts `unpaidAmount` and is keyed by supplier id, with `oldest`. It is THE figure of the Owed box, Payments to suppliers and the suppliers list.
- `SI_FILTER_KEYS` / `invoiceFilterParams` / `invoiceFilterOf` are THE filters in an address, so the screen and both exports always agree.

The screen is ONE shared view, `src/app/(app)/supplier-invoices/SupplierInvoicesView.tsx`:
- `place` is supplier-invoices | parts-accounts (`siHref`).
- The actions read a whitelisted hidden `from` to come back to the tab.
- The answer of Mark checked / Query shows inside the opened invoice next to its buttons (`data-si-outcome`), at the top only when nothing is open; the pages no longer print it.
- One pay button per unpaid LPO (`data-si-pay`); paid/unpaid lines per LPO (`data-si-share-paid/-unpaid`); `data-si-partly-paid`.

`loadSupplierInvoices({ strict })`:
- strict throws: Parts accounts, the view (red notice), the suppliers page (red notice), both exports (503), the check/query actions ("Couldn't read … nothing was changed") and `supplierInvoicesToCheckCount`.
- The default logs with `logAppError('supplier-invoices.load')` and reads as none: the job card and Waiting on you.
- `notifyInvoiceReceived` never fails a finished save (try/catch + log).

The sheet is `supplierInvoiceSheet`. Export `/api/export/parts-accounts?tab=lines|lpos|invoices|payments|returns|problems|all`: on invoices it takes the tab's own filters; dates go in as Dubai days through `dubaiDay`.

The LPO page's pay card (`id="paid"`) shows for every supplier LPO, a stock order without a car included (`data-stock-order-pay`).

Suppliers: `src/lib/supplier-rules.ts`:
- `supplierNeedsTrn` = THE no-TRN rule (15 digits), used by the suppliers page, the LPO page and Parts accounts.
- `supplierSearchHit`: phone through `nationalNumber` (00 / 971 / trunk 0 off, landlines and mobiles both ways), TRN by its digits.
- `/parts/suppliers?q=&filter=no_trn|unpaid` with `data-supplier-unpaid` "Unpaid AED x (n)".

Hooks: `data-pa-totals/-tile/-tab/-filters/-quick/-list/-row/-state/-problem/-severity/-export/-export-all/-pay/-partly-paid/-record-credit`.

Lessons:
- `part_items.supplier` is text, so a supplier filter matches by id OR by name, and a main LPO's "N suppliers" name must never stand for the part's supplier.
- A heavy page should not live-refresh on `part_items` (the Parts table autosaves).
- A box's figure and the list it opens must come from one shared predicate.
- A supplier invoice can be split over LPOs that are paid one by one, so "paid" is per LPO, never per invoice.
- A section built before another section's endings reached main read every ended part as "Removed" and lost it (the endings write the part inactive with removed_at): a list of parts must ask the ONE ending rule before `removed_at` and `is_active`.

Document scan + supplier invoices by accounts only, 4 November 2026 (no migration; note `docs/scan-invoice-4-nov-note.md`; tests `npm run test:scaninvoice` 18 checks, units `doc-scan` + `invoice-facts` in `npm run test:unit`). THE scanner is `src/lib/doc-scan.ts` (pure, no dependencies: `detectQuad` Otsu threshold -> largest bright area -> extreme-point corners, `warp` projective straighten, `enhance` bw adaptive threshold / grey contrast stretch / colour, `pagesToPdf` hand-written PDF with JPEG pages) behind `src/components/DocumentScanner.tsx` (CameraCapture, "Paper found - drag a corner", four 44px corner handles, several pages into ONE PDF; a PC with no touch screen falls back to the file picker, a picked PDF passes through), a "Scan document" button in the ONE shared uploader (`JobFileUpload` prop `scan`, default on, and `QueuedProofUpload`); InspectionMedia keeps its own flow. Supplier invoices are entered by ACCOUNTS only: the upload door refuses a parts `supplier_invoice` file (`job-file.ts` kindAllowed, 403 proven), `supplierInvoice`/`enterInvoiceFromList` are role-gated, the LPO page's form and the files' X are canPay-only (Parts see "Invoice with accounts: keep the paper ready"), an accounts query asks Parts to check the PAPER (they cannot correct the entry). Receiving never asks for the invoice: `ReceivePanel` has no box (`data-receive-invoice-note`), `receiveTicked`/`receivePurchaseOrder` set to_follow by themselves and tell accounts at once ("Parts received from <supplier> for <job> · <plate> - collect the supplier's invoice from Parts and scan it", href `/supplier-invoices?open=follow:<poId>`), the received line reads "Received · invoice with accounts" (`data-received-words`), accounts' Waiting carries `sicollect:` rows and cron step 24's daily reminder goes to accounts. Accounts enter on the Supplier invoices page (`ScanEnterInvoice` on a waiting row: the ONE box with prefill, "Read the scan" -> `readInvoiceScanAction` -> pure `invoiceFacts`/`invoiceDateIso` in `price-import.ts` for text PDFs (number, date, gross, discount, before VAT, VAT, net, "adds up" check; a 15-digit TRN is never the number) or `ocrInvoiceFacts` in `import-ocr.ts` for camera scans (ANTHROPIC_API_KEY-gated), `LinePrices` per-line invoice prices, remark -> LPO notes + job event) or on the LPO page (`SupplierInvoiceForm`); a replacement file next to an old Parts upload is logged by the register route. Suites reworked to the flow: sim a14/a14b, test-p section 2, test-price-changes, test-invoice2, test-supplierinvoice (the ONE-box mash/future-date/5%-tick rules now proven as accounts on the LPO page). Lessons: a test's fake tablet camera is chromium's `--use-file-for-fake-video-capture` with a hand-written y4m (YUV4MPEG2), and hasTouch makes `(pointer: coarse)` true so the scanner opens the camera; a hand-written Helvetica text PDF (BT/Tj) is enough for mupdf's structured text and proves the text reader without a fixture binary.

Money from the estimate, Part 1, 4 November 2026 (the owner's urgent task, case E-00061/LARS; migration `20261204090000_estimate_money.sql` WAITING FOR APPLY, backup `backups/2026-11-04T11-08-18`; note `docs/estimate-money-4-nov-note.md`; tests `npm run test:estmoney1` 31 checks + unit `estimate-money` 21). A company pays BEFORE the car arrives: THE rules are pure `src/lib/estimate-money-rules.ts` (BillTo + `cleanBillTo`/`billToProblem` 15-digit TRN/`billToWords`/`billToFromCustomer`, `partialOf` % or AED, `advanceSplit` VAT taken OUT landing exactly, `advanceWordsOf`, `estimateBadge` prepaid/part_paid/proforma_unpaid, `paidOfWords`, `EM_FILTERS`, `validUntilFrom`) and server `src/lib/estimate-money.ts` (`createEstimateProforma` — complete = the estimate's own lines through THE draft rule `draftFrom({job:null, quotes:[estimate], lines, parts:[], charges:[]})`, partial = ONE advance line; ONE open proforma per estimate; `invoices.estimate_id` is a PLAIN uuid — a second FK to quotations would break every unhinted embed; bill_to + valid_until stored on the invoice, bill_to kept on the estimate; `voidEstimateProforma` unpaid only, owner+accounts, logged; `convertEstimateProformaIfPaid` — complete → the full tax invoice queued for Zoho, partial → the ADVANCE tax invoice with `deposit_for_quotation_id = estimateId` so Zoho posts the MONEY as an advance and never the invoice, and the job's final proforma folds it like every deposit invoice: NOTHING twice; `afterEstimatePayment` from recordPaymentCore's job-less branch AND the cheque-clear action (which now re-checks pending charges too); `estimateMoneyMany` counts the whole version FAMILY by number; `loadEstimateMoneyRows({customerId})`). Wiring: paymentChoices offers the proforma ("Estimate E-x · car not here yet"), money list kind "estimate" rows (`estimate:<id>`, awaitingGroup due_now, MoneyRow's label), customer panel `estimateMoney` lines, /estimates money badges + `?money=` filter chips (`data-em-filter`), the estimate page's `EstimateMoneyCard` (`data-em-card/-mode/-partial-kind/-partial-value/-billto-*/-issue/-send/-void/-badge/-paid/-tax/-proforma/-error`) with `estimates/money-actions.ts` (create returns the filled WhatsApp message from the new seeded template `whatsapp_estimate_proforma_template` + short link; markSent stamps sent_by/at + event), the invoice PDF + customer page print the bill-to block, "Reference <E-number>", "Valid until" and the not-a-tax-invoice words (`InvoiceBundle.estimate/billTo/validUntil`, ready-gated merge in loadInvoice), and the JOB-LESS RECEIPT FIX: paid-to-date from the INVOICE's own payments when there is no job, the car from the invoice snapshot, recordPaymentCore stamps vehicle_id on invoice payments. `payment_requests`/`payment_reports` carry `estimate_id` (job optional) for the next push (advisor link + payment report on estimates); accounts gained `viewEstimates` (read-only page — loadQuotation is admin-client, so RLS needs no change). Lessons: the e2e sql route swallowed a MISSING PARAMETER (five params for six placeholders — the insert made no row and the suite chased a phantom server bug; count placeholders first); a SendLinkDialog that opens a beat after the action means a blind Escape fires too early — wait for `[data-send-message]`, then Escape, then wait for it to go.

Money from the estimate, Part 1b, 4 November 2026 (same migration; test-estmoney1 grown to 45 checks, sections E and F). The advisor's PAYMENT LINK on an estimate: `ensurePaymentRequest` takes `estimateId` (one live request per estimate+kind; `payment_requests.job_id` nullable, `estimate_id` plain uuid) and `payPageData` reads a job-less request's people from the estimate, counting a PARTIAL link paid from the payments on its proforma AND the tax invoice it became since the send; `prepareEstimatePayLink`/`markEstimatePayLinkSent` in estimates/money-actions.ts (open proforma required, gateway link validated, the filled whatsapp_payment_request_template returned whole; a /p/ page shortens under the generic /m/ prefix - only GATEWAY links are never shortened). The advisor's PAYMENT REPORT on an estimate: upload target `estimate_report` (SendTarget + UploadTarget + UPLOAD_TARGETS; slip at `estimate-reports/<estimateId>/<reportId>/<fileId>`, register writes NO row - createEstimateReport lists the folder), `QueuedProofUpload`/`useQueuedProof` gained `extraFields` (the per-file reportId), `createPaymentReport` takes `estimateId` XOR jobId and branches to `createEstimateReport` (same-key, resend-after-query, second-ask, quotation_events, bell href `/estimates/<id>?pay-report=<id>` via `estimateReportHref`); `loadReports` reads `estimate_id` ready-gated WITHOUT an embed (plain uuid: `estimate:quotations(number)` killed the whole select - the numbers are read apart) and an estimate report's jobNumber IS its E-number so `reportLine` stays the owner's wording; `closeReportWithPayment`/`afterReceiptForReports`/`reportToRecord`/`reportWaitingItems` handle job null (recordPaymentCore's job-less invoice branch matches reports via `estimateOfInvoice`); the window: `loadPaymentChoices` filters a report's targets to the estimate's proforma and returns `reportTargetKeys` - RecordPaymentHost keys prefill/fixedKey/initialProofs on them (`onReportTarget`), never on `target.jobId === report.jobId` (null !== "" silently killed every estimate prefill); PaymentForm's JOB-LESS proof branch accepts the report's slip as THE proof (`required={proofs.length === 0}`, `data-proof-from-report`) instead of demanding a second file. PaymentReportButton/ReportDialog take `estimateId` (the slip through QueuedProofUpload, Send gated on the slip ARRIVED). Lessons: an OR'd wait (`[data-pay-targets], [data-record-payment-error]`) passes on the error node - read the window's words before trusting the next step; textContent never shows input VALUES, so "the form is empty" needs the DOM, not the text dump.

Money from the estimate, Part 2, 4 November 2026 (same migration; test-estmoney1 at 54 checks, section G): THE CAR ARRIVES. `moveEstimateMoneyToJob(estimateIds, jobId, by)` in estimate-money.ts (idempotent: invoices of the carried FAMILIES with job_id null get the job, then their payments; returns the history words with what moved and the paid sum) is called by createGateIn right after the carried stamping (try/catch + logAppError: the gate-in never fails over money). The treaty that makes "nothing twice" automatic: a PARTIAL estimate proforma carries `deposit_for_quotation_id = estimateId` FROM BIRTH (like bodyshop deposit proformas) and `convertEstimateProformaIfPaid` stamps its payments' `deposit_invoice_number`/`deposit_quotation_id` - so on the job, ensureProforma's existing deposit fold closes the advance documents into the final proforma ("Deposit invoiced (INV-x)"), a COMPLETE pre-paid tax invoice simply IS the job's live tax invoice (ensureProforma returns it, never rebuilds), and syncInvoiceRow applies the moved payments (now job advances with zoho ids) to the final invoice. Calendar: the grid chip `data-appt-prepaid` (Ctx.prepaid map from loadEstimateMoneyRows) and the booking page's `data-booking-prepaid` line name the estimates and say the money follows the car. Regressions: the one-target report window and the job flows stayed green (test-s3-payreport 76, test-payanytime 75, test-estparts, test-addest).

Money from the estimate, Part 3, 4 November 2026 (migration `20261204120000_estimate_money2.sql` WAITING FOR APPLY with #1; test-estmoney1 at 77 checks, sections H-K): CHANGES, CANCELLATIONS, REFUNDS. estimateMoneyMany gained `over` (paid − credited − total when > 0), `credited` (the family's issued CNs) and `combinedOn` (an open proforma on ANOTHER estimate whose `covers_estimate_ids` overlap this family, read with `.overlaps()`; the BASE's total becomes the combined proforma's total). `createEstimateProforma` takes `combineIds` (accepted additional estimates of the same customer+car, none with its own proforma/invoice): ONE proforma through draftFrom with every estimate as a quote, `covers_estimate_ids` = the siblings' family ids, events on each. `createEstimateCreditNote(estimateId, amountIncl, reason, …)` — the parent is the newest live FULL tax invoice when one exists (posted: parent+CN enqueued like issueCreditNote) else the advance invoice (never in Zoho: `flagZohoAttention("creditnote", cnId, words, {handOnly})` makes the row red-by-hand FROM BIRTH, and a later cnrefund fails with the phase-3 parentClosedByHand words instead of waiting). `settleEstimateOver` (owner; keep credit | refund; reason on the CN; blocked by a pending cheque) and `cancelEstimateWithMoney` (owner; optional FEE stays invoiced, CN = invoiced − credited − fee; open proforma voided first and refused if it somehow holds unconverted money; refused while combinedOn — void the combined proforma first; then cancelled + accounts told). The plain `cancelEstimate` refuses with directions while family money exists. `estimateExpiryHold` in cron section 6: an estimate past valid_until that HOLDS money is never expired — one `expiry_hold` quotation_event + bells to the leads and the owner, never twice. Card UI: `data-em-over(/-reason/-credit/-refund)`, `data-em-combined-on`, `data-em-combine(-box)`, `data-em-cancel(-open/-reason/-fee/-keep/-save)`, `data-em-done` + `data-em-cn-open`; the refund itself is the EXISTING CN-refund form (`data-cn-refund`, confirmMoney "cn-refund"). Revision-UP needs nothing new: the card measures against the newest accepted version and the top-up is just another partial proforma.

Money from the estimate, Part 4, 4 November 2026 (no new migration; test-estmoney1 at 89 checks, section L — THE TASK IS COMPLETE): ORDER PARTS NOW before the car arrives. `orderEstimatePartsNow` in estimate-money.ts (advisors+owner; accepted estimate, priced part lines required): a HELD job once per family (status 'booked', is_open false, stage 'quote', estimate_id — the bodyshop-booking shape; its event says "opened as a HELD job"), the parts through the EXPORTED `estimateLineRows` (quotes/actions.ts — idempotent by client_key `estimate:<lineId>`, so Start quotation later never doubles; its quotationId parameter only lands in the RETURNED rows, so a discarded call with a placeholder id gives just the part side-effects), then `order_status 'none' → 'to_order'` for priced to-order parts (the ESTIMATE's acceptance is the approval — toOrder counts nothing else), then `createMainLpo` (its closed-job guard now lets a BOOKED job through) + the no-advance owner bell ("LPO to approve, NO ADVANCE"). The approval rides the deposit-override rails: the LPO page's depositShort gains the booked-estimate branch ("NO ADVANCE received on E-x (AED 0.00 of … paid) – the car is not here yet") and decidePurchaseOrder a parallel `noAdvance` gate (non-owner refused; the owner's reason logged as "No advance received: …" in deposit_override_reason). GATE-IN OPENS THE HELD JOB: the new page's `used` filter and createGateIn's `taken` filter skip BOOKED jobs (the estimate stays ticked), `reuse = booked ?? heldJob` updates that job (department from the form, estimate_id kept via the carried estimate or the held job's own when unticked), so ONE J-number carries the parts, LPOs and money. UI `OrderPartsNow.tsx` (`data-order-parts-now/-done/-error/-open`). Lessons: `loadJobLpos().toOrder` counts order_status 'to_order' ONLY — a part created outside applyCustomerResponse must be stamped or "Nothing to order"; a status write AFTER a returned sub-step (the CN before the cancel) races a test that reads on the sub-step's until — wait on the LAST write.

Resend the vehicle check-in link anytime, 4 November 2026 night (queue 3; migration `20261204150000_checkin_copy_template.sql` = one settings row, WAITING FOR APPLY with backup `backups/2026-11-04T19-40-57`; note `docs/resend-checkin-4-nov-note.md`; test `npm run test:resendcheckin` 19 checks). Permission `sendCheckInLink` (owner, service_advisor, gate_in) guards creating/marking-sent check-in links in `sendApproval`/`markApprovalSent` (the fuller `sendApproval` checks — promised date etc — stay); the gate-in details page `/jobs/[id]/gate-in` now admits gate-in staff (`viewJobs OR sendCheckInLink`) and carries the SAME `ApprovalSendControl` (fee pre-approval included). A NEW SEND CANCELS EARLIER OPEN LINKS (status created/sent/opened → cancelled, one job_events note "earlier check-in link … expired") — never an approved one; the cancelled link's page shows `data-checkin-expired` "This link has expired – ask OFJ for a new one" with Call OFJ, its PDF answers 410, and `/api/approve` refuses in the same words. AFTER the job's `first_approval_at` every link is READ-ONLY: the page reads the APPROVING request (name, time, charges_snapshot) whatever token opened it, shows `data-approved-line` "Approved by <name> … on <date>" and no approve form, and the route refuses a second approval (the JOB is the lock, not the link). The job card's approval box gains `data-resend-checkin` "Resend the check-in link (opens read-only)" once approved, and the send message switches to the copy wording (setting `whatsapp_checkin_copy_template`, in TEXT_KEYS + the Settings form; built-in default until the row is applied); `SendControls` takes `copyTemplate` and the dialog's "Someone else…" radio (`data-send-other`, `data-send-other-phone`) types any number — who it went to is logged per send. Lessons: the gate-in page fills the template with make/model/plate only and leaves `[name]` to the send control (it names each recipient); `feeInputOf` lives in `inspection-fee-server`, not `inspection-fee`.

Parts inspection access, 5 November 2026 night (queue 4; no migration; note `docs/parts-inspection-access-5-nov-note.md`; test `npm run test:partsinspect` 16 checks). The read-only page access (Fix 2.6) and the Print (internal) menu with its own renderer `src/lib/pdf/inspection-internal-pdf.tsx` + route `/api/pdf/inspection-internal/[jobId]` (`?mode=full|findings|blank`, `?photos=1`; Fix 5, test-fix5) existed from the 4 Nov fix batch — this task added THE WAYS IN: the /parts car row is now a DIV (the row Link wraps the content `flex-1`, so data attrs stay on the outer div) with a separate "View inspection" Link (`data-view-inspection=<jobId>`) — a row inside one big `<Link>` cannot nest another link; the /parts/[jobId] header gained the same button; and `/parts/inspections` (requirePermission priceParts, admin-client reads of inspections→jobs→vehicles→staff READ APART — no embeds, no customers table touched at all) lists every inspection newest first under Cars in the workshop / Cars that have left with `?q=` plate/job/VIN search (`data-inspection-row=<jobId>`, `data-inspection-status`), linked from /parts (`data-inspection-list-link` "Inspection reports"); layout-check EXTRA_PAGES parts gained it. Lessons: ALWAYS check for an existing implementation before writing a route — a Write to `/api/pdf/inspection-internal/[jobId]/route.ts` clobbered Fix 5's working route (git checkout restored it; the renderer edits were reverted too); `formatPlate` takes `PlateFields` with `plate_country: string` NOT NULL and a loose car's words come from `variant`, never `summary`; a probe of `/api/inspection/body` must pass the real `inspectionId` or the not-found answers before the 403 role gate. Post-push fix (cfae497): the page's vehicles embed said makes/models where the tables are vehicle_makes/vehicle_models - a SUBSTRING grep for "make:makes(name)" matched INSIDE the correct spelling and "confirmed" the wrong one, the whole read failed silently and plates showed a dash; check:live's embed probe caught it, and the suite now asserts the plate RENDERS on the row. Grep an embed spelling with word boundaries or read the hit line.

Parts management menu group, 5 November 2026 night (queue 5, from Mathew; no migration; note `docs/parts-management-menu-5-nov-note.md`; test `npm run test:partsmenu` 12 checks). THE group shape is `NavGroup {group, label, items}` in `src/components/SidebarNav.tsx` (`NavEntry = NavItem | NavGroup`; Shell and MobileTopBar take `NavEntry[]`, so the computer sidebar and the phone drawer draw groups through the ONE component): `GroupRow` opens by itself on any of its pages (`manual: boolean | null` + the prev-value setState-in-render pattern on pathname — the React compiler refuses setState in an effect), a tap decides until the next navigation, the header takes the active style only while a child page is open with the group CLOSED, and the header badge (`data-nav-group-badge`) sums the sub badges. Hooks: `data-nav-group=<key>`, `data-nav-group-open`, `data-nav-group-toggle`, `data-nav-sub`. The layout groups LPOs · Supplier invoices · Parts accounts · Suppliers (each sub keeps its OWN show rule, the group shows when any sub does; addresses unchanged) where LPOs sat, and counts the badges with cheap head counts on purchase_orders only (pending_approval; to_follow; received AND (unchecked OR queried) — per LPO, so a split invoice can read one higher than the grouped page; the page is the truth). Lesson: a sub-badge pill is reached as `[data-nav-sub][href=...] span span`, and badge figures in a shared-DB test must be asserted as header = sum of subs, never as fixed numbers.

Part stickers 50 × 25, 5 November 2026 night (queue 6 first half — the fuller stickers prompt still queued MERGES into this, never built twice; migration `20261205090000_part_sticker_50x25.sql` = the stored size 40×30 → 50×25, WAITING FOR APPLY with backup `backups/2026-11-04T21-23-50`; note `docs/part-sticker-50x25-5-nov-note.md`; tests `npm run test:sticker50` 7 checks + test-z S4 updated). `PartSticker` is the owner's dictated landscape layout: u = h/25, QR left `min(21u, h − 2·pad)` (`data-sticker-qr`), right column logo (4u) → job number BIG (FitText 5.8u, one line) → position (`data-sticker-pos`) → the black bar with the name + "n OF m" INSIDE the right column (never full width — that is what gave the QR only 17 mm); the date is off the face (behind the QR). `FitText` gained `maxLines` (default 2; the narrow name bar passes 3): a VERY long name may take 3 lines toward the 0.9 mm hard floor — the big job number is the arm's-length line. DEFAULTS sticker_part_width/height_mm 50/25; the labels page's `?test=1` sample and the Settings preview both carry pos "FRONT LEFT" so a printer test shows the whole layout. STALE-TEST fix: test-z S4 still expected the pre-19-Oct "Print all (N)" + per-sticker "Print this one" — the StickerSheet has had ticks + ONE `data-print-selected` "Print selected (n)" since; S4 now asserts that and the 3-line/≥4.5px name rule. Lessons: FitText's `minMm` prop was never wired (the wrap floor is `min(oneMin, 0.9)` mm) — honouring it suddenly could overflow other stickers, so the line cap went in as `maxLines` instead; a blunt `split(a).join(b)` on `' });'` broke four unrelated lines of a probe — patch scripts get exact one-line anchors or a full Write.

Off-site backups part 1, 5 November 2026 morning (the owner approved the proposal: Backblaze B2 in his name, scheduled from GitHub's cloud, NEVER from the PC; migration `20261205120000_offsite_backup_jobs.sql` = three background_jobs watch rows, WAITING FOR APPLY with backup `backups/2026-11-05T05-15-52`; note `docs/offsite-backups-5-nov-note.md`; unit `tests/unit/offsite-backup.test.mjs`). `scripts/offsite/lib.mjs` holds the shared pieces: `connectLive`/`connectTest` prefer `LIVE_DB_URL`/`TEST_DB_URL` (the POOLER — GitHub runners have no IPv6 and `db.<ref>` is IPv6-only) and fall back to the PC's derived direct host; AES-256-GCM files (`OFJB2601` magic + 12-byte IV + tag at the end; decrypt reads the tail first), `toPrune` (pure retention), a plain-fetch B2 client (authorize/upload with fresh-URL retries + sha1 verify/list/download/delete), and `heartbeat` = recordJobRun's exact update by key. `backup-db.mjs` (nightly 04:30 Dubai, `offsite-backup.yml`): read-only dump of every table + `_manifest.json` counts → tar.gz → encrypt → B2 `db/daily/` + the month's first copy to `db/monthly/` → prune 30/12 → heartbeat (the read-only session must be switched OFF before the heartbeat); `--dry-run` = the full local roundtrip (proven on real data: 111 tables, 250,256 rows, 24.7 MB). `backup-storage.mjs` (Sundays): incremental by an ENCRYPTED manifest in B2, per-file encrypt, deleted app files stay. `restore-test.mjs` (monthly): newest copy into the TEST project's `restore_check` SCRATCH SCHEMA (`create table … (like public.…)` — identity/generated become plain columns so dumped values insert; TEST's anonymised public data untouched), counts vs the manifest + known records, schema dropped, heartbeat on LIVE. `migrate-local-backups.mjs` = the one-off PC move (upload each `backups/` folder, verify by download+decrypt, list, `--delete` removes only verified). `checkLateJobs` now ALSO bells a failed `offsite_*` run at once (late_notified_at is the episode flag; other jobs unchanged). Secrets set via `gh secret set` with GH_TOKEN = the git credential (gh auth login refuses it for missing read:org, but GH_TOKEN works for api/secret calls; probe `gh api repos/...` WITHOUT a leading slash — Git Bash rewrites `/repos/...` into a filesystem path). `docs/disaster-recovery.md` is the owner's plain-words guide (names only). Lessons: GNU tar on Windows treats any `C:\…` ARGUMENT as a remote host ("Broken pipe" / "Cannot open") — pass the archive relative to cwd and directories as `/c/…` (no-op on Linux); a `| tail` pipe masks a script's exit code (again).

Declined-invoice fix, 5 November 2026 morning (URGENT, J-00970/INV-00007; no migration; note `docs/declined-invoice-fix-5-nov-note.md`; tests `npm run test:declinedfix` 17 checks + unit `declined-doc` 5; test-declined2's three note checks follow the new behaviour). THE rules are pure `src/lib/declined-doc.ts`: `isDocItem` (labour lines, consumables, bare "Other", service packages NEVER on the document), `cleanDeclined` (whitespace/case dedup, the worst severity wins), `groupDeclined` (Replace now → Replace → Advisory, alphabetical), `declinedSummary` ("38 recommended items declined by the customer – listed on page 2|below"), `splitDeclinedNotes` (an OLD invoice's note block out + its bullets parsed back). `InvoiceBundle.declined` (invoice-data): declined_work rows when the note carries the marker or issue_reason says declined-all, else the note's own bullets. The invoice PDF strips the block from the note, prints the ONE summary line after the lines, and draws `DeclinedPdfSection` as the LAST child with `break` — rows of TWO cells (each row wrap={false}) so the two-column list pages cleanly; the 40-line note inside the tail's unbreakable block was the overlap. The customer page renders `DeclinedWorkSection` (src/components/DeclinedWork.tsx, `data-declined-section/-group/-item/-count`) + `data-declined-summary`; the job card shows the same section from declined_work. `declineAllWork` AND `declineToGateOut` write NO list into invoices.notes any more (issue_reason is the flag). THE MONEY PAGE'S LINES BOX: once a live document exists it shows THAT document's invoice_lines + stored totals (`data-lines-source="invoice"`, `data-lines-total`; "INV-x's own lines"), the draft only before any document — a declined car showed "None · 0.00" against a 1,500.00 invoice. checkPdf ALREADY had the overlap rule; no test had ever FED it a declined invoice — test-declinedfix renders one with 47 items and E5 asserts zero problems. Live healed on deploy (PDFs render fresh): INV-00001/-00004/-00007; the sent two are worth resending. Lessons: `inspection_fee_choice` is LOCKED after first approval — bundling it into a jobs update loses the WHOLE update silently (set `inspection_fee_aed` alone); a server component's text appears in the HTML AND in Next's inline payload, so a count-occurrences assert must slice the section's own markup; `grep | head -3` hid the one real `declinedNote` caller (quotes/actions' declineToGateOut).

Supplier invoice edit, 5 November 2026 morning (URGENT, LPO-00037-2/Gargash, J-00969; migration `20261205150000_supplier_invoice_edit.sql` WAITING FOR APPLY with backup `backups/2026-11-05T06-25-24`; note `docs/supplier-invoice-edit-5-nov-note.md`; tests `npm run test:supinvedit` 31 checks + the typed-VAT rows in `tests/unit/supplier-invoice-rows.test.mjs`). Cause: the LPO's invoice card renders only while `supplier_invoice_status !== "received"` — an entered invoice could never be changed. ONE action `editSupplierInvoice(poId, formData)` in parts/order-actions.ts (owner + accounts, never viewingAs; remark >= 5 characters COMPULSORY; `back` from the form keeps the page's own query): it runs `applySupplierInvoice(po, lines, formData, staff, { edit: true })` — edit mode stores `purchase_orders.supplier_invoice_vat_aed` (box `invoice_vat`; empty = 5% as before) and CLEARS `supplier_invoice_checked_at`, and an edit NEVER auto-queues the Zoho bill (the fresh "Mark checked" does; a bill already posted turns red "fix by hand" through the existing changed-invoice rule, whose words live in zoho_outbox.last_error) — then writes old → new per field (number, date, Before VAT, VAT, each line's price) with the remark and `paid_difference_aed` into the server-only `supplier_invoice_edits` (SERVER_ONLY in test:security; e2e-cleanup deletes it before purchase_orders in all three blocks). A paid LPO's payment is NEVER touched: the difference is stored, told ("the supplier owes us / is owed AED x more – the recorded payment is untouched") and shown in the history; a failed history insert answers "the invoice was changed, but recording who edited it failed" (never SAVE_FAILED after a real write). ONE component `src/components/SupplierInvoiceEdit.tsx` (`SupplierInvoiceEditButton` + `InvoiceEditHistory` fed by `loadInvoiceEdits`): on the LPO page's Record box and on the opened Supplier-invoices row (one button per LPO of a split invoice; the same view serves Parts accounts). The form is SSR-rendered HIDDEN (`hidden={!open}`) so it posts without JavaScript — that is also how the suite proves the server refusal: harvest the form's own fields ($ACTION included) from the accounts page and replay the POST from the parts browser (error answers land in the redirect URL). Live hooks: `data-si-edit`, `data-si-edit-form`, `data-edit-number/-date/-amount/-vat/-remark/-save`, `data-edit-line=<lineId>`, `data-vat-warning` (|VAT − 5%| beyond 0.01 × lines, amber, NEVER blocks), `data-edit-total`, `data-edit-paid-note`, `data-invoice-vs-approved` / `data-si-vs-approved` ("Approved 12,228.30 → Invoiced 11,684.82 (−543.48)", pure `approvedVsInvoiced` in supplier-invoice-rows.ts), `data-invoice-edit-history`/`data-invoice-edit`/`data-edit-change`/`data-edit-paid-diff`. THE TYPED VAT IS THE INVOICE'S VAT everywhere: `InvoiceShare` carries `vat`/`withVat`, `row.vat` = the shares' sum, paid/unpaid amounts from `withVat` (supplier balances, the Owed box and Payments to suppliers follow), the LPO page's pay figure (`payVat = invoiceVat ?? 5%`, the Record box prints "AED x + VAT y = z") and `billErpFigures.gross` (reconciliation, monthly close, sync previews; Zoho itself still computes 5% on the bill, so a VAT off 5% beyond rounding is a NAMED reconciliation difference). Ready-gates in supplier-invoices.ts: `invoiceEditReady` (the table), `invoiceVatReady` (the column, joined to loadSupplierInvoices' select like queryCols; billErpFigures and the two pages probe it the same way). Lessons: test-supplier-invoices was STALE since the 4-Nov receive rebuild (its section A typed the invoice into the receive panel that no longer asks) — reworked to receive-then-enter-as-accounts via `[data-si-enter]`; a server-action form rendered only on the client carries no $ACTION fields, so a form that must post without JS (or be replayed by a test) is SSR-rendered hidden instead of conditionally mounted.

Enter price myself, 5 November 2026 (URGENT, E-00069 "STAR LIGHT WITH SHOOTING STAR MODULE LARGE"; migration `20261205180000_advisor_price_parts.sql` WAITING FOR APPLY with backup `backups/2026-11-05T08-03-52` (part_items.priced_by_advisor/price_checked_at/price_checked_by, read through `advisorPricePartsReady`; estimates need nothing); note `docs/enter-price-myself-5-nov-note.md`; proof `test:sim` path i (19 checks), test-estparts D, test-s10-pricing C, unit pricing-done). CAUSE: "I have a price" opened its boxes as a ROW inside the sideways-scrolling parts table - on a phone/tablet scrolled right they opened off-screen left (measured x = -499 px at 390, -97 px at 800). SECOND CAUSE (the "jumps back to With the parts desk"): the editor's throttled `refresh()` cleared the whole `dirty` set even while an autosave op was still in flight, so a refresh landing mid-save merged the server's OLDER copy over the line; `useQuoteAutosave` now returns `hasPending()` (queue or busy), the editor keeps it in `pendingRef` (set in an effect: the hook is made after `refresh`, which it calls back) and the refresh timer returns early while pending (the queue's own onSaved refreshes again). ONE centre window `src/components/PartPriceDialog.tsx` (portal; `data-price-dialog/-cost/-supplier/-markup/-selling/-sell-line/-low-warning/-below-warning/-confirm/-min-note/-save/-close`, `data-price-mode`): plain `MoneyBox` inputs (cleanNumberText/parseNumberText from NumericInput, updated on every key - the shared NumericInput's 1 s settle kept Save greyed after quick typing), the selling box keeps exactly what is typed while focused (`sellTyping`), cost compulsory and never 0, supplier optional, markup % or AED or typed selling price (markupFromSell-style), red tick for cost < AED 5 or selling below cost, `controlClass` now exported from ui.tsx. THE RULE CHANGE (parts-rows `rowMissing`): an advisor's own price (`advisor_price` "1" or "checked") counts as PRICED at once and needs only a real cost (supplier optional, a stand-in still refused) - the desk's "Price is right ✓" is a later look, never a hold; `loadEstimatesToPrice` lists an estimate only for parts to price or prices to send (`estimateTaskWords`: task first, "· n priced by advisor" as info; button labels never "Check the price"); `pricingTodoOf` never counts an advisor's own price as "to send". Estimates: save-route `advisorPrice` (supplier optional; the desk told "Priced by advisor" ONLY when the line was with them), new `backToDesk {id}` ("Send to parts desk instead": price cleared, "Advisor had: AED x each from y (name)" appended to parts_note, back on the desk's list); editor buttons `data-est-send` "Send to parts desk", `data-est-own-price` "Enter price myself", `data-back-to-desk`, `data-priced-by` "Priced by <name>, <time>" (`settings.staffNames`); ESTIMATE_PART_WORDS.advisor = "Priced by advisor". JOBS (once applied): save-route `partPrice {partId, cost, supplier, confirmCost, markupMode, markupValue}` (owner + advisors via the quote route; refused when ordered/ended/desk-priced; THE cost check 409 with `partId`; writes the part, `syncLineForPart` makes/updates the line, then the markup floored at the minimum for advisors - the route's LAST write, so tests wait on `markup_percent`, never on the part row; job event + parts notified "Priced by advisor · J-…") and `backToDesk` for a job line (cost cleared, note in availability_note); the editor shows unpriced job parts under `data-job-unpriced-parts` (`data-job-unpriced=<partId>`) and, at once after Save, `data-job-priced-now` with the counters (`partsPricedShown` = server count + `pricedHere`) moving instantly; `settings.advisorPriceParts`/`partPricedBy`. The desk's Parts page: `TableRow.advisorPriced` → `JobAdvisorPrice` chip (`data-advisor-priced="open|checked"`, `data-desk-price-right` → `checkPartPrice` in parts/actions.ts); `valuesOfPart` sets `advisor_price`; `partAdvisorCols()` in parts-data.ts appends the columns to a select once ready (parts-waiting, PartsDashboard, /parts, the job's Parts page). Carry-over: `estimateLineRows` copies priced_by_advisor/price_checked_at/_by onto the part. The pop-up check opens "Enter price myself" from a job part with no price. Lessons: a production test build that predates the last source edit serves the OLD screen - grep `.next-test/server/chunks` for a new hook before trusting a run; a job part's save takes ~10 s from this PC to the test database (~100 sequential calls), so anything on screen must not wait for it; a left join on the part row resolves before the line exists - wait on the line's own field.
